Ezekiyeli 1

Wugubulilwe wulongozi kuli Ezekiyeli, kulawa kuli Mulungu

(1:1–7:27)

Ligoda lyo Mulungu lyo wutemi

1 Ane mukohani Ezekiyeli mono nili mwana wa Buzi, nekalaga hamonga ne Wayahudi i wahamywa, he kolongo lya Kebali, mu yisi ya Babiloni. Ale, mwi'zuwa lye 'hano lya mu mwezi we kane, wa mu mwaka we makumi madatu, wulanga wukagubuka, nghagubulilwa no Mulungu.
2 (No mwaka wuwo, wali wo mwaka we 'hano ku wulawa lono mutemi Yehoyakimu yategulilwa ku wuhamywe.)
3 Na lono nali nili ha nyenje ye likolongo lya Kebali, kuko ku Babiloni, nghahulika u Muwaha Mulungu yakulonga nane, ne vilungo vyakwe vikanyiziza.

4 Langa ta, nghawona libeho lyo mukukunzo likuza likulawilila ku lubavu lwe sumuka. No wubulile wo moto, wabulilaga mwi'vunde yiwaha, sunga no wulanga wose wukawa wudunghu mbavu zose. No wubulile wuwo wono wabulilaga, wukawa wukung'alala kotya nha shaba.
5 Na mugati ye libeho lilyo, yikaza hwani ye viwumbwa vyumi vine, vina futa yo munhu.
6 Na vyose vyono vine, vyali vina wusu wuli hane ne mabawa mane mane.
7 Ne magulu gavyo gali gagolocile, ne zinzayo ze magulu gavyo, zali nhe zinzayo ze magulu ge zing'ombe. Nazo zang'alalaga nha shaba nghusulwe muno.
8 Hamonga na zizo, kanyhi vyose vyono vine, vyali vina miwoko mine mine hasi ye mabawa gavyo, mabawa gose vyono mane.
9 Navyo viwumbwa vyose, vyali vigatandakuye mabawa gavyo, vigapamize cimonga ne ciyagwe. Na lono vyajendaga, vikawa vikujendana hamonga du akuno viyipamize ne mabawa gavyo, kanyhi hamba si vyapitukaga yaye lono vyajendaga.
10 Navyo viwumbwa vivyo vyose vyono vine, vyali vina wusu wuli hane, wono wisimajiye-simajiye. Ku mwande yayo vyali vina hwani ya wusu wa munhu, no lubavu lo kulume, vyali vina hwani yo wusu wa simba, no lubavu lo kucekulu, hwani yo wusu wa ng'ombe, na ku migongo yavyo, vyali vina hwani yo wusu wa 'ndi.
11 Ne mabawa gavyo, gali matandakule gepamize, ciwumbwa ne libawa lye ciyagwe cono vihegame naco. Agalya i mabawa ganji mejete mejete, vyali vigubucize miwili yavyo.
12 Navyo vyose vyali vilanjile mbavu zose vyono yine. Na ku soko yiyo, vyasumaga kubita ku lubavu lo lose kono u muhe wahilaga vibite, navyo ko vyabitaga, hamba kuwa na soko yo kupituka yaye.

13 Na muli viwumbwa vivyo vyumi, mwali mocili cinhu cinji cono cejelaga cihwanile mamuli, cono camulikaga ako na ako. No moto nawo wakaga no kulavya mamuli.
14 Navyo i viwumbwa vyumi, vyamanghaga ako na ako no kuwuya kotya limuli.
15 Nane lono nali nghali nivilanjile vilimu vivyo vyumi, nghawona maguludumu mane gapamize hasi mu muhangha, ha mhelo ye viwumbwa vivyo cimonga cimonga, nha cinji ciwumbwa ne guludumu lyaco.
16 Ne maguludumu gose, gali gehwanile, gang'alalaga nhe libwe lyo wuguzi wukamu muno. Nago maguludumu gose vyono mane, gali gena hwani monga, no wijele wo wunoze wago wali wihwanile, liguludumu mugati ye liguludumu liyagwe,
17 Maguludumu ho gasume kubiduka no kubita ko kose mu malanga gose vyono mane.
18 Ne migo ye maguludumu gago, yali yimemile meso gose.
19 Ne mapango go gose lono viwumbwa vivyo vyumi vyatulukaga, maguludumu gago nago, vyatulukanaga hamonga ne viwumbwa vivyo. Na lono viwumbwa vivyo vyumi vyagulukaga, ne maguludumu nago gagulukaga nhavivyo.
20 Na lono i viwumbwa vivyo vyumi vyabitaga hanhu ho hose hono vyasakaga kubita, ne maguludumu nago gakanoza nhavivyo du, hambi i viwumbwa vivyo vyumi, nha vyo vyagalongozaga i maguludumu gago.
21 Holeka ku soko yiyo, i maguludumu ganozaga nha vyono viwumbwa vyanozaga, hono viwumbwa vivyo vyumi vituluce, nago maguludumu gatulukaga, na hono viwumbwa vime, nago maguludumu gemaga, na hono viwumbwa viguluce mucanyha, nago maguludumu gagulukaga nhavivyo.

22 Na mucanyha ye mitwe ye viwumbwa vivyo vyumi, mwali na hwani ya mwanya, yihwanile liwala lye libwe lyono linha lilole lyo kogofya.
23 Na hasi yo mwanya, vikawa kwima viwumbwa vivyo vyumi, akuno vigoloye ne mabawa gavyo mejete mejete gepamize cimonga ne 'bawa lye ciyagwe, akuno vigubice miwili yavyo, ne mabawa ganji ga galya i mejete mejete gono gasigaye.
24 Hanyuma nghahulika cihumbi ce mabawa gavyo lono vyagulukaga, cikawa cihwanile ne lizi lye lilenga, cihwanile nhe cihumbi ce wasilikale wenji wo wulugu, cihwanile ne lizi lyo Mulungu u Mudaha Vyose. Navyo vyali vyeza kutima hasi, vikagatenya mabawa gavyo,
25 ninga hamba nhavivyo lizi likajendelela kuhulikwa mu wulawa mu mwanya wono wali ha mitwe yavyo.

26 Na mucanyha yo mwanya wa mu mitwe yavyo, hali na cinhu cono cejelaga nhe 'goda lya nhojo, yinozwe ne vibwe vyo wuguzi wukamu vye liwala lye dedede. Na mwi'goda lilyo lye nhojo, cali cikaye cinji cihwanile futa ya munhu.
27 Nghawona yiyo futa yo munhu yikung'alala kotya shaba, mugati yo moto. Yikawa kung'alala muno hanhu hose, no
28 wung'alale we mawala gose gono yali yina, wali wihwanile ne mawala ge licilalyambwa. Na wuwu wali wo wijele we nhojo wono walajilaga nha vyono Muwaha Mulungu yali baho.

Mulungu yakumucema Ezekiyeli yawe muwizi

Nane nali neza kuziwona zizi, nghagwa wugubama. Hanyuma nghahulika lizi lyo Muwaha Mulungu likunonjela likutya,


Ezekiyeli 2

1 “Gwe go munhu, ta winuce, hambi 'ne nha nghusaka kulongana nagwe.”
2 Na lono lizi lyali likali likulongana nane, hobaho muhe wo Mulungu yakenjila muli'ne, yakanyimya wima. Hanyuma kanyhi nghahulika lizi lijendeleye likutya,
3 “Gwe go munhu, ane nghukutuma kuli wanhu wa Isilayeli. Hambi nha wambedegala no kundeka, kanyhi vyo wakali wakumbedegala du, nha vyono wali wambedegaye i walezi wawo.
4 Wasina cihuliko, hamba si wanyogopile 'ne yaye. Holeka nane nghukutuma wubite wukawamanyize vyono ane no Muwaha Mulungu nghuwalonjela.
5 Hono wakuhulice hamba ng'o, ninga wowa wakondya kumanya vyono muwizi kawaziza.

6 “Ninga agwe go munhu, wulecele lukulu kuwogopa hamba kuzogopa zinghani zawo. Ninga wumanye nha vyono hamba mabijili ne mamivya gakuhome nagwe kowa nha mono wikaye hono ho zili zi'nje. Ninga 'gwe weze wukamile, wulecele lukulu kuwogopa i wabedegazi wawo, hamba kuzogopa nghani zawo.
7 Nagwe kowalonjela zose zono nokulonjela, hono wakuhulice hamba wabeze, ninga agwe wuwalonjele du. Hambi wene nha wali cibede muno.

8 “Ninga agwe go munhu, wuhulice ta zono nghukulonjela. Nagwe wulanjilile, wulece kuwa wuli mubezi nhe wanhu wawo. Ale, wahame mulomo wako, wulye cono nokupela.”
9 Hobaho nghawona muwoko wugoleywe, na langa ta, wibate citabu cipalinje.
10 U muwoko wuwo, wukawa kucitandakula citabu cico cipalinje, nane nghawona cali cina mandiko mbavu zose. I cililo co wusungu cali candicigwe mumo hamonga no wugule!


Ezekiyeli 3

1 Mulungu yakatya, “Gwe go munhu, ale wulye citabu cici, hanyuma ho wubite wukalongane ne wanhu wa Isilayeli.”
2 Hobaho nghahama mulomo wangu, nayo yakawa kundisa i citabu cico.
3 Hanyuma kanyhi yakatya, “Gwe go munhu, wulye citabu cico cono nghukupela, wucimemye mu mulomo wako.” Nane nghawa kucilya, naco cikawa cili muzinzi muno mu mulomo wangu nha wuci.

4 Hanyuma kanyhi Mulungu yakanonjela yakatya, “Gwe go munhu, wubite kuli wanhu wa Isilayeli wukawalonjele zose zono nghulonjeye.
5 Nane hamba si nghukutuma kuli wanhu wono wakulonga milonjele mijenzi ng'o, langa nghukutuma kuli Waisilayeli.
6 Na hono nali nghutume, kuli makungugo gono gena milonjele mikamu, yono nagwe si wuyimanyile yaye awo langa wali wakuhulice zono wukuwalonjela.
7 Ninga muli wanhu wa Isilayeli, howa hasina hamba yumonga mono kokuhulika yaye. Kanyhi hamba na nene siwosuma kuhulika yaye. Hambi wose nha wasina cihuliko, kanyhi wali cibede.
8 Ninga nane nokunoza nagwe wuwe wusina cihuliko kuli wene, kanyhi wuwe wuli nghakamo nha vyono wene wenha.
9 Kanyhi nokunoza wuwe wusikame nhe 'bwe, kanyhi wuwe wukangaye nhe alimasi. Holeka wulece kuwogopa wawo i wanhu wabedegazi.”

10 Mulungu yakajendelela kunonjela kanyhi yakatya, “Gwe go munhu, zinghani zangu zose zono nghulonjeye wuzikumbucilaje, kanyhi wuzibate mu nhumbula yako.
11 Hanyuma wuwabitile wayago wono wahamywa, wuwalonjele zono nghuwalonjela ane no Muwaha Mulungu. Nawo hono wakuhulice hamba walece kukuhulika, ayo yowe yo nghani yawo.”

12 Hobaho u muhe wo Mulungu wukanyinula, nane nghahulika kunyuma yangu, wukolome wa yizi yiwaha likulonga likutya. “Nhojo yo Muwaha Mulungu, yilumbizwe ku wulanga kucanyha!”
13 Hanyuma, nghahulika cihumbi ciwaha ce mabawa ge viwumbwa vyumi, lono vyepumunhaga mu mwanya, hamonga ne lizozo lye maguludumu. Lizozo lilyo, likawa nhe lizozo lye litulo.
14 No muhe wo Muwaha Mulungu, wukanyiziza kwe vilungo viwaha wukanyasula. Na lono muhe wakwe wanyasulila kwe vilungo, nghahulika wusungu muno sunga nghakolwa ne likuwo.
15 Hodu nghawa kubita ku Teli-abibu, ha nhumbi ye likolongo lya Kebali, hono wekalaga i wahamywa, nghekala baho vilo mupunghati, akuno memile no wizine wa kuli zono naziwona no kuzihulika.

Muwaha Mulungu yakumuwika Ezekiyeli yawe mulindilizi

(Eze 33:1-9)

16 Vyali vyeza kupuluta vilo mupunghati, u Muwaha Mulungu yakalonga nane kanyhi yakatya,
17 “Gwe go munhu, ane nghukuwika wuwe mulindilizi we nghungugo ya Isilayeli. Nagwe wubite wukawalonjele ze suguzi zangu zose zono nghulonjeye.
18 Nane lono nghumulonjela u munhu mubi vyono kofwa so wubi wakwe, nagwe hono wulece kumusugula yayipitule mijendele yakwe ho yeponye, ale, u munhu yuyo mbeka kofwa so wubi wakwe. Ninga nane nokuwala gwegwe vyono nagwe wuli musutyi, yo gwegwe mono wumwambuze yafwe.

19 “Ninga hono wumusugule munhu mubi nayo ho yalece kuwunena wubi wakwe, ale, munhu yuyo, kofwila mu wubi wakwe, nagwe kokwiponya wumi wako, kowa wusina wuhoswe yaye.

20 “Na hono munu we ling'ani yetume zo wubi, nane hono mbice cikobazo hali mwene nagwe hono wuwe si wumusugye yaye, ale munhu yuyo kofwa. Kofwa kusoko yo wubi wakwe. Nane hamba si nozikumbucila yaye ne ziswanu zono yazituma. Ninga nane nokuwala 'gwe, vyono wuli musutyi, hambi nha yo gwegwe mono wumwambuze yafwe.
21 Na hono wumusugule munhu muswanu vyono yalece kwituma zo wubi, nayo yakuhulice, no kuleka kwituma zo wubi, ale, yuyo kowumipa, nagwe kokwiponya wumi wako, hamba kowa wusina wuhoswe yaye.”

Ezekiyeli yakuwa yali cibwibwi

22 Vilungo vyo Muwaha Mulungu vikanyiziza, nghahulika yakunonjela yakutya, “Ale, Ezekiyeli, winuce wubite kwi'lolo, nane nokulonjela kuko zono nghusaka nghulonjele.”
23 Ale, nane nghawa kubita kuko kwi'lolo. Na kuko nghaluwona lukumyo lo Muwaha Mulungu, nha vyono naluwona halulongozi ha nyenje ye likolongo lya Kebali. Nane nghawa kugwa hasi wugubama,
24 Ninga u muhe wo Mulungu wukanyiziza wukanyinula no kunyimya wima. Nayo Muwaha Mulungu yakanyitya, “Gwe Ezekiyeli, alu wuwuye ku kaya yako, wubite wukedindile mu nyumba.
25 Nagwe go munhu, wokuwopa ne milejehe, nagwe hamba si kosuma kanyhi kulawila kunze kuli wanhu.
26 Nane nolunamatiza lulimi lwako mu malaka gako ho wuwe cibwibwi, ho wulece kusuma kuwasugula i wanhu wawa i wabedegazi.
27 Na hanyuma kolo nghulonga nagwe, no kuludohola lulimi lwako kanyhi, ho wusume kulonga, ale baho nhondo ho kowalonjela zono ane no Muwaha Mulungu nghuwalonjela. Na lulo wanji wa muli wene wohulika, ninga wanji wozibedegala zinghani zako, hambi nha wanhu we nghungugo yono yisina cihuliko yaye.”


Ezekiyeli 4

Ezekiyeli yakunoza cihwanicizo co wupilimwe wa Yelusalemi

1 U Muwaha Mulungu yakatya, “Gwe go munhu, wusole litofali wuliwice ha mwande yako, hanyuma wulikwale cihwani ce likaya lya Yelusalemi mucanyha yalyo.
2 Hanyuma wulinozeze cihwani co wupilimwe walyo, wuwicile ne makweme galipilime, wuliwicile ne majinji ne zinghambi ze wasilikale, wuliwicile ne matinde go kulimwagulila likaya, mbavu zose.
3 Hanyuma kanyhi, wusole siniya ya cuma, wuyisimye yiwe nhe 'jinji lya cuma haciligati yako ne likaya. Nagwe wulidome likaya. Nalyo likaya lyowa nha lyakondya kupilimwa no wulugu, nagwe kowa yo gwegwe mono walipilima no kulihoma. Na cici cowa co citanjilo kuli nghungugo ya Isilayeli.

4 “Hanyuma wuwe kugona hasi, wugoneye muwoko wako wo kucekulu, nane nokudinghuza uwubi wose we nghungugo ya Isilayeli. Nagwe kowa wuli baho du wuli mu wusungu ku soko yo wubi wawo, sunga vipulute vilo magana madatu na makumi 'cenda.
5 Hambi nha nakondya kukutemezezya'gwe lizuwa limonga limonga liwe nha wo mwaka monga monga, sunga lono wulipicizwe wawo wulamalicila.
6 Nagwe kolo wazikondya zizo, wipitucizaje kanyhi, wugonele ku lubavu lo kulume vilo makumi mane, so wubi wa Yuda. Nayo yowa nhavivilya du, lizuwa limonga limonga, lyowa nha wo mwaka monga monga wo wulipicizwe wawo.

7 “Nagwe kogadomya meso gako gawe gawulanjile wupilimwe we likaya lya Yelusalemi. Hanyuma wuliyunzize likaya muwoko wako, wuwe kuliwilila.
8 Nane nokuwopa no lubano, ho wulece kusuma kupituka-pituka wulanje ako na ako, sunga wuvifisilize vilo vye milewo yako.

9 “Alu, wusole ngano ndodo, ne shayili, ne zinandala, ne zifwili, no wuhemba, hamonga no muhoni. Vivyo vyose, wuvihanze mu ciya cimonga, winozeze mukate. Na yiyo yowa yo ndigwa yako mu vilo vivyo vyose vye magana madatu na makumi'cenda, lono kowa wukali wugoneye ku lubavu lwako lo kucekulu.
10 Nagwe yowa yikunojeye wulye cipimo ca wuzito wa gulamu magana mejete na makumi madatu du, zo mukate wuwo mwi'zuwa limonga. Nawo wuzito wuwo wa gulamu zizo, wokukwila, nagwe kowa wigute sunga i mitondo yaco.
11 Kanyhi yowa yikunojeye wuwe kung'wa malenga macefu macefu du, nha cinji, weze kung'wa vikombe vyejete du mwi'zuwa limonga.
12 Kanyhi yikunojeye wusole mavi go munhu gono makavu, wupembele moto wono kowukasicila mukate wuwo. Hanyuma wuwe kuwulila ha meso ge wanhu wose.”

13 U Muwaha Mulungu yakatya, “Ne nghani yiyo yo yikulajila vyono Waisilayeli wowa kulya zindigwa zono nyavufywe, kolo neza kuweyagaziza mu zi'si zi njenzi.”

14 Ninga nane nghamutya, “Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu, ng'o, ye! Hambi 'ne ku wulawa katali nha nghali sineyavufya hamba hadodo. Kanyhi ku wulawa wudodo wangu sunga nalu, nghali sinalya yaye, nyama ya nimu yo yose yono yifwiliye, hamba yono yiwulagwe ne malimu ga mu mbago. Hamba nghali sinalya ndigwa yo yose yono nyavufywe.”
15 Nayo Mulungu yakamhitula yakatya, “Ale, viswanu, na hono yili nhavivyo, hodu, nokuleka witumamile matope ge ng'ombe si 'nhe mavi go munhu, wunyanyile mukate wako wuwo.”
16 Kanyhi yakatya, “Gwe go munhu, ane nolicepula ligawo lye zindigwa lya mu Yelusalemi. Ne wanhu wono wali mumo, wologazika muno no kwihwelela, vyono lulo, ndigwa yono wolowa kulya, hamonga ne malenga gono wolowa kung'wa, vyose vyolowa kwamula kupimwa mu cijelo, hanyuma ho wawa kuvitumamila.
17 Na mu mapango gago, i wanhu wa Isilayeli wolowa na wanjile wuwaha muno wa ndigwa, hamonga ne malenga. Na ku soko yiyo, wologanda muno ku soko yo wubi wawo.”


Ezekiyeli 5

Ezekiyeli yakuziceta zimvwili zakwe

1 U Muwaha Mulungu yakatya, “Gwe go munhu, wusole mumaje mukali, wuzicete zindevu zako hamonga ne zimvwili zako. Na kolo waziceta, wuzisole wuzipime wuzito wayo, hanyuma wuzitemanule mafungu madatu.
2 Wusole lifungu limonga, wuziticimize mu moto, wuziticimizize mwi'kaya mugati, lono milewo yamalika. Hanyuma kanyhi wulisole lifungu linji, nazo wuzidumulanje no mumaje wako, akuno wukujenda, lono wukuwuka mwi'kaya. Ne lifungu lyono lisigaye, nazo wuzigumile mucanyha ziyagale mu mbeho, nane nozityatilila no mumaje wangu.
3 Ninga hamba nhavivyo, wusigaze mvwili nyhefu, wuziwope mwi'cindo lye myenda yako.
4 Hanyuma yayo kanyhi, wusole mvwili nyhefu za muli zizo, wuzigumile mu moto wuzilece zitakamile. No moto wolowa muli zene, wutandale mwi'kungugo lyose lya Isilayeli.”

5 Muwaha Mulungu yakatya, “Langa, ta, ali lyo likaya lya Yelusalemi, lyono naliwika haciligati ye yinze, hamonga ne ma'si ganji gose gono galipilime.
6 Ninga Yelusalemi yagabedegala malajilizo gangu, nokwilajila vyono yene yo 'mbi muno si nhe ma'si ganji gono galipilime. Hambi Yelusalemi nha yalema kuwibata malajilizo gangu, hamonga no wulajilize wangu.
7 Holeka anye wa mwi'kaya lya Yelusalemi, alu muhulicize zono nghulonga, ane no Muwaha Mulungu. Alu vyono mwakondya kugabedegala malajilizo gangu, hamba kuwibata yaye no wulajilize wangu, alu, mweyambalila nyenye nyo weneco, mweyinjiza mu magayo, si nhe makungugo go gose gono gamupilimenyi. Hambi nha mwayityatya myoto ye makungugo ganji.
8 Holeka luno ane no Muwaha Mulungu, nghumulonjelenyi vyono, ane nawa nili mulugu wenyu. Nomulipicizenyi ko wulipicizwe wono i wanhu we makungugo gose wowuwona calinze.
9 Ku soko aluno mukwituma zinghani zose zono si zikunyendeza. Holeka nane noyilipiciza muno Yelusalemi mu nghango yono nghali nayitendela ne lizuwa linji, mu nghango yono si nilayitendela kanyhi yaye.
10 Na mu ndipicizwa yiyo, iwalezi wa mu Yelusalemi, wololya i wana wawo, nawo wana wololya walezi wawo. Nolomulipicizenyi no kumwiyagazizenyi mu malanga gose, kanyhi holowa hasina hamba yunji mono yalasigala mumi.

11 “Holeka ane vyono nili Mulungu mumi, azi zo nghani zo nghutya. Hambi 'nye nha mwayavufya Nyumba yangu ku soko ye zimbi zenyu, hamonga ne zinghani ze cevu zono mukwituma. Holeka nane nomulecenyi hamba si nomuwonelenyi lusungu yaye.
12 Lifungu lilongozi lye wanhu wako, wofwila mwi'kaya so matamwa na so nzala, lifungu lye kejete, awo wolowulajilwa kunze ye likaya no mumaje, nawo we lifungu lye kadatu lye ndumilizo, noloweyagaziza mwi'beho liwaha, hanyuma nowatyatilila no mumaje wangu.

13 “Nanye baho ho mololiwonela nhondo likuwo lyangu hamonga no wukali wangu, sunga lono nolowa nayimala nhunduji yangu nene no mweneco. Na kolo zizi zejela, baho nanye nhondo, ho molomanyila vyono ane no Muwaha Mulungu yo nene mono nonjile zizo kuli 'nye, vyono ngholigwe likuwo muno so wula wihuwilwa wenyu.
14 No munhu wo wose wa mu nghungugo zose wono wamupilimenyi, mono kowa kukola baho, kolowa musecenyi.

15 “Na Kolo nasimba no kukolwa likuwo no kumulipicizenyi, i makungugo gose gono gamupilimenyi, golokwibatwa no woga wuwaha muno. Wolomulanjenyi nha muli wanhu we 'wifyo, kanyhi wolomulijenyi.
16 Nane noloyicepula ndigwa yenyu yiwe nyhefu, nzala yimwaganenyi. Nanye molotamiswa muno ne nzala, molowa nha mukugumwa ne zisonga zinghali muno, zono zikumongomezenyi.
17 Nane nolomugumililenyi nzala mbaha, hamonga ne malimu ga mu mbago gawawulaje wana wenyu. Kanyhi nolomugumililenyi matamwa, ne mafwa, hamonga no wulugu wumuwulajenyi muno. Nene no Muwaha Mulungu nalonga.”


Ezekiyeli 6

U Muwaha Mulungu yakuwuhosa wuhembi we vihwani

1 Muwaha Mulungu yakanonjela yakatya,
2 “Gwe go munhu, ale, wugalangaje matunda ga Isilayeli, wugalonjele zono nghukulajiliza.
3 Wugalonjelaje matunda ga Isilayeli vyono ganojeye gazihulice zinghani zo Muwaha Mulungu. Gahulice zono ane no Muwaha Mulungu, nghugalonjela matunda, ne vigongo, hamonga ne makolongo, ne malolo. Vyono ane nohongomeza no mumaje hanhu baha hose hono wanhu wakuvihembela vihwani.
4 I makumbicilo gawo golomwagulwanga, ne vikumbicilo vyawo vyo kufusiza wuvumba, navyo vyolotulwangwa. Ne wanhu wose, wolowulajilwa ha mwande ye vihwani vyawo.
5 Nane noloyiyagaza mivimba ye Waisilayeli ako na ako. Nologeyagaza mafupa ge mivimba yawo, gapilime vikumbicilo vyawo.
6 Sunga ne makaya gose ge Waisilayeli, golokongomezezwa lukulu, ne makumbicilo gawo hamonga ne vihwani vyawo, vyose vyolotulanjililwa lukulu. Navyo vikumbicilo vyawo vyo kufusiza wuvumba, vyose vyolopondanjizwa no kwiyagazwa, ne vinhu vyose vyono wowa wanozize, navyo vyolokwajililila lukulu hamba kwijela kanyhi yaye.
7 Ne wanhu wolowulagwa mbavu zose. Na wose wono walasigala womi, wawo wolomanya vyono ane nili Muwaha Mulungu.

8 “Ninga nolowasigaza wanji walece kuwulagwa, nawo wawo wono wolosigala,
9 wolokwiyagalila mu zinghungugo zinji, wakekale kuko nha wafungwa. Nawo lono wowa wali kuko, wolonghumbucila lono wali mugati ye makungugo, no kumanya nha vyono yo nene mono niwalipicize no kuwakoza cevu, ku soko yo wuwula wihuwilwa wawo. Hambi nha wakondya kundeka 'ne, no kwenda vihwani si nha nene. Holeka ku soko yiyo, wolowa wakondya kwiyavufya weneco ku soko yo wubi, hamonga ne makani ge cevu gono wetumaga.
10 Nawo nhondo ho wolomanyila vyono yo nene Muwaha Mulungu, na vyono sunga ne zinghani zangu kuli wene si zali za bwete yaye.”
11 Muwaha Mulungu yakanyitya, “Gwe Ezekiyeli, wutowe maganza, wuyisambe yisi kwa vilungo! Hanyuma wulile ko wusungu muno ku soko ye mabi gose ge Waisilayeli, hamonga ne makani ge cevu gono wagetumaga. Nalu wose wofwa no wulugu, hamonga ne lizala ne matamwa.
12 Nawo wono wali ku zi'si zo kutali, wotamwa no kufwa, nawo wono wali baho behi, wowulagwa no wulugu. Nawo wono wowa wakali wasigaye, awo lizala lyowakopola. Nawo wose woliwona likuwo lyangu vyono linha.
13 Ne mivimba yawo wose, yolowa yiyagaye mu vihwani vyawo, yipilime vikumbicilo vyawo. Kanyhi yinji yolowa yiyagaye mu vigongo vyose vitali, sunga na mu matunda gose. Sunga na mu masina ge mibici yose yono yidedewaye, na mu mibici yose yo munghola ye mahamha menji. Sunga ne hanhu hose hono wene walavizyaga makumbiko gawo ge vilavywa vinyanywe kuli vihwani vyawo. Na ku soko yiyo wose ho wolomanyila vyono ane yo nene Muwaha Mulungu.
14 Heya mbeka, nane nolowugolola muwoko wangu niyongomeze yisi yawo. Nolowugolola muwoko wangu, niyinoze yisi yawo yiwe yibalangu, ku wulawilila kwi nyika lya ku lubavu lwa kutakama, sunga ko kwi'kaya lya Libula lyono lili ku lubavu lwa kusumuka. Hamba si nolohasigaza hanji ho hose hono wekaye Waisilayeli. Na mu nzila yiyo, wose ho wolomanyila vyono ane yo nene Muwaha Mulungu.”


Ezekiyeli 7

Ndumilizo ye yisi ya Isilayeli yasejelela

1 Muwaha Mulungu yakanonjela kanyhi yakatya,
2 “Gwe go munhu,

ayi yo nghani yono ane no Muwaha Mulungu

nghuyilonjela yisi ya Isilayeli nha vyono.

Ayi yo ndumilizo ye yisi yose ya Isilayeli!

3 “Gwe go yisi ya Isilayeli,

indumilizo yako yakondya kuza.

Nagwe koliwona likuwo lyangu,

hambi 'ne nha nghukulipiciza ku soko ye zimbi zono wetumaga.

Nane nokuling'aniza mu nghango ye mawifyo gako kuli'ne.

4 Nane nomulipicizenyi du,

hamba si nomuwonelenyi lusungu yaye.

Langa nomulipicizenyi du

ku soko ye makani genyu ge mawifyo gono mwitumaga.

Nanye baho nhondo ho molomanyila

vyono ane yo nene Muwaha Mulungu.”

5 Kanyhi azi zo Muwaha Mulungu yakulonga yakutya,

“Alu wulanje!

Magazo mawaha gokuziza gakwilecela linji ne liyagwe.

6 Hambi zose nha zamalika.

Na wuwu wo mudumo wazo.

Nagwe hodu yo ndumilizo yako.

7 Ne ndumilizo yenyu yikuza kuli'nye nyo

wanhu wose mono mwikaye mu'si.

Ne lidaha lyasejelela,

lono holowa hasina kanyhi wusangalale we zisikuku

u mu matunda gono mugasiwanuye,

langa holowa no wucelewale du.

8 “Nanye lu moliwona likuwo lyangu musunho.

Hambi nha nghumuling'anizizenyi mu nghango ye metumo genyu.

Nane nomuling'anizenyi mu nghango ye mawifyo genyu vyono genha.

9 Nane nomulipicizenyi du,

hamba si nomuwonelenyi lusungu yaye.

Langa nomulipicizenyi du

ku soko ye makani genyu ge mawifyo gono mwetumaga.

Nanye baho nhondo ho molomanyila

vyono ane yo nene no Muwaha Mulungu,

na vyono yo nene mono nghumulipicizenyi.”

10 Langa ta, lizuwa lye magazo lyeza kumuzizenyi 'nye nyo Isilayeli.

Woyi he kuli'nye so magazo gago mawaha.

Hambi wiwoni wenyu nha woduma.

11 Ne magazo gago nha goleta zimbi muno.

Holowa hasina cinji ca muli wene,

cono colosigala yaye, hamba sawo,

hamba lilumbo lyawo, hamba lukumyo lwawo.

12 Lipango likuza.

Nalyo lizuwa lyasejelela lono holowa hasina kanyhi

munhu mono kolowa kugula cinji hamba

mono kolowa kwambaza cinji.

Hambi wulipicize wo Mulungu,

nha wolowaziza wanhu wose.

13 Kanyhi holowa hasina musuluza wo wose

mono kolosuma kuhaliza co cose

cono yali yakondya kutaga.

Hambi likuwo lyo Mulungu lulo

nha lyolowagwila wanhu wose.

Na mwi'pango lilyo,

holowa hasina munhu mubi mono kololekwa yawumipe.

14 Hambi ndulele nha yololila ho yiwawikanize wanhu wose.

Ninga hasina hamba munhu monga

mono kololawila yabite ku wulugu yaye,

hambi likuwo lyo Mulungu nha lyolowagwila wanhu wose.

Wulipicizwe we Waisilayeli so wubi wawo

15 Kunze kolowa wihomi mu zinzila.

Namo mu zikaya molowa matamwa ne lizala.

No munhu wo wose mono kolowa mu mawimbi mwi'daha lilyo,

ayo kolofwila mu wihomi.

Nayo mono kolowa mwi'kaya mwi'daha lilyo,

nayo kolofwa so matamwa ne lizala.

16 Na wanhu wanji wolotiziza mu matunda

nha nziwa zono zogofywa mu wikala mu malolo.

Ne wanhu wose wolowulilila wubi wawo.

17 Ne miwoko yawo yose yolotimba,

ne magulu gawo golocicima.

18 Nawo wolovwala magunila,

akuno wakucicima so woga wuwaha muno.

Woloyiceta mitwe yawo, wolokozwa cevu ciwaha muno.

19 Na kanyhi wolozigumilanga

mu zinzila zahabu zawo hamonga ne zifweza zawo,

hamba kuzisaka kanyhi yaye.

Hambi nha hasina zahabu

hamba fweza zono zosuma kuwaponya yaye,

kolo wukali wo Mulungu wukuwaziza.

Hamba si wolosuma kuzitumamila

ho ziwegutye masulumizi gawo,

hamba kuwegutya mu zinda zawo.

Hambi zizo zi zahabu hamonga ne zifweza,

nha zo zawatajila mu wituma wubi.

20 Lulo, wedunhumulilanga

mu wuswanu we vibwe vyawo vyo wuguzi wukamu,

ninga wakavitumamila mu wunozeza vihwani vye mawifyo.

Na yiyo yo soko yono yikaleka nayo Muwaha Mulungu,

yakayinoza sawo yawo yiwe nha vinhu vyavufye kuli wene.

21 “Nane nolowaleka wajenzi waze wavitece,

nawo wanhu wabi waze wayisole sawo yawo yose, wayavufye.

22 Nane hamba si nolowemila yaye wawo wahizi

kolo weza kuyibena no kuyavufya Nyumba yangu.

23 “Alu nghani zose zolowa zakondya kwitiwa,

hambi yisi nha yolowa yimemile cililo ce mafwa,

nago makaya golowa gali mu wulugu.

24 Ale, nane mwi'pango lilyo

ho nilagaletela baha i makungugo mabi muno,

gaze gazisole zikaya zenyu.

Sunga ne wanhu wenyu we vilungo,

wolowula vilungo lulo,

kolo nawatuma makungugo waze wahavufye

i hanhu henyu hono muhembelaga.

25 Wuhodanjice wolokuza.

Nanye mololondola mulamu,

ninga hamba si molowuwona yaye.

26 Ne magazo golowa kuza getunjiliye gakwilecela linji ne liyagwe.

Ne mapwepwe nago,

golowa menji ako na ako.

Nanye molowa kuwalomba wawizi

ho wamulonjelenyi zono zokuza.

Nawo wakohani,

wolowa wasina nghani yo yose yo kuwejiza wanhu.

Nawo wanyamhala,

lulo wolowa wasina cono wowasugula wanhu.

27 Sunga no mutemi nayo, kolowa kulila du,

nayo u mwana wo mutemi kolowa yasina witumbilo kanyhi.

I wanhu wose nawo,

wolowa wakucicima du so woga wuwaha.

Nane nomulipicizizenyi mu nghango

yono nanye mwawaling'anizaga wanji.

Na zizi zose zolomulajilenyi

nha vyono ane yo nene Muwaha Mulungu.”


Ezekiyeli 8

Wugubulilwe we kejete wa Ezekiyeli kulawa kuli Mulungu

(8:1–10:22)

Wuhembi we Vihwani mu Yelusalemi

1 Na mwi'zuwa lye hano, lya mu mwezi wo mutandatu, wa mu mwaka wo mutandatu, wa mu wuhamywe wetu, mwi'zuwa lilyo, wakawa waza walongola wetu wo wuhamywe wetu, wakulawa ku yisi ya Yuda, wakawa wacehile ha kaya yangu hamonga nane. Nane nghawona vilungo vyo Muwaha Mulungu vyanyiziza nhinicizo kwe vilungo muno.
2 Nane nali neza kugalamila kucanyha, nghagubulilwa, nghawona hwani ya munhu yakwijela nho wijele wo moto. Ne hwani yiyo, ku wulawilila ha ciwino sunga ko ku migulu, yejelaga nha moto. Na mu wulawilila ha ciwino sunga ko kucanyha, yali yinha wijele wo wubulile wo wung'alale nha cuma ca shaba nghusulwe muno.
3 No munhu yuyo, yakagolola cihwani co muwoko wakwe, yakanyibata mu cizukwi ca mu mutwe wangu. Na mu wugubulilwe wuwu, u Muhe wo Mulungu wukanyinula mu mwanya mucanyha, wukanhegula sunga ko ku Yelusalemi. Wukambika ha mulango wo lubavu lwa kusumuka, wo kwinjilila mu Nyumba yo Mulungu, i hanhu hono hali hawicigwe cihwani cono cali ce liwifyo muno hali Mulungu.
4 Na baho nghayiwona kanyhi nhojo yo Mulungu we Waisilayeli, nha vyono nayiwona mwi'zuwa lilyo lono nali nili he kolongo lya Kebali.
5 Na lulo baho, u Mulungu yakanonjela yakatya, “Gwe go munhu, ale wupituce ta wulanje ku lubavu lwa kusumuka.” Nane nali neza kupituka, nanje nhavi, nghaciwona cihwani cono cali ce liwifyo muno hali Mulungu, ciwicigwe behi ne cikumbicilo ca ha mulango wo kwinjilila mu Nyumba yo Mulungu.
6 Mulungu yakanonjela kanyhi yakatya, “Gwe go munhu, wule, wukuziwona zono wakwituma? Wulanje ta zinghani ze mawifyo zono wetumaga baha i wanhu wa Isilayeli, wakuminga 'ne ho mbite kutali ne hanhu hangu hono helile. Ninga hamba nhavivyo agwe wugole du, kogawona ne makani ganji menji ge mawifyo gono gacizize na gaga.”

7 Hanyuma yakawa kusola, yakanhegula sunga ko ku mulango wo kwinjilila mwi'kumbo lya kunze, yakandajila lilanga lyono lyali yitobole mwi'jinji.
8 Yakanonjela yakatya, “Gwe go munhu, ale wuhimbe baho mwijinji.” Nane nali neza kuhimba, nghawona baho mulango.
9 Nayo yakanonjela kanyhi yakatya, “Ale, winjile mugati, wuziwone zimbi zono zili mumo. Zinghani ze cevu zono wanhu wa Isilayeli wazitumaga bahano.”
10 Ale, baho, nane nghawa kwinjila mugati, nghawa kuzilanga za mumo. Nghawona mu majinji mbavu zose wukakate wa wutakalang'onyo wa manhu menji du, nha cinji mazoka, hamonga ne malimu menji du i mavufywe. Hamonga ne mang'onyo-ng'onyo menji du gono Waisilayeli wagahembaga.
11 Nghawawona ne walongola makumi mupunghati we Waisilayeli wono wali wali baho, hamonga na Yazaniya u mwana wa Shafani. Nawo wawa wose wali webate viya vyo kufusiza wuvumba, munhu na cakwe mu miwoko yawo. Ne lyosi lyo wuvumba lyabitaga kucanyha.
12 Nayo Mulungu yakambuza yakatya, “Gwe go munhu, wule, wukuziwona ta zono wakwituma cinyele i walongola we Waisilayeli? Wuwalangaje ta, wose wakuhemba mu nyumba yono yimemile vihwani. Akuno wakwikojela wakutya, ‘Mulungu nha si yakuciwona! Hambi nha yakondya kuyileka yisi.’ ”
13 Hanyuma kanyhi Muwaha Mulungu yakanonjela yakatya, “Ane nghukulonjela nha vyono, koziwona zinghani nyinji ze mawifyo zono zicizize zizi.”

14 Yakasola kanyhi yakanhegula ku mulango wa ku lubavu lwa kusumuka we Nyumba yo Mulungu, yakawa kundajila wacekulu wono wakulila, ku soko ye lifwa lyo mulungu mono yakucemwa Tamuzi.
15 Baho yakambuza yakatya, “Gwe go munhu, wule, wukuziwona zizo? Ninga nghukulonjela nha vyono wukali, ninga koziwona zimbi muno muno zono zicizize na zizi.”
16 Hanyuma kanyhi, yakawa kusola, yakawa kunhegula sunga ko kwi'kumbo lya haciligati ye Nyumba yo Mulungu. Na baho naho, nghawawona wanhu waficile kotya nha wanhu makumi mejete na wahano nhavi, wemile behi no mulango we hanhu hono helile, haciligati ye cikumbicilo no mulango. Wali wemile wahapituciye migongo i hanhu hono helile, akuno wakugundama walanjile ku lubavu lwa kucilima wakulihemba lizuwa.
17 Muwaha Mulungu yakanonjela kanyhi yakatya, “Gwe go munhu, wule, waziwona zizo? Awa i wanhu wa Isilayeli, hamba si wakucilwa yaye ne zinghani ze bwete, zo wituma zinghani zizi ze mawifyo, zono wuziwene baha. Hamonga no kuyimemya wubi yisi yose. No kuzitumila zizo zose bahano mono Nyumba yangu. Ne nghani yiyi yikucila kunghoza likuwo 'ne. Kanyhi lanje vyono, wakucila kunhovula muno mu nzila yono 'mbi muno muno.
18 Ninga wuwalekaje 'gwe, hambi nha wowuwona wukali we likuwo lyangu. Nane si nowaleka hamba kuwawonela lusungu yaye. Nawo hamba wandilile kwe mazi mawaha muno muno, ane hamba si nowahuliciza yaye.”


Ezekiyeli 9

Yelusalemi yikulipicizwa

1 Hobaho nghahulika Mulungu, yakucema ne 'zi yiwaha yakutya, “Anye nyo wanhu mono molyongomeza likaya, ale muze, muleteleze ne vitwalo vyenyu.”
2 Hobaho baho du wakaza wanhu wali mutandatu, wakulawilila ku mulango we Nyumba yo Mulungu wo lubavu lwa kusumuka, akuno wose wadinghuye ne vitwalo vyawo. Na muli wene, mwali mo yuli yunji, mono yali yavwalile myenda ya bafuta, akuno yadinghuye cidawo co wino hamonga ne peni yo kwandicila. Na wose wakenjila, wakema ha cikumbicilo ce shaba.

3 Hanyuma ya zizo, nhojo yo Mulungu we Waisilayeli, yikenuka yikulawa mwi kelubi kono yikalaga, yikawa kubita sunga ha mulango we Nyumba yo Mulungu. Nayo Mulungu yakawa kumucema munhu yuyulya mono yali yavwalile myenda ye bafuta, yakawa kumulonjela yakamutya,
4 “Alu, wubite wukole muciligati ye likaya lyose lya Yelusalemi, wukawawicile vitanjilo mu vihanda vyo wusu wawo, wanhu wose wono wakuwona wusungu no kugazika mu zinhumbula zawo, ku soko ye makani ge mawifyo gono getumwagwa mwi'kaya lili.”
5 Nghamuhulika Mulungu yakuwalonjela awalya i wanji nawo yakuwatya, “Ale, nanye wose mumutyatyaje yuyo muyenyu, nanye mukole muciligati ye likaya muwawulaje wanhu wose, mulece kusigaza hamba monga yaye, hamba kumuwonela lusungu munhu wo wose ng'o.
6 Muwawulagaje i wakombi wose, ne wa wazelelo wose, ne wahinza wose, ne wanyina zo munhu wose, hamonga ne wana wadodo wose. Langa du mulanjilile, mulecele lukulu kuwapamiza wanhu wose, wono wena vitanjilo mu vihanda vyo wusu wawo. Ninga hamba nhavivyo, mwamulizizaje bahano du mono Nyumba yangu.” Hobaho nawo wakawa kwanziza baho, wakawawulaga walongola wose, wono wali wali baho ha Nyumba yo Mulungu.

7 Hanyuma, Mulungu yakawalonjela kanyhi yakawatya, “Alu muyavufyaje Nyumba yangu, muyimemye ne mivimba, hanyuma ho mulawanize mubite muketume nha vyono nonjile!” Hodu, hobaho wakawa kwanza kuwawulaga wanhu mwi'kaya.
8 Na lono mulimo wuwo wali wukali wujendeleye, ane no mweneco, nghawa sigaye baho nili wene du. Nane hobaho nghegwisa hasi wugubama, nghazoza nghatya, “Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu, alu hongo wayikolelwa muno likuwo Yelusalemi, sunga lu weza kusaka wuwawulaje wanhu wose wono wakali wasigaye mu Isilayeli?”
9 Ninga Mulungu yakamhitula yakatya, “I wanhu wa Isilayeli na Yuda, wetuma wubi muno. Hambi yisi yose nha yimemile wu wulaji, kanyhi wakondya kuyinjiza Yelusalemi mu wu wula cihuliko, wakondya kugabena malajilizo gangu. Kanyhi wakutya, ane no Muwaha Mulungu, nakondya kuyileka yisi yawo, na vyono ti, hamba si nghuziwona na zono wetumaga.
10 Ninga nane lu, hamba si nowawonela lusungu yaye, langa nowatendela wene, nha vyono nawo wawatendelaga wanji.”
11 Hanyuma ya zizo, u munhu yuyulya mono yali yavwalile myenda ye bafuta, yakawa kuwuya, yakawa kumuloloseza Muwaha Mulungu yakutya, “Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu, ane nakondya kuzifisiliza zose zono wandajiza nizitume.”


Ezekiyeli 10

Ulukumyo lo Muwaha Mulungu, lukuwuka mu Nyumba yo Mulungu

1 Nane nali neza kulanga ha mwanya wono wali wuli hacanyha ye makelubi, nghawona cinhu cinji cono cejelaga cihwanile libwe lye liwala lyo wulanga cejelaga mucanyha yawo, kotya wijele we hwani ye 'goda lye nhojo.
2 Baho Mulungu yakamulonjela yuyulya munhu mono yali yavwalile myenda ye bafuta, yakamutya, “Ale winjile haciligati ye maguludumu, hasi ye makelubi, wugasole makala go moto, wuyimemye miwoko yako yose vyono myejete, hanyuma wugeyagaze mwi'kaya lyose.”

Nane nghawa nimulanjiliye munhu yuyo lono yabitaga.
3 Nayo lono yenjilaga, i makelubi gali gemile ku lubavu lwa kutakama lwe Nyumba yo Mulungu, nalyo livunde likamema mwi'kumbo lya mugati.
4 Ne nhojo yo Mulungu, yikelajila yikulawa mu makelubi, yikabita sunga ko kunze ye Nyumba yo Mulungu. Hanyuma i Nyumba yose yo Mulungu, yikamemywa ne livunde lilyo. Nalyo likumbo lyose lyang'alalaga so wuzelu we nhojo yo Mulungu.
5 Ne cihumbi co mulilo we mabawa ge makelubi, cikahulikwa sunga mo mwi'kumbo lya kudeha. Hambi cihumbi nha cali cihwanile lizi lyo Mulungu Mudaha Vyose.

6 Nayo Muwaha Mulungu, yali yeza kumulonjela yuyulya munhu, mono yali yavwalile myenda ye bafuta, nha vyono yabite yakeme haciligati ye maguludumu, gono gali gali hasi ye makelubi, u munhu yuyo yakabita yakenjila, yakema behi ne liguludumu linji.
7 Nayo yumonga wa muli wawalya makelubi, yakatajila muwoko wakwe mu moto, wono wali wuli baho behi nawo, yakasola makala ganji, yakamupela mu miwoko munhu mono yali yavwalile myenda ye bafuta, nayo yali yeza kugabocela, hobaho yakawa kutuluka, yakawa kubita.
8 Nane nghawona makelubi gose, gali gena cinhu cono cejelaga nha muwoko wa munhu, hasi ye mabawa gayo gose.
9 Kanyhi, nghawona hali ho gali maguludumu mane gose gehwanile, yikelubi ne maguludumu lyayo lili ha mhelo yayo, no wijele we maguludumu wihwanile liwala lye libwe lya Belulo.
10 Nago maguludumu gago, gejelaga gakung'alala nha mabwe go wuguzi wukamu. Ne maguludumu gose, gali gena liguludumu linji, lyono lyali lili mugati ye liguludumu liyagwe, liguludumu ho lisume kubiduka viswanu, no kutyatya kono linojeye libite.
11 Nawo makelubi lono wabitaga, wasumaga kubita ako na ako, mu zimbavu zose vyono yine, hamba kupituka-pituka ako na ako yaye. Nawo wajendanaga hamonga du ko kose kono wasakaga kubita, hamba kwileka yaye, akuno wakawa wasina soko yo kupituka-pituka yaye.
12 Nayo imiwili yawo, ne vyanza vyawo, ne miwoko yawo, ne mabawa, ne maguludumu, vyose vyali vimemile meso kose kose.
13 Ne maguludumu gaga, gali gehwanile ne maguludumu gono nagawona ha wugubulilwe wangu wulongozi.

14 Ne makelubi gose, gali gena wusu wuli hane hane, wusu wulongozi, wali wusu wa ng'ombe nume, we kejete, wusu wa munhu, we kadatu, wusu wa simba, na we kane, wusu wa 'ndi.
15 (Ne makelubi gaga, gali gehwanile na gono nagawona he kolongo lya Kebali.) Nago makelubi lono gagulukaga mucanyha,
16 hobaho nago maguludumu vyagulukanaga mucanyha hamonga ne makelubi. Sunga na lono makelubi gatandakulaga mabawa gayo, gawa kuguluka, hamba nhavivyo, ne maguludumu nago vyo vyali vili hamonga nago du.
17 Na lono makelubi galekaga kujenda, nhavivyo nago maguludumu, galekaga kujenda. Na lono makelubi gagulukaga, ne maguludumu gago nago vikabitana hamonga. Hambi imakelubi nha go gagalongozaga i maguludumu.

18 Nayo nhojo yo Muwaha Mulungu yikawa kuwuka ha mulango we Nyumba yo Mulungu, yikabita kwima hacanyha ye makelubi.
19 Ale, baho, nago makelubi gakawa kutandakula i mabawa gawo, gakawa kuguluka lulo baho lono nali nghali nigalanjile, vikawa kubitana ne maguludumu hamonga. Gakema hadodo ha mulango wa kucilima we Nyumba yo Mulungu, ne nhojo yo Muwaha Mulungu yali yili hacanyha yawo.
20 Nane nghawa kwizuka nha vyono kumbe wo wa walya makelubi wono nawawona hasi yo Mulungu we Waisilayeli he kolongo lya Kebali.
21 Ne makelubi gago gose, gali gena wusu wuli hane hane, ne mabawa mane mane, hamonga ne cinhu cinji cono cejelaga nha muwoko wa munhu, hasika ye mabawa gawo gose.
22 Sunga no wusu wawo, wejelaga wihwanile no wusu wuwulya du wono nawuwona he kolongo lya Kebali. Nawo makelubi wawo, wajendanaga wose hamonga walanjile ku mwande du.


Ezekiyeli 11

Yelusalemi yikuling'anizwa

1 Kanyhi muhe wukanyinula wukandeta sunga ko ku mulango wo lubavu lwa kucilima ye Nyumba yo Mulungu. Nali neza kufika kuko, nghawona wanhu makumi mejete na wahano, wemile behi no mulango. Na muli wene, mwali mo yuli na Yazaniya u mwana wa Azuli, hamonga na Pelatiya u mwana we Benaya, i walongola wawaha we nghungugo.
2 U Muwaha Mulungu yakanyitya, “Gwe go munhu, i wanhu wawa, nha wakwihwesa zo wuhile wubi du, no kuwalanjiza i wanhu wa mwi'kaya lili, zinghani zono sizinojeye yaye.
3 Hambi wene nha wakutya ti, ‘Cozizenga kanyhi zikaya zetu musunho. Hambi likaya nha lyehwana nyungu nhelece mu moto, nase ceza nha nyama yono nhelekwe mu nyungu yiyo, ninga hamba nhavivyo, nyungu yiyo yicicitiye, ho cilece kupya no moto weneco.’
4 Holeka 'gwe go munhu, wuwahosaje so nghani.”

5 U muhe wo Muwaha Mulungu, wukanyiziza kwa vilungo, nayo Muwaha Mulungu yakanonjela niwalonjele wanhu zinghani zakwe azino; yakatya, “Anye nyo wanhu wa Isilayeli, ane manyile zose zono mukuzilonga, sunga na zose zono mukuziwikaniza.
6 Hambi nha mwakondya kuwawulaga wanhu wenji mu muno mwi'kaya lili, sunga ne likaya lyose, lyamema mivimba ya wanhu.

7 “Holeka nane no Muwaha Mulungu wenyu, nghumulonjelenyi nghutya nhavi. Mbeka likaya lili, lyaweza nyungu nhelece mu moto, ne wanhu wono wowa yo nyama yono yotelekwa mumo, yo yiyo mivimba ye wanhu wono mwakondya kuwawulaga! Ninga nanye lu, hamba si mokwikala mwi'kaya lili yaye, langa nomutajenyi kunze ye likaya!
8 Kanyhi 'nye muli wadodi ku mumaje, holeka 'ne, nowaleta wanhu wono wowa wena ne mimaje yawo, ho wamuhomenyi.
9 Nolomuwusenyi mwi'kaya, nimuwicenyi mu miwoko ye wanhu wajenzi. Kanyhi nakondya kumuling'anizenyi wufwe.
10 Nanye wose molowulajilwa mu wulugu, mu yisi yenyu nyenye nyo weneco. Na mu nzila yiyo, nhondo i wanhu wose ho wolomanyila vyono, ane yo nene Muwaha Mulungu.
11 Sunga ne likaya lili hamba si lyolomuponyenyi yaye, nha vyono nyungu yiyicitilaga moto, nyama yono nhelece mugati yayo. Ninga ane nolomulipicizenyi du hanhu ho hose hono molowa mu yisi ya Isilayeli.
12 Nanye nhondo, ho molomanyila vyono, ane yo nene Muwaha Mulungu, kanyhi na vyono lono mwali mwakondya kupegwa malajilizo gangu, hamba si mwagebata yaye, langa mukajendelela du, ku wituma ku hwani ye malajilizo ge makungugo gono gamupilimenyi.”
13 Nane, Ezekiyeli lono nawilaga, baho du Pelatiya yakagwa hasi yakafwa. Nane hobaho nghawa kwigwisa hasi wugubama, nghazoza nghatya, “Gwe go Muwaha Mulungu, alu mwene, ho kowawulaga wanhu wose wono wakali wasigaye mu yisi ya Isilayeli?”

U wihuwizo wo Mulungu kuli Wahamywa

14 Muwaha Mulungu yakanonjela kanyhi yakatya,
15 “Gwe go munhu, i wanhu wono wekaye mu Yelusalemi, wakulonga i zako hamonga na ze wayago Waisilayeli wose wono wahamywa. Wakutya ti, ‘Alu i wanhu wose wono wahamywa, nha weza wali kutali muno, hamba si wosuma kanyhi kumuhemba Muwaha Mulungu. Holeka Muwaha Mulungu, yakondya kucipela yisi yiyi yose yiwe yetu sese.’

16 “Holeka, wuwalonjele wanduguzo wono wali kuko ku wuhamywe, wuwatye nha vyono, yo nene mono nawategula kuko, ho wakekale kutali mu makungugo. Kanyhi wuwalonjele nha vyono, hamba yiwe yo nene mono naweyagaziza mu ma'si ganji, ninga si lyopuluta yipango yiwaha muno, nane nowa nili hamonga nawo kanyhi, mumo mu ma'si kono wowa wekaye.
17 Holeka wuwalonjelaje zose zono nghulonga ane no Muwaha Mulungu. Hambi nha nowakongholeza wose hamonga, ku wulawa mu ma'si kono nali niweyagazize, niwagomolele kanyhi yisi yawo ya Isilayeli.
18 Na kolo wawuya ku yisi yawo, yolowa yiwanojeye waze wawusize lukulu vinhu vyose vyavu, hamonga ne vihwani vyose vye mawifyo, vyono wolovyagana mumo mu yisi yawo.
19 Nane nolowapela nhumbula mhya hamonga no wuhile wupya. Nolowawusiza lukulu u wu wula cihuliko cawo, hamonga ne nhumbula ye 'bwe yono yili muli wene. Nolowapela nhumbula yo winenenyhezi na ye cihuliko.
20 Nawo nhondo, ho wolowukamisa wulajilize wangu, no kugatyatya ko wihuwilwa malajilizo gangu. Nawo wolowa wanhu wangu, nane nolowa Mulungu wawo.
21 Ninga langa, nolowalipiciza i wanhu wose, wono mitima yawo yendile kuhemba vinhu vyavu, hamonga ne vihwani vye mawifyo. Nolowalipiciza mu nghango ye metumo gawo.” Nhavivi vyo yakutya u Muwaha Mulungu.

U lukumyo lo Mulungu lukuwuka mu Yelusalemi

22 Hobaho i makelubi nago, gakawa kuguluka, vikabitana hamonga ne maguludumu gagalya. Ne nhojo yo Mulungu wa Isilayeli, yali yili hacanyha yago.
23 Hobaho i nhojo yikawa yawuka mwi'kaya, yikabita ku lubavu lwa kucilima ye litunda.
24 Hanyuma kanyhi, mu wugubulilwe wangu, u muhe wo Mulungu wukandinghula kanyhi, wukangomola sunga ko ku wahamywa, ku Babiloni. Hobaho baho wugubulilwe wangu wukawa kumbucila.
25 Nane nghawa kuwalonjela wandugu zangu i wahamywa zose zono Muwaha Mulungu yandajila.


Ezekiyeli 12

U muwizi yakwihwananizwa no munhu mutizizi

1 U Muwaha Mulungu yakanonjela kanyhi yakatya,
2 “Gwe go munhu, wumanye vyono wikaye hamonga ne wanhu wafyuzi. Hambi wene nha wena meso go kuwona, ninga hamba si wawonaga yaye. Wena ne makutu go kuhulicila, ninga hamba si wahulikaga ng'o, vyono wene nha weza wafyuzi.

3 “Holeka 'gwe go munhu, alu wuwopele viya vyako, winoze wuwe nha wukutiza, hanyuma wuwe kutuluka ne misi ha meso gawo. I wanhu wose ho wakuwone vyono weza kuwuka no kubita hanhu hanji. Na lunji holeka wawo wanhu wafyuzi, wotanga nhunizo yayo.
4 Holeka wuviwopelezaje viya vyako, nhe vivya vyono vikuhamywa mu madaha ge misi, wose ho wasume kukuwona, kanyhi wasume kukuwona na lono wukuwuka wukutuluka mu madaha ge cinyamihe, nha munhu mono wukubita ku wuhamywe.
5 Nawo lono wowa wakali wakulanjiliye, agwe wutobole lidilisha mwi'jinji lye kaya yako, wuwe kuvikoza viya vyako baho he'dilisha lilyo.
6 Wene wawe wakulanjiliye lono wukuvidinghula viya vyako mwi'wega, sunga wutuluce wuwe kwiyajiliza mu citi, cono cokwiwajiza ne meso gako, wulece kuwona na kono wukubita. Ne nghani yiyi yono kokwituma, mbeka yowa suguzi mbaha muno kuli Waisilayeli.”
7 Nane nghawa kunoza zose, nha vyono Muwaha Mulungu yanonjela nitume. Nane mwi'zuwa lilyo, nghawa kuviwikaniza viya vyangu nha nili muhami. Na mwi'zuwa lilyo, lipango lye cinyamihe, lono citi cali ceza kwinjila, nghawa kutobola lilanga kwe miwoko yangu, nghawa kulawila kunze, ne wanhu wakumbona. Nane nghawa kuviwegala viya yangu, mu mawega ha meso gawo, nghawa kutuluka.

8 Ne mitondo yaco hakali mitondo, u Muwaha Mulungu yakanonjela kanyhi yakatya,
9 “Gwe go munhu, alu wulanje ti, vyono Waisilayeli wakukuwuza, wakusaka wamanye cono wukwituma.
10 Nagwe wuwalonjele zose zono ane no Muwaha Mulungu nghuwalonjela mu Yelusalemi, hamonga ne wanhu wose wono wekaye mumo.
11 Wuwalonjele nha vyono, zose zono witumile, co citanjilo ca zose zono zowaziza wene. Hambi nha wolowa wahamizi kanyhi wolowa wawanda.
12 Na yuyo muwaha mono yawatemile, kolowegala viya vyakwe mwi'wega ne cilo, no kukolela mwi'langa lyono wolowa wamutobolela mwi'jinji, yawe kutiza. Nayo kolowa yafufunize meso gakwe, hamba si kolosuma kuwona na kono yakubita.
13 Ninga notandaza lwavu lwangu hanhu hose, ho nimugwizize mumo. Hanyuma niwe kumutegula sunga ko kwi'kaya lya Babiloni, i hanhu hono kolofwila, hamba kuyiwona ne yisi yeneco vyono yinha.
14 Nane noloweyagaza ako na ako ku zimbavu zose, wose wono wamupilime wamutaze, hamonga ne walindilizi wakwe wose. Ne wanhu wolowalondola ho wawawulaje.

15 “Nane kolo nakondya kuweyagaziza mu makungugo ganji, na mu zisi zinjenzi, nawo ho wolomanyila vyono ane yo nene Muwaha Mulungu.
16 Ninga si nolowawulaga wose mu wulugu, hamba mu nzala, hamba mu matamwa, langa nolowasigaza wanji wa muli wene, lono wali kuko mu makungugo, ho wanzalice nha vyono zinghani ze mawifyo zono wetumaga, zali nghani 'mbi muno. Kanyhi ho waze wamanye vyono ane yo nene Muwaha Mulungu.”

I citanjilo co muwizi mono yakucicima

17 U Muwaha Mulungu yakanonjela kanyhi yakatya,
18 “Gwe go munhu, wulye ndigwa yako ko wucicime, kanyhi wung'we ne malenga gako ko woga.
19 Wuwalonjele we nghungugo yose, nha vyono, ayi yo nghani yo Muwaha Mulungu, kuli wanhu wose wa Yelusalemi, wono wakali wekaye mu yisi yawo. Wuwalonjele wutye vyono wowa kucicima, lono wakulya zindigwa zawo, kanyhi wowa wamemile woga, lono wakung'wa malenga. Ne yisi yawo yolekwa nyhiwa. Hambi wanhu wose wono wekaye mumo nha si wakugebata malajilizo gangu.
20 Holeka makaya gose gono aluno gamemile wanhu, gose gokongomezwa, ne yisi nayo, yowa nyhiwa yisina wanhu. Nawo baho nhondo, ho womanyila vyono, ane yo nene Muwaha Mulungu.”

Mbazi yono wanhu wayizoweye, ne nghani yono si wayizoweye

21 U Muwaha Mulungu, yakanonjela kanyhi yakatya,
22 “Gwe go munhu, nhawule lu, i wanhu wa Isilayeli, wayizoweye muno mbazi yiyi yono yikutya, ‘Vipango vikupuluta, no wuwile wayo weza bwete?
23 Alu wuwalonjelaje nha vyono, ane no Muwaha Mulungu noyidumya kamonga i mbazi yiyo. Nayo hamba si yilahulicilwa lukulu kanyhi mu Isilayeli. Langa wamanye nha vyono, lipango lyakondya kuza, lyono u wuwizi wuwo wofisilizwa!’

24 “Wuwalonjele nha vyono, mwi'daha lilyo, holowa hasina kanyhi wugubulilwe wo wulambi, hamba wuwile wo wajilize.
25 Hambi ane no Muwaha Mulungu, nha yo nene du mono nolowa kuwalonjela zo wuhile wangu, na zono nolowa kuwalonjela, zose, zolowa kufisilizwa, hamba kucela yaye. Ninga mumanye anye nyo we cibede, nha vyono ane mu vilo vyenyu, nolomutendelenyi zose, nha vyono nali nakondya kumusugulenyi lu lulya. Vyo nghutya ane no Muwaha Mulungu.”

26 U Muwaha Mulungu yakanonjela kanyhi yakatya,
27 “Gwe go munhu, i Waisilayeli wakwihwesa nha vyono wugubulilwe wako, hamonga no wuwile wako, vikali vili kutali si vyofisilizwa mbela mbela yaye.
28 Ninga agwe wuwalonjele nha vyono ane no Muwaha Mulungu nghutya, i vilo vivyo, hamba si vyotinya kuza. Na zono nakondya kuzilonga, mbeka zofisilizwa du. Vyo nghutya ane no Muwaha Mulungu!”


Ezekiyeli 13

U wuwizi wo wulambi

1 U Muwaha Mulungu, yakanonjela kanyhi yakatya,
2 “Gwe go munhu, wuwawilile no kuwahosa i wawizi we Waisilayeli wono wewililaga caka, nha vyono wakwihwesa weneco du mu zinhumbula zawo, wuwalonjele nha vyono, yiwanojeye wateje makutu, wayihulicize i nghani yo Muwaha Mulungu du.”
3 Wuwalonjele kanyhi, wamanye nha vyono Muwaha Mulungu yakutya, “Woyi he, kuli wawo i wawizi walele! Hambi nha wawilaga za mu mahweso gawo weneco du, na kumbe hamba si wagubuliywe nghani yo yose yaye.
4 Nye nyo wanhu wa Isilayeli, i wawizi wenyu wawo, yo nghani ya bwete du, hambi nha wehwana nhe macewe gono gekalaga mu matongo ge likaya.
5 Wono si wakusuma hamba kugalanjilila ne majinji gono gamwaguka, hamba kugazenga kanyhi yaye. Holeka ku soko yiyo, i Waisilayeli si wolosuma kwitaza yaye, lono wulugu weza kuza mwi'zuwa lyo Muwaha Mulungu.
6 Hambi wugubulilwe wawo nha wugubulilwe wo wulambi du, no wuwile wawo wose nha wuwile wa bwete du. Ati wakwiyitya nha vyono wakuwila zinghani zangu, ninga 'ne no mweneco, ho si niwatumile yaye. Hamba nhavivyo wene wakwihwesa nha vyono zinghani zawo zowa za mbeka!
7 Ninga wuwalonjele vyono'ne nghutya, wugubulilwe wenyu wowo wono mukuwuwona, hamonga no wuwile wenyu, zo nghani za bwete, kanyhi za wulambi du. Hambi nha mukutya nha vyono, ati ane Muwaha Mulungu yo nene mono nimutumenyi, na kumbe ane nghali hamba si namutumenyi, hamba kumulonjelenyi cinji yaye.”

8 Holeka u Muwaha Mulungu yakuwalonjela i wanhu we liwala lilyo yakutya, “Nghani zenyu nha za wulambi. No wugubulilwe wenyu, nawo nha wa wulambi du. Holeka nane nghumuling'anizenyi.
9 Nomuling'anizenyi no kumulipicizenyi, anye wose nyo wawizi mono mulongaga zo wugubulilwe wenyu wo wulambi, no kuwajiliza wanhu ku soko yo wuwile wenyu wono si we mbeka. Holeka ku soko yiyo, nanye si mowalilizwa yaye hamonga ne wanhu wangu, mu wihangulize wawo, sunga ne matagwa genyu, si gokwandikwa yaye mu zinghomo ze wanhu wa Isilayeli. Nanye hamba si molowuyila lukulu ku yisi yenyu. Nanye nhondo baho, ho molomanyila vyono, ane yo nene Muwaha Mulungu.

10 “I wawizi wakuwakonga wanhu wangu, wakutya ho wuli mulamu, na kumbe ho hasina mulamu yaye! Alu wanhu wangu wazenga lijinji ne mabwe gono gatimbile. Wawizi nawo walihiliwa lijinji ne swakala.
11 Ninga wuwalonjelaje wawo wawizi, nha vyono lijinji lyawo lyogwa du. Hambi 'ne nha noleta mvula mbaha ye mabwe, ho yilikumhe lijinji lilyo, ne mbeho yo mukukunzo yololiputa kwa vilungo.
12 Lijinji lyologwa hasi, ne wanhu wose wolomuwuzenyi zo wuswanu we swakala yono mwahiliwa, cali cicici.”

13 Holeka nayo Muwaha Mulungu yakutya nhavi, “Nane nomuletelenyi libeho lyo mukukunzo mwi'kuwo lyangu, hamonga ne mvula mbaha, yono yowa yihangahanjile ne mabwe ge mvula, vilikumule lijinji.
14 Nane nolikumula lijinji lilyo lyono walihiliwe ne swakala, noliyagaza, nigalece mabwe go musinji wayo gasina cinji mucanyha yayo. Nalyo lijinji lyologwa limuwulajenyi nyenye wose. Na mu nzila yiyo, wanhu wose, ho wolomanyila vyono ane yo nene Muwaha Mulungu.

15 “Nalyo lijinji, hamonga na wono walihiliwa swakala, baho ho wololiwonela likuwo lyangu. Nane baho nhondo, ho nilakulonjelela nha vyono, lijinji lili hasina kanyhi, hamonga na wawo wono walihiliwa swakala.
16 Nha cinji wawo wawizi wono wayihuwizaga Yelusalemi zo mulamu, na kumbe hasina mulamu!” Zo Muwaha Mulungu yazilonjile.

U wuwizi kuli i wawizi we cicekulu wo wulambi

17 U Muwaha Mulungu kanyhi yakatya, “Alu 'gwe go munhu, luno ta, wuwalangaje i wacekulu wose wa mu wanhu wako, wono wawilaga. Wuwaling'anize nawo,
18 wuwalonjele zono u Muwaha Mulungu yakuwalonjela nawo, yakutya,

“Woyi hee! Kuli'nye nyo wacekulu, mono muwahonelaga wanhu zindasimi, no kuwanozeza wanhu zimhiji, ho wazivwale mu mitwe yawo, ho wawe ne vilungo vyo kuwuhodanjila wumi we wanhu wanji. Hambi nha mwendile muwe ne vilungo vyo wumi na vye lifwa, kuli wanhu wangu, so cambuzi cenyu nyenye nyo weneco.
19 Kanyhi mwakondya kumbedegala'ne hamwande ye wanhu wangu ku so wubocela shayili ndodo du yono yimemile mu maganza, hamonga ne vibegulwa vicefu vye mikate. Mukuwawulaga wanhu wangu wono si wanojeye kufwa. Mukuwaleka womi wono si wanojeye walekwe wekale womi. Na ku soko yiyo, mukuwakonga-konga i wanhu wangu, nawo wakumwitumbilenyi.”

20 Ku soko yiyo, nayo Muwaha Mulungu yakutya nhavi, “Ane nizisulile muno zimhiji, hamonga ne zindasimi, zono mukwitumamila, ati ho musume kuwa ne vilungo vyo wumi ne lifwa. Ninga 'ne nowawusa mu miwoko yenyu, niwakomboze i wanhu wono mwali muwatemile.
21 Noziwusiza lukulu zimhiji zenyu, niwalece kamonga wanhu wangu wawuce mu miwoko yenyu, sunga cibitilila. Nanye baho nhondo, ho momanyila vyono ane yo nene Muwaha Mulungu.

22 “Alu, mulanje ta, ku soko yo wulambi wenyu, mwakondya kuwahodanga i wanhu wanji i waswanu, wono nali si nyendile niwatamise. Na kanyhi, mukuwemila i wanhu wabi, walece kunena zo wubi wawo, ho wawuponye wumi wawo.
23 Ninga luno mumanye, nha vyono wugubulilwe wenyu wo wulambi, hamonga no wuwizi wenyu wo wulambi, vyose vyaficila ndumo yayo. Nane nghuwaponya wanhu wangu mu wulawa mu vilungo vyenyu, ho musume kumanya nha vyono, ane yo nene Muwaha Mulungu.”


Ezekiyeli 14

U Mulungu yakuwuhosa wuhembi we vihwani

1 Wanji wa muli walongola we Waisilayeli, wakanyiziza wanonjele za kuli wuhile wo Muwaha Mulungu.
2 Nayo Muwaha Mulungu yakanonjela yakatya,
3 “Gwe go munhu, i wanhu wawa, wakondya kuvyenda vihwani ne mitima yawo yose, navyo vyo vikuwatonga ku wituma wubi. Alu wakwihwesa vyono ane nane ndahile kuwapitula hongo?
4 Alu nagwe wuwalonjelaje Waisilayeli wose wuwatye nhavi, ‘Ane no Muwaha Mulungu, yo nene mono nomupitula munhu wo wose, mono yakondizye kuvipitucila vihwani no mutima wakwe, no kutongwa navyo ku wituma wubi, kanyhi ho kawa kuza kujela kumuwuza muwizi. Nomupitula nha vyono yanojeye, mu nghango yo winji we vihwani vyakwe!’
5 Ninga vihwani vivyo, hamba viwe vyakondya kuwanoza Waisilayeli ho wandece 'ne, ninga nitumbiye nha vyono lipitulo lyangu lyowanoza Waisilayeli wawe ne cogopo kanyhi kuli'ne.

6 “Holeka aluno, wuwalonjele Waisilayeli zono nghuwalonjela ane no Muwaha Mulungu, nghutya, ‘Ale mbuyilenyi 'nye nyo wanhu wangu, mumhitucile nene, muwuce mu vihwani vyenyu vye mawifyo.’

7 “Ninga yunji wo wose wa muli Waisilayeli, hamba wa muli wajenzi, wono wekalane hamonga ne wanhu we mhuga ye Waisilayeli, mono kondeka 'ne, no kuhemba vihwani, hanyuma kanyhi ho kawa kubita kuwuza kuli muwizi, nene no mweneco yo nomupitula munhu yuyo.
8 Nolewana no munhu yuyo, nimunoze yawe co citanjilo, kanyhi nimutemanulile lukulu na mu mhuga ye wanhu wangu, nanye ho mumanye nha vyono ane yo nene Muwaha Mulungu.

9 “No muwizi hono yakocwe-kocwe, no kulonga nghani yono si ye mbeka, ale mumanye nha vyono nene no Muwaha Mulungu, yo nimukocize u muwizi yuyo. Nane nomongomeza no kumutemanulila lukulu mu wanhu wa Isilayeli.
10 U muwizi yuyo, hamonga no munhu yuyo, mono yabitile kumuwuza muwizi, wose vyono wejete, wotuga wulipicizwe wono wiling'aniye.
11 Nane nokwituma zizi, i Waisilayeli ho walece kundeka'ne, no kwiyavufya so wubi wawo. Hambi nha yiwanojeye wawe wanhu wangu, nane niwe Mulungu wawo.” Yakutya Muwaha Mulungu.

Noha, Danyeli na Jobu

12 U Muwaha Mulungu yakanonjela kanyhi yakatya,
13 “Hono yisi yinozeze wubi, no kuwa si nyihuwilwa kuli'ne, ale, nane nowugolola muwoko wangu, niyiwusize witumbilo wayo we zindigwa, niyiletele nzala, niwawulaje wanhu, hamonga ne mitugo.
14 Hamba i wanhu wawa wadatu nha cinji Noha, Danyeli, na Jobu, wali wawe wakali wekaye baho, u wuswanu wawo si wali wutaze cinji co cose, langa wuswanu wawo wali wuwaponye wumi wawo weneco du. Vyo yakutya u Muwaha Mulungu.
15 Hamba lunji, nosuma kutuma malimu ga mu mbago, gawahawanje wanhu, no kuyinoza yisi yiwe ya kogofya, no kuwanoza wanhu walece kujenda-jenda ako na ako.
16 Hamba i wanhu wawo wadatu, Noha, Danyeli, na Jobu, wali wawe wakali wekaye mumo, mbeka mbeka, vyono mumipaga, ane no Muwaha Mulungu, nawo nhavivilya, si wali wasume kuwaponya hamba ne wana wawo weneco. Langa wali weponye wumi wawo weneco du, nayo yisi yali yikale wuciwa.

17 “Hamba lunji nosuma kuleta wulugu mu yisi yiyo, hamonga ne vitwalo vyono vyongomezaga, ho viwamale i wanhu wose, hamonga ne mitugo zose.
18 Hamba iwanhu wawo wadatu, Noha, Danyeli, na Jobu, wali wawe wakali wekaye mumo, mbeka mbeka, vyono mumipaga ane no Muwaha Mulungu, si wali wasume kuwaponya hamba i wana wawo, langa wali weponye wumi wawo weneco du.

19 “Na hono nali ndete wutamwa wa tawuni, mu yisi yose, niwawulajile wanhu ne mitugo mwi'kuwo lyangu.
20 Na hamba Noha, Danyeli na Jobu, wali wawe wali mumo, mbeka mbeka, vyono mumipaga ane no Muwaha Mulungu, ane no Mulungu mumi, si wali wasume kwi'ponya weneco hamonga ne wana wawo. Langa u wuswanu wawo, wali wuwaponye wumi wawo weneco du.”
21 Na zino, zo Muwaha Mulungu yakulonga yakutya, “Noloyiletela Yelusalemi ndipiciza zangu yine, zinzito muno. Yinongozi, wulugu, ye kejete, nzala, ye kadatu, malimu makali ga mu mbago, na ye kane, litamwa, vize viwongomeze wanhu hamonga ne mitugo yose.
22 No munhu wo wose mono kopona hamonga ne wanagwe, ale wuwalanjililaje lono wakuza kuli'gwe. Wuyiwone nghango yo wubi wawo vyono winha. Baho nagwe ho komanyila nha vyono kumbe u wulipicize wono nghuwuleta mu Yelusalemi, wo wulipicizwe we ling'ani.
23 Kanyhi ho komanyila nha vyono kumbe, hali ho yili soko mbaha muli zose zono netuma.” U Muwaha Mulungu yo yalonjile zizi.


Ezekiyeli 15

I cihwanicizo co Muzabibu

1 U Muwaha Mulungu yakanonjela kanyhi yakatya,
2 “Gwe go munhu, mwene, u muzabibu wina lilumboci sinho mubici wunji? Hamba wuswanuci wono wuli mwi'nhambi lyo mubici wo muzabibu, hono wukuwuling'aniza ne mibici ya mu mbago?
3 Wule, kosuma kuwitumamila u mubici wayo, u mulimo wunji wo wose? Hamba kosuma kuwunoza cisomeko co kutumbicila vinhu vyo vyose?
4 Langa wene, muswanu du ku ngwagu, so wupembela moto. Nawo moto, kolo wazikongojeza zisonje zayo zose vyono nyejete, hasigala haciligati yayo, hono naho hakondya kutakamila, wule, kosuma kuwitumamila so wunozeza cinhu co cose mbi?
5 Ayo yowa nha nghani ya bwete du, vyono sunga na lono wali wukali si natakamila, vyo wali wusina mulimo wunji wo wose. Holeka lu lono wakondya kutakamila, ho wokwila mulimoci waco, langa yo nghani ya bwete du.”

6 Naluno u Muwaha Mulungu, vyo yakulonga yakutya, “Vyono, u muzabibu wusolwaga ku mbago, wuwa kutakamizwa mu moto, ale nane, vyo nowasola i wanhu wose wono wekaye mu Yelusalemi,
7 niwalipicize. Wene wakondya kwiponya mu wulawa mu moto, ninga luno, moto wowongomezeza lukulu. Nane kolo nghuwalipiciza, nagwe baho nhondo, ho kolomanyila nha vyono yo nene Muwaha Mulungu.
8 Hambi nha weza si wehuwilwa kanyhi kuli'ne, holeka nane noyinoza yisi yiwe nyhiwa.” Vyo yakutya u Muwaha Mulungu.


Ezekiyeli 16

Wuwula wihuwilo, wa wekazi wa mwi'kaya lya Yelusalemi

1 U Muwaha Mulungu yakanonjela kanyhi yakatya,
2 “Gwe go munhu, wuyimanyizaje Yelusalemi mawifyo gayo gono yitumile.
3 Wuyilonjele Yelusalemi zono u Muwaha Mulungu yakuyilonjela yakutya, ‘Agwe Yelusalemi, u wimo wako no lulelo lwako, walawa mu yisi ya Kanani. U sogo yali Mwamoli, u nyina yako yali Muhiti.
4 Nagwe lono walelelwa, hali hasina munhu mono yakudumula lunyeku, hamba kukuhovuga, hamba kukutajila mwino, hamba kukuwopa ne cimwenda yaye.
5 Hali hasina munhu wo wose mono yakuwonela lusungu yaye, ho yakutendele hamba monga bwete ya muli zizo. Na lono walelelwa, hali hasina hamba monga mono yali yakwendile. Ninga watagwa kunze mwi'balangu du.’

6 “Nane nali neza kukola baho, nghakuwona agwe wukugalangala mu sakami yako. Wali wukwiliye sakami yose muwili wose. Ninga nane, hamba si nakuleka yaye ho wufwe.
7 Langa nghakunoza wuwiluce nho mubici muswanu. Nagwe wukawiluka wina ne vilungo, wukatalimha muno, wukawa muhinza muswanu muno. Ne vitombo vyako, vikawa vimile viswanu muno, ne zimvwili zako nazo zikawiluka viswanu, ninga hamba nhavivyo wali wuli cipwi du.

8 “Ninga nali neza kukola lwe kejete, nghawona nha vyono, alu cipango cakondya kufika, co kukwenda. Hobaho nghawugubika muwili wako ne likoti lyangu, nghakwihuwiza, nghanoza cilagane nagwe nha vyono nokutola, nagwe ku wulawilila baho, wukawa wuli wangu.” Azi zo Muwaha Mulungu yakutya.

9 “Hodu, hobaho nghasola malenga nghakuhovuga, nghayeza sakami yose mu muwili wako. Nghakubaka mafuta.
10 Nghakuvwika musweko muhambwe, nghakupela ne malatu gono mahone ne nyhingo swanu muno, hamonga ne citambala ce bafuta co kwiwopa ku mutwe, no mwenda we halili.
11 Nghakuhamba ne vibwe vyo wuguzi wukamu, nghakuvwika bangili mu miwoko yako, no mukufu mu singo yako.
12 Nghakupela sunga ne nyamwidole yo kuvwala mu mhula yako, ne viheleni, hamonga no mukumbo muswanu wo kuvwala.
13 Nhavivi vyo wahambwa zahabu ne fweza, ne vivwalo vyako vye bafuta swanu, hamonga no wihambo. Wukalya wusaje wuswanu wono wunamuce, hamonga no wuci ne mafuta. Wukawa muswanu muno, sunga wukawa mugolece wo mutemi.
14 Nagwe wukawa ne lilumbo yiwaha muno muli zinghungugo zose, ku soko yo wuswanu wako. Hambi nha yo nene mono nakunoza ho wijele wuli muswanu muno.” Azi zo yakulonga u Muwaha Mulungu.

15 “Ninga lu nagwe wukwiyitumbila so wuswanu wako, sunga nhondo aluno, weza kusenyha no munhu wo wose mono kopuluta baho.
16 Wukawa kusola sunga ne myenda yako yinji, no kuyihamba mu hanhu hako hono kuhembelaga, nha vyono wenha i wacekulu wasenyha. Wukawa kwilavya kuli munhu wo wose du.
17 Wukawa kuyisola zahabu, hamonga no wihambo wono nakupela, wukayinoza vihwani vye cilume wulawa kusenyha navyo.
18 Wukasola myenda yo wihambo yono nakupela, wukawa kuvivwika vihwani. Wukavilavizya vihwani, i mafuta hamonga no wuvumba wono nakupela.
19 Ane nghakupela ndigwa yo wusaje wono wunamuce, hamonga ne mafuta, no wuci, ninga 'gwe, wukavilavizya vihwani, ho wutuje wendezwe kuli vyene.” Azi zo yakutya u Muwaha Mulungu.

20 “Hanyuma kanyhi, wukawasola i wanago we cilume, ne we cicekulu wono wandelela, no kuwalavya kuli vihwani. Wule, zizi si zikulajila u wubi wono wukwituma, nha vyono weza si wuli mwihuwilwa kuli'ne,
21 hamba wali wulece kuwasola wanangu no kuwalavya kuli vihwani?
22 Na mu mapango gako gose ge mikalile yako, ye cevu yo wusenyha, hamba si wayikumbucilila lukulu i mikalile yako ya ha wudodo, nha vyono wali wuli cipwi, wukwigalagaza mu sakami yako.”

I mikalile yo wusenyha ya Yelusalemi

23 Muwaha Mulungu yakukutya, “Woyi hee! Woyi hee! Gwe Yelusalemi! Hambi nha wetuma wubi wuwo wose,
24 no kuzenga hanhu ho kuhembela vihwani, i hanhu hose, mu zinzila zimbaha zose, hamonga no kwituma makani go wusenyha.
25 Wakondya lu kuwutipiza wuswanu wako mu madabaga. Ku soko yo kwilavya gwegwe mweneco kuli munhu wo wose mono yakukola baho, no wusenyha wako wukawa wucizize nhondo zose.
26 Kanyhi wukagona ne Wamisili, no wusenyha wako wukanghoza muno'ne likuwo.

27 “Alu nane nakondya kukugololela muwoko wangu nghuling'anize, ne ndigwa yako ye nhondo zose, niyicepula, kuli'gwe. Nagwe lu, nghondya kukutajila mu miwoko ya wono wakusulile i Wafilisiti, wono wakugawonela cevu i metumo gako go wusenyha.
28 Wukasenyha ne wanji wose, hamba kwiguta yaye, hanyuma wukabita kuwatyatya Waashuli, wukasenyha nawo, ninga nawo hamba si wakwigusya yaye.
29 Wukabita ku Wababiloni i wanhu wasuluza wawaha, wukasenyha nawo, ninga hamba kukwigusya ng'o.
30 Alu, azi zo yakulonga u Muwaha Mulungu yakutya, ‘Agwe Yelusalemi, wetuma zizi zose nho mucekulu musenyha mono yasina hamba cevu yaye.
31 Na mu zinzila zose zimbaha, agwe wuzenjile hanhu ha wuhembelo we vihwani, no kwituma zo wusenyha. Ninga nghani yo wizine, agwe si lunji wusina mapesa nha vyono wenha i wasenyha wanji, holeka wukwituma zizo, ng'o, wisimajiye ne wasenyha wanji.
32 Langa winha nho mucekulu mutolwa, mono kasenyhaga ne wajenzi, si nho kumwenda mulume wakwe du yali wene.
33 Kanyhi cimanyile nha vyono i wacekulu wasenyha, wo wakuwa kuhongwa ne walume, ninga agwe, yo gwegwe mono kuwahongaga i wendece wako wose we cilume, kuwa kuwakwejeleza ku wulawa hanhu hose, ho waze wagonane nagwe.
34 Na mbeka agwe, zinghani zako zisimajiye na ze wacekulu wasenyha wayago, agwe wuli wene du. Hambi nha hasina mulume mono kakutyatyaga ho yasenyhe nagwe, langa gwegwe yo kwitegulaga ku walume no kuwalipa, ninga agwe si wakulipaga cinji yaye. Holeka mbeka, wisimajiye muno.’ ”

Wuling'anizi wo Mulungu kuli Yelusalemi

35 Holeka 'gwe go Yelusalemi go malaya! Alu wuhulicize zono Muwaha Mulungu yakulonga yakutya,

36 Azi zo yakulonga Muwaha Mulungu, “Alu vyono kuyigubula myenda yako, kuwa kwikala cipwi so wusenyha wako, kuwa kwiyilavya go mweneco kuli wendece wako, na kuli vihwani vyako vyose vye cevu, na kanyhi kuwa kuwawulaga sunga ne wanago, kuwalavya makumbiko kuli vihwani.
37 Ku soko ya zizo zose, nane lu, nowakonghola hamonga i wendece wako wose, wono wuwendile, na wono wuwasulile. Nowaleta hali'gwe niwatandalice, wakupilime, hanyuma, nghugubule myenda yako, niwalece waciwone cipwi cako.
38 Nane nokuling'aniza so wusenyha wako, no wuwulaji wako. Nghuwulajile mu wulipicize wukali we likuwo lyangu.
39 Nghutajile mu miwoko yawo, nawo wahakumulanjile lukulu, i hanhu hako hose ha wuhembelo, hono kwitumamilaga so wusenyha wako, no kuhemba vihwani. Nawo wolokusolela myenda yako yose, hamonga ne vibwe vyo wuguzi wukamu, waze wakulece baho wuli cipwi.

40 “Wolokuletela mapuga ge wanhu, wakutowanje ne mabwe. Wolokutemasya nhugu nhugu ne mimaje yawo.
41 Wolozipembela zikaya zako, no kugaleka mapuga ge wacekulu, wawuwone wulipicizwe wako. Nane nolokupeza no wusenyha wako, hamonga no wuhonga i walume i wendece wako.
42 Nane baho likuwo lyangu ho lilamalicila, niwe kusenjela. Nane si nolokolwa kanyhi ne likuwo, nhondo no wivu wangu ho wokuwucila.
43 Ane nghuwona, 'mba, wasemwa zono nakutendela ha wudodo wako, holeka alu weza kwituma zinghani, zono zikunghoza'ne likuwo. Na yiyi yo yikaleka wutuje wulipicizwe ku soko ya zizo. Alu nhawule 'gwe, wukawa wukwihanza-hanza mu wusenyha wako, ne vinhu vye liwifyo?” Azi zo yakutya u Muwaha Mulungu.

Nha vyono yenha nyina yo munhu, nayo mwanagwe we cicekulu vyo kowa yenha.

44 Muwaha Mulungu yakatya, “I wanhu, wosuma kuyitumamila i mbazi yiyi kuli'gwe Yelusalemi, yono yikutya, ‘Nha vyono yenha nyina yo munhu, ale, nayo mwanagwe we cicekulu vyo kowa yenha.’
45 Agwe mbeka wuli mwana we cicekulu wo nyina yako, mono yamusula u mulume wakwe hamonga ne wanagwe. Wihwanile i wanduguzo we cicekulu, wono nawo wawasula i walume zawo hamonga ne wana wawo. Nagwe Yelusalemi hamonga ne makaya mayago, u nyina yenyu yali Muhiti, no sogo yenyu yali Mwamoli.

46 “U lumbulyo muwaha, yo Samaliya, ku lubavu lwa kusumuka, hamonga ne vitumbi vyayo. Nayo u lumbulyo mudodo, yo Sodoma, hamonga ne vitumbi vyayo, ku lubavu lwa kutakama.
47 Ninga wule, wazityatya zinzila zawo hamba wetuma mu nghango ye mawifyo gawo? Aa, ng'o! Agwe si wetuma nhavivyo, langa nagwe wukahodanjika hadodo du, kuciza wene vyono wali wenha muli zose zono wetuma.
48 Ninga mbeka, nha vyono ninha ane no Mulungu mumi, u Muwaha Mulungu, nghutya nhavi, ‘U lumbulyo Sodoma hamonga ne vitumbi vyakwe vyose, si wetuma zimbi zono zinha zizi, zono agwe Yelusalemi wetuma, hamonga ne vitumbi vyako.
49 Ninga hamba nhavivyo, nayo Sodoma, hamonga ne wanagwe we cicekulu, wakedunhumula muno ku soko ye zindigwa nyinji zono wali wena, hamonga no wikale wawo wo mulamu no wucehe. Ninga si wawalanjilila i waciwa na wono wali wena wanjile.
50 Langa wene, wakawa wali mu widunhumuzi du, hamonga no wuwula cihuliko, no kujendelela kwituma zono 'ne nizisulile. Na ku soko yiyo, holeka nane nghawongomeza, nha vyono wumanyile nagwe.’

51 “Hamba Samaliya, si yetuma wubi wono wuficile nusu yo wubi wono agwe wetuma. Agwe Yelusalemi, wetuma makani ge mawifyo muno muno, si nha vyono Samaliya yetuma ku wulawa katali. Ne mahodango gako, gakawanoza wanduguzo we cicekulu, wejele wali ling'ani so mawipizo gako gono wetuma.
52 Ninga nalu, yikunojeye wucikangalile cevu cako. Hambi u wubi wako nha we wifyo muno, wucizize we wanduguzo we cicekulu, wono luno wakwijela hali'gwe wasina masutya. Holeka aluno, yikunojeye wucidinghule cevu cako, hambi nha yo gwegwe wuwanozize wanduguzo we cicekulu wejele wali ling'ani.”

I wanhu wa Sodoma, ne wanhu wa Samaliya wologomolwa

53 Muwaha Mulungu yakayilonjela Yelusalemi yakatya, “Ane nowapela kanyhi wumoteswe we yisi ya Samaliya, hamonga ne vitumbi vyayo vyose, nayo Sodoma, hamonga ne vitumbi vyayo vyose. Heya, sunga na gwegwe nagwe nokumotya nhavivyo.
54 Ninga, kolokolwa soni, ne cevu cako colowalajila i wanduguzo we cicekulu, nha vyono wene waswanu wasina masutya.
55 Nawo kanyhi wolomoteswa, ninga nagwe Yelusalemi, hamonga ne vitumbi vyako vyose vyologomolwa.
56 Ninga wule, agwe Yelusalemi, si wayisekaga Sodoma mu vilo vivilya, lono wali wukali mu wiwoni wako,
57 na lono zimbi zono wetuma, zali zikali sinamanyika yaye? Ninga aluno wose mwehwana. Naluno i Waedomu, ne Wafilisiti, hamonga ne wekalani wayago, wono mwikalane behi, wono wakusulile, nawo wokwanza kukusekanga nagwe.
58 Holeka yowa yikunojeye wugadinghule gwegwe i mabi gose go wusenyha wako, hamonga ne mawifyo gako gono wetuma.” Vyo yakutya u Muwaha Mulungu.

I cilagane cono cokwikala cibitilila

59 U Muwaha Mulungu yakulonga yakutya nhavi, “Ane nomutendelenyi nha vyono munojeye. Hambi nha mwabedegala wihuwizo wenyu, no kucibena cilagane.
60 Ninga ane nocikumbucila cilagane cono nacinoza nanye, lono mwali mukali wadodo. Na kanyhi nonoza cilagane nanye, cono cokwikala cibitilila.
61 Nanye mokumbucila ze metumo genyu gono mwetumaga lulo, muwe kukolwa cevu, kolo mwawagomola i wandugu zenyu we cicekulu, i wawaha ne wadodo. Ninga nane, nowanoza wawe wana wenyu we cicekulu, hamba yiwe zizi si zali zili ku lubavu lwe cilagane cangu, cono nghucinoza nanye.
62 Nocinoza wupya cilagane cangu kuli'nye. Nanye baho, ho momanyila vyono ane yo nene Muwaha Mulungu.
63 Nane baho, ho nolomunenelenyi i masutya genyu gose gono mwetuma. Ninga anye molowa mugakumbuciye no kukolwa cevu, hamba no kulonga cinji kanyhi.” Vyo yakutya u Muwaha Mulungu.


Ezekiyeli 17

Fumbo ye zi'ndi no muzabibu

1 Muwaha Mulungu yakanonjela kanyhi yakatya,
2 “Gwe go munhu! Wuwalonjelaje Waisilayeli i fumbo,
3 ho wamanye vyono ane yo nene Muwaha Mulungu, mono nghuwalonjela vyono, baho katali, kwali na 'ndi mbaha, yono yali yina mazoya maswanu ku wulangwe, hamonga ne mabawa mawaha muno, gono gatandalaga hanhu hawaha.

Ale, 'ndi yiyo, yaguluka yikabita sunga mu Matunda ga Lebanoni, na kuko,

yikapanyula cisonje co mubici wo muhagata,

4 yikacisola cisonje cico, yikavidumula vinhambi vyayo,

vyono vyali kucanyha yayo yikavitegula sunga ko ku yisi ye wasuluza,

yikaviwika mwi'kaya lye wakwawi.

5 Hanyuma kanyhi,

yikayisola mbeyu ya mu mbeyu za mu yisi ya Isilayeli,

yakayihadika i hanhu hono

ho wuli muhangha wono wina moso mwinji,

i hanhu hono hali ho gali ne malenga menji

nhondo zose go kuyimwajilila ho yisume kuwiluka.

6 Nayo mbeyu yiyo yikacindika,

mubici wukawiluka viswanu,

wukawa muzabibu wono wutulile viswanu, kanyhi wutindigaye.”

Ne manhambi gayo gakapasicila, gakawiluka,

gakayipitucila 'ndi, ne milela yayo yikehimbila muno hasi.

No muzabibu wukawa kupasicila manhambi menji,

wukatula ne mahamha menji.

7 “Hakawa na 'ndi yinji kanyhi,

yono nayo yali yina mabawa mawaha muno,

ne mazoya menji muno.

Alu, u muzabibu wukayilanzya milela yayo kuli 'ndi yiyo,

hamonga ne mahamha gayo,

wukawa kwihwesa nha vyono,

wotuga malenga menji kuciza malenga gono gali

mu busitani mono wali muhadikwe.

8 Ninga hamba nhavivyo,

u muzabibu nha wali wakondya kuhadikwa

mu muhangha wono wina moso mwinji,

hono ho gali ne malenga menji,

ho wusume kuwiluka no kulela zabibu,

no kuwa muzabibu muswanu.

9 “Holeka nane no Muwaha Mulungu nghwiwuza nghutya,

alu mbeka u muzabibu wuwu,

wokwikala wuwiluce hongo?

Alo'ndi nongozi, si yoyikula milela yayo,

no kuyidumula mikalo yayo,

no kugabenanjiza manhambi gayo ho wunyale?

Hanyuma wuwe kuwula vilungo,

mu nghango yono si wosaka kanyhi

wanhu wenji wawukule milela yayo.

10 Heya, mbeka, muzabibu wakondya kuhadikwa,

ninga wule, wojendelela kuwiluka hongo?

Hamba si wosuma kunyazwa ne mbeho ya kucilima kolo yeza kuwuputa?

Hamba lunji kunyalila baho hono ho wuli,

hono wukuwilucila?”

Nhunizo ye fumbo yiyo

11 U Muwaha Mulungu yakanonjela kanyhi yakatya,
12 “Ale, ta, wawuzaje wawo i wabedegazi, hono wawe wayimanyile nhunizo ye fumbo yiyi. Wuwalonjele nha vyono, u mutemi wa Babiloni yakaza ku Yelusalemi, yakamusola mutemi, hamonga ne wetumami wakwe wose, yakawategula sunga ko ku yisi yakwe ye Babiloni.
13 Yakamusola na wo lulelo lwe citemi, yakawika cilagane nayo, yakamwilahiza yamuhulice mwene. Yakawasola ne wanhu wa mu yisi wono wena vilungo,
14 ho yalilece likungugo lisina vilungo. I cilagane cakwe ho cisume kukamiswa.
15 Ninga hamba nhavivyo, u mutemi we yisi ya Yuda, yakafyula, yakatuma wetumami ku Misili, ho wamupele fwalasi hamonga ne lipuga yiwaha lye wasilikale. Ninga wule, alu mbeka Yuda kosuma kupona? Aa ng'o! Si kosuma kubena cilagane, kanyhi ho yalece kulipicizwa.”

16 Muwaha Mulungu yakutya, “Ninga mbeka, nha vyono ne nili Mulungu mumi, u mutemi yuyo kolofwila ku Babiloni, vyono yakondya, kuwubena wilahize wakwe, hamonga ne cilagane cakwe, vyono yavinoza no mutemi we yisi ya Babiloni, mono yo yamuwika mwi'goda lyo wutemi.
17 Hamba nhavivyo, i vilungo vye lipuga lye wasilikale wa Falawo u mutemi we Misili, si vyosuma kumutaza cinji mu wulugu, hambi u mutemi we yisi ya Babiloni, nha kozenga magagala, hamonga ne magomawakali, ho yawawulaje wanhu wenji muno.
18 Vyono mwene mutemi wa Yuda kabena cilagane hamonga no wilahize wakwe, na ku soko yo wituma zizi zose, holeka nayo hamba si kopona yaye.”

19 Muwaha Mulungu yakutya nhavi, “Nha vyono 'ne, nili Mulungu mumi, nomulipiciza mutemi wa Yuda, ku soko yo wubena cilagane, hamonga no wilahize wakwe, wono yelahiziza mwi'tagwa lyangu, nha vyono kowukamisa.
20 Nolutandaza lwavu lo mupelembi ho nimwibatile mumo mu lwavu lulo. Nimutegule kuko ku Babiloni, nghamulipicizize kuko. Hambi nha yawa si mwihuwilwa kuli'ne.
21 Ne wasilikale wakwe wose i waswanu, wose wowulajilwa mu wulugu. Na wono wosigala, wose wokwiyagalila mbavu zose ze yisi. Nanye baho nhondo, ho molomanyila vyono ane yo nene Muwaha Mulungu mono nizilonjile zizi.”

Witumbilo wono Mulungu yakuwihuwiza

22 Azi zo yakuzilonga u Muwaha Mulungu yakutya,

“Nocisola cisonje co mubici wo muhagata.

Nicibene i cinhambi cayo cidece.

Nghacihadice mucanyha ye litunda litali muno.

23 Mwi'tunda litali muno,

lya mu yisi ya Isilayeli.

Naco copasicila manhambi,

no kulela mbeyu,

no kuwa mubici muhagata muswanu muno.

Ne zindeje ze mawala gose zilekala mumo,

zolowona wuceho mu mubeho wayo.

24 Ne mibici yose ya mu yisi,

yolomanya vyono,

ane yo nene Muwaha Mulungu,

mono niyidumulangaga mibici mitali,

no kuyinoza mibici midodo yiwiluce yiwe mitali.

Niyinyazaga mibici yono yidedewaye.

No kuyinoza mibici mikavu, yidedewale.

Ane yo nene u Muwaha Mulungu,

mono nizilonjile zizi,

nane nozifisiliza zizo zose,

nozituma nha vyono nonjile.”


Ezekiyeli 18

U Munhu na lwakwe

1 Muwaha Mulungu yakanonjela kanyhi
2 yakatya, “Alu mbazi yiyi yina nhunizoci, yono yikalaga yikulongwa mu yisi ya Isilayeli, yono yikutya,

‘Wababa, walya zabibu zindece,

ninga i wana, meno gawo gakafwa yitenene.’ ”

3 Muwaha Mulungu yakutya nhavi, “Na mbeka vyono ane yo nene Mulungu mumi, holeka si mowuyajilila lukulu kanyhi kuyilonga mbazi yiyi.
4 Hambi u wumi we wanhu wose, nha wangu nene. U wumi wo mulezi, sunga no wumi wo mwana. Holeka u munhu mono kokwituma wubi, yuyo yo kofwa.

5 “Na lunji yisolaga, hawa ho yuli munhu muswanu kanyhi yali ling'ani, na kanyhi wanhu wono si wamumanyile Mulungu.
6 Mono si kahembaga vihwani vye Waisilayeli, hamba kulya vye makumbiko malavye mu hanhu hono halemezwe, mu wuhembelo we wanhu wono si wamumanyile Mulungu. Kanyhi yawe yakali namulonza mucekulu wa munhu yunji, hamba kugona no mucekulu wa munhu yunji, lono yali mu vilo vywakwe vyo mwezi.
7 Hamba si yamukonya munhu, langa yamulipa viswanu mono yamudayaga, hamba si yamuboka yunji sawo yakwe kwe vilungo. Kanyhi yawe yakuwalisa wono wadabuce, no kuwapela vivwalo wono wasakaze.
8 Kanyhi yawe munhu mono kakopeshaga, ninga si kadayaga ngomola. Mono si yendile kwituma zimbi, langa kanozaga wuling'anizi we ling'ani muli munhu no muyagwe.
9 No munhu we liwala lilyo, kagebataga malajilizo gangu, no kuwukamisa wulajilize wangu. U munhu yuyo yakuwa yali ling'ani, nayo holeka kowumipa.” Vyo yakutya u Muwaha Mulungu.

10 “Yisolaga na lunji, u munhu yuyu, kawa yena mwana. No mwana yuyo kawa muhizi, kanyhi muwulaji, mono yakwituma lunji likani limonga lya muli gago,
11 gono u sogwe si kagetumaga yaye. U mwana yuyo, yawe ya kulya ndigwa ze makumbiko malavye mu hanhu hono halemezwe, mu wuhembelo we wanhu wono si wamumanyile Mulungu, na kanyhi akuno ho yakumulonza no mucekulu wo muyagwe.
12 Kanyhi yendile kuwakonya i waciwa, akuno muhizi, no kuwaboka wanhu sawo yawo kwe vilungo, hamba kugomola cono munhu yunji yacipinjice kuli mwene. Kanyhi, yawe yendile kubita ku wuhembelo we wanhu wono si wamumanyile Mulungu, no kuhemba vihwani vye mawifyo.
13 Kanyhi yawe munhu mono kakopeshaga hanyuma kadaya ne ngomola. Mukwihwesa u munhu we liwala lili, kowumipa hongo? Nghukulonjela mbeka nha vyono si kowumipa yaye. Hambi nha kakondya kwituma zinghani zizi ze liwifyo, holeka kofwa du. Nayo kowa yeyambuza u wumi wakwe mweneco.

14 “Ninga kanyhi lunji citye, yisolaga u munhu yuyu we kejete, nayo kawa yena mwana. Nayo u mwana yuyu, kaziwonaga i zimbi zose zono u sogwe kazitumaga, ninga mwene mwana ho yalece kwiga musambo wuwo mubi wo sogwe.
15 Mwene mwana yuyo, yawe si yakuhemba vihwani vyono wakuvihemba Waisilayeli, hamba kulya zindigwa ze makumbiko, i malavye kuli wuhembelo we wanhu wono si wamumanyile Mulungu. Kanyhi yawe mono si kalonzaga mucekulu wa yunji,
16 hamba kumwiwonela yunji, hamba kumuhiziza munhu yunji yaye. Kanyhi mwene yawe mono kawagomolelaga wanhu vinhu vyawo vyono wavipinjice kuli mwene. Kawalisaga wono wali nzala, no kuwapela vivwalo wono wasawuce.
17 Kanyhi mwene yawe si yendile kwituma zimbi, hamba kuwakopesha wanhu no kusaka ngomola. Na kanyhi mwene yawe yawibate wulajilize wangu, no kugakamisa malajilizo gangu. Ale, u munhu we liwala lilyo, si kofwa yaye, ku soko yo wubi wo sogwe, langa mwene kowumipa du.
18 Na ku lubavu lunji, u sogwe mweneco yo yawakonyaga wanhu no kuwahiziza, no kwituma nhondo zose i zimbi kuli wayagwe. Holeka yuyu sogwe yo, kofwa so wubi wakwe wono mweneco yali kuwituma.”

19 Na lunji kokwiwuza go mweneco wuwe kutya, “Alu, nhawule u mwana yuyo nayo, yalece kuwusola u wubi wo sogwe? Ninga lipitulo lyayo lyoli, yuyo u mwana, nha yetuma ziswanu na ze ling'ani. Yebata wulajilize wangu, no kuwutyatya viswanu, holeka mwene yimunojeye yawumipe.
20 Langa mono yakwituma wubi, ale, yuyo du yo kofwa. Umwana, si kofwa so wubi wo sogwe, hamba sogo yo munhu nayo, si kofwa yaye ku soko yo wubi wo mwanagwe. U munhu muswanu kohumbilizwa so wituma ziswanu wakwe. No munhu mwituma zimbi, nayo kofwa so wubi wono ketumaga.

21 “Na hono u munhu mubi yapituce, yalece kwituma zo wubi, yebate wulajilize wangu, no kwituma ziswanu na ze ling'ani, ayo si kofwa yaye, langa kowumipa.
22 No wubi wakwe wose wonenelwa, nayo kowa mumi, hambi nha ketuma ze ling'ani.”
23 Muwaha Mulungu yakutya nhavi, “Wulenyi! Mukwihwesa vyono'ne niwusangalalilaga 'mbi u wufwe wo munhu mubi? Aa, ng'o! Ane nyendile munhu yuyo yanene wubi wakwe, yekale mumi.
24 Ninga langa, hono munhu we ling'ani yalece kwituma ziswanu, ho yanze kwituma zinghani zimbi, ze cevu, zono i wanhu wabi wakuzitumama, wule u munhu yuyo kowumipa 'mbi? Aa, ng'o! Ne ziswanu zose zono yetumaga, si zokumbucilwa yaye. Nayo kofwa du ku soko yo wubi wakwe no wu wula wihuwilwa wakwe.”

25 Ninga na lunji kotya nhavi, “Alu mwene zizi zono Muwaha Mulungu yakwituma, wule nghani zizi swanu? Alu muhulicize nimulonjelenyi anye nyo Waisilayeli. Alu anye mukwihwesa nha vyono zinzila zangu si zili ling'ani hongo? Ninga mumanye nha vyono zinzila zenyu zo si zili ling'ani yaye.
26 Kanyhi mumanye nha vyono, u munhu we ling'ani, hono yalece kwituma ziswanu, yanze kwituma zimbi, na hanyuma ho yafwe, kofwila mu wubi wakwe wono yali kwituma.
27 Ninga hono u munhu mubi yapituce, no kuwuleka wubi wakwe wono yali kwituma, ho yetume zo mugoloka na ze ling'ani, yuyo kowumipa.
28 Hambi nha yakumanya zo wubi wono yali kwituma, no kuleka kuwituma. Holeka u munhu yuyo si kofwa yaye, langa kowumipa.
29 Ninga anye nyo Waisilayeli mutezaga vyono, ‘Muwaha Mulungu ketumaga zo si zili ling'ani.’ Mwihwesaga nha vyono zinzila zangu si zili ling'ani? Ninga mumanye nha vyono zinzila zenyu nyenye zono si zili ling'ani yaye.

30 “Holeka ane no Muwaha Mulungu, nghumulonjelenyi anye nyo Waisilayeli, nha vyono, nomuling'anizenyi munhu na lwakwe, mu nghango ye metumo gakwe gono yetuma. Alu muwuye, mumhitucile, muwulece u wubi wono mwitumaga, mulece kuwuleceleza u wubi wenyu wumongomezenyi.
