31 Muwutajilaje lukulu u wubi wenyu wose wono mwetumaga, mwinozeze nhumbula mhya, no muhe mupya. Alu anye nyo Waisilayeli, nhawulenyi mukwiyambalila ne lifwa?
32 Ninga ane, si nyendile yunji wo wose yafwe. Holeka mupituce muwulece u wubi wenyu, mwikale womi.” Vyo yakutya u Muwaha Mulungu.


Ezekiyeli 19

U lwimbo lo wukalale

1 U Muwaha Mulungu, yakanonjela yakatya nimbe lwimbo lulu lwe cililo, niwembile i wawaha wa Isilayeli.

2 U nyina yenyu simba yo lukumyo mu zisimba zinji.

Yazilisaga zi nyanagwe haciligati ye zisimba zinume.

3 Yikayilisa nyanagwe monga,

yikayijiza no wupelemba, yikalya wanhu.

4 Nago makungugo,

gakazihulika zizo zinghani zayo,

wakayigwiza mwi'kombo.

Wakayibata ne zindobogo, wakayikwega,

wakayitegula sunga ku yisi ya Misili.

5 Nayo nyina yayo, yaza wona yayilindila sunga

no witumbilo wakwe wajilila.

Yikasola mwana yunji kanyhi wa muli zi nyanagwe,

yikayinoza yikawa simba nghali muno.

6 Nayo yali yeza kuwa yawiluka,

yikawa kujenda ako na ako muciligati ye zisimba.

Nayo yikeyijiza kupelemba, yikalya wanhu.

7 Yikagakumulanga i magomawakali gawo,

yikaganoza makaya gawo gakawa matongo.

Sunga ne wanhu wa mu'si, wakamema no woga,

mu mapango gose lono yikukoloma.

8 Hobaho we makungugo,

wakekongholela hamonga wakulawa mu zi'si zose,

ho wayihome.

Wakalutandaza u lwavu lwawo lo wupelembi,

wakayibata mu mutego wawo.

9 Wakayitajila mwi'tundu wakayitegula

ko mutemi we yisi ya Babiloni.

Wakayiwika hasi yo wulindilizi we vilungo,

i ngholoma yayo, ho yilece kuhulikwa kanyhi

mu matunda ga mu yisi ya Isilayeli.

10 Kanyhi u nyina yako,

yali yehwanile no mubici wo muzabibu,

wono muhadikwe behi ne malenga.

Na vyono hali hogali malenga menji,

holeka muzabibu wali wina mahamha menji,

kanyhi wukalela mikalo minji muno.

11 Ne manhambi gayo gali gali vilungo,

gakawiluka, gakawa nghome ze citemi.

Nawo mubici wo muzabibu,

wukatalimha wukawa mutali kuciza i mibici yinji.

Ne wanhu wose wakawuwona wutali wayo,

na vyono wali wudedewaye muno.

12 Ninga so likuwo,

wukakulwa hamonga ne milela yayo,

wukagwiswa hasi.

Ne mbeho ya kucilima,

yikayinyaza mikalo yayo.

Ne manhambi gayo,

gakabenanjika gose, gakanyala,

gakapembelwa mu moto.

13 Nalu, wuhadikwa mu mbago,

mu yisi nghavu yono yisina malenga.

14 Ne lisina lyo muzabibu lyapya no moto.

No moto wuwo wagatakamiza manhambi gayo,

hamonga ne mikalo yayo.

Holeka i manhambi gago,

si golowa na vilungo kanyhi,

hamba si golowa nghome kanyhi ze citemi.

Alu lo lwimbo lwa cililo

lono lukuwa kwimbwa kenji na kenji.


Ezekiyeli 20

U wuhile wo Muwaha Mulungu, no wufyule wo munhu

1 Na mwi'zuwa lye likumi, lya mu mwezi we'hano, wa mu mwaka wo mupunghati wa mu wuhamywe wetu. I wanyamhala wanji we Waisilayeli, wakanyiziza, wakawa kwikala ha mwande yangu, ho wambuze za kuli Muwaha Mulungu.
2 Nayo Muwaha Mulungu yakanonjela yakatya,
3 “Gwe go munhu, wuwalonjele wanhu wawa, wuwamanyize nha vyono Muwaha Mulungu yakutya. Anye muza mumbuze, ho mumanye zo wuhile wangu, si yo nhavivi hongo? Ninga nane mbeka, no Mulungu mumi, si nghusacila lukulu anye mumbuze nghani yo yose.” Ane yo nene u Muwaha Mulungu vyo nghutya.

4 “Wule, nagwe go munhu, kosuma kuwaling'aniza? Ciba heya wuwaling'anize. Wuwakumbucizaje ze mawifyo gono walezi wawo wetuma.
5 Wuwalonjele zono nghulonga. Vyono lono namuhagulila Isilayeli, nghawehuwiziza, kanyhi nghelajila kuli wene ku yisi ya Misili, nghawalonjela vyono, ane yo nene u Muwaha Mulungu wenyu.
6 Na mwi'daha lilyo, lyo nehuwiziza nha vyono nowawusa ku wulawa mu yisi ya Misili, niwategule ku yisi yono nawahagulila, yisi ndezi yono yina moso mwinji muno, yisi swanu kuciza zi'si zose.
7 Ninga nghawalonjela nha vyono, yiwanojeye waviwusize lukulu vihwani vyose muli wene, vyono wali wavyenda, ho walece kwiyavufya ne milungu yo wulambi ya mu yisi ya Misili. Hambi ane nha yo nene u Muwaha Mulungu, u Mulungu wenyu.
8 Ninga wene wakebavya, hamba si wenda no kuhuliciza yaye. Hamba si waviwusa vihwani vye milungu yawo ye mawifyo, hamba kuyileka i milungu ye Wamisili yaye. Sunga nhondo, nasakaga niwalajile likuwo lyangu liwaha, kuko ku Misili.
9 Ninga kanyhi hodu nghawa kwiyimila. Ninga zizo, netuma ku soko ye litagwa lyangu, ho lilece kwavufywa ha meso ga we makungugo wono wali wekaye hamonga nawo, wono ha meso gawo, nemanyiza kuli Isilayeli nha vyono nowawusa mu yisi ya Misili.

10 “Holeka nawawusa mu yisi ya Misili, nghawaleta mu nyika.
11 Nghawapela malajilizo gangu, no kuwalajila wuling'anizi wangu, wono munhu wo wose kosuma kuwumipa, hono yawitumame.
12 Kanyhi nghawawicila no wibate we mazuwa ge Sabato, ciwe citanjilo hagati yetu ane na wene. Ho wamanye vyono, ane yo nene mono nghuweza.
13 Ninga hamba nhavivyo, wene wakewubavya mu nyika, wakeyavufya. Wakawubena wulajilize wangu, wakagalema malajilizo gangu, gono gamupelaga wumi munhu wo wose mono yakugebata. Wakazavufya ne zisabato zangu. Sunga nhondo nasakaga niwalajile likuwo lyangu vyono linha, niwongomezeze mumo mu nyika.
14 Ninga kanyhi, hodu ngheyimila. Nane nanoza nhavivyo ku soko ye litagwa lyangu, ho lilece kwavufywa ha meso ga we makungugo, wono wambona vyono nghuwawusa i wanhu wa Isilayeli ku Misili.
15 Hamba nhavivyo nane mumo mu nyika, nghehuwiza nha vyono, du, nane si nowategula kanyhi ku yisi yono nali nawapela, yisi ndezi, yono yina moso mwinji, kanyhi yisi swanu muno kuciza zi'si zose.
16 Nehuwiza nhavivyo, vyono wene wagalema malajilizo gangu, wakawubena no wulajilize wangu, wakazavufya ne zisabato zangu, wakenda kuvihemba vihwani vyawo du.

17 “Ninga hanyuma kanyhi, nghawa kuwawonela lusungu. Nghawa kudumula nghani, nha vyono ndece kuwawulajila mu nyika.

18 “Ninga hamba nhavivyo, si naleka, langa nawasugula i wana wawo mu nyika nghawatya, Mulece kujendela mu malajilizo ge walezi wenyu, hamba kuyityatya myoto yawo, hamba kwiyavufya nyo weneco so wuhemba vihwani vyawo.
19 Ane yo nene Muwaha Mulungu, u Mulungu wenyu. Holeka muwibataje wulajilize wangu, hamonga ne malajilizo gangu.
20 Muyezaje Sabato yangu, ho ciwe co citanjilo ce cilagane cetu cono canoza, kanyhi ho ciwe co cikumbucizo kuli'nye, nha vyono ane, yo nene u Muwaha Mulungu, u Mulungu wenyu.

21 “Ninga hamba nhavivyo, ne likungugo lilyo nalyo, wakalema kuhulika 'ne. Wakawubena wulajilize wangu, hamba si wagebata ne malajilizo gangu yaye, gono gamupelaga wumi u munhu wo wose mono yakugebata. Kanyhi wakazavufya Sabato zangu. Nane ku soko yiyo, nasakaga niwalajile likuwo lyangu vyono linha, niwawulajile mumo mu nyika, niwongomeze wose.
22 Ninga kanyhi hodu ngheyimila. Nanoza nhavivyo, ku soko ye litagwa lyangu, ho lilece kwavufywa ha meso ga we makungugo, wono wawona vyono nghuwawusa i wanhu wa Isilayeli mu yisi ya Misili.
23 Ku soko yiyo, nghehuwiza kanyhi mumo mu nyika. Nghelahiza nha vyono, noweyagaziza mu yinze yose.
24 Nane netuma zizi, vyono wene wagalema malajilizo gangu, wakawubena no wulajilize wangu, wazavufya sunga ne zisabato zangu. Wakahemba vihwani vivilya vyono i walezi wawo wavihembaga.

25 “Hanyuma nane nghawa kuwapela wulajilize wono si wuswanu, hamonga ne malajilizo gono gasina wumi.
26 Hodu nghawa kuwaleka weyavufye weneco so vilavywa vyawo. Kanyhi nghawaleka wawalavye makumbiko, i wana wawo we cilume walongozi kulelwa. Na yiyi yali yo nzila yo kuwalipiciziza, no kuwalajila nha vyono ane yo nene Muwaha Mulungu.

27 “Nalu agwe go munhu, wuwalonjele i Waisilayeli zono ane nene Muwaha Mulungu nghuwalonjela. Na yiyi yo nzila yinji nayo, yono i walezi wawo wanoza, wakanhovula so wuleka kuwa no wihuwilwa wawo.
28 Ninga nane, lono nawenjiza mu yisi yono nali nehuwiza nha vyono nowapela. Nawo wali weza kugawona matunda matali, hamonga ne mibici yono yidedewaye, ati hodu, wakawa kuvilavizya makumbiko. Nane nhondo, ho wakanghoza likuwo muno, so makumbiko gawo manyanywe, hamonga ne divayi yono wayilavya.
29 Ninga nane nawawuza nghatya, Alu nhunizo yayo nyhici, i hanhu baho ha wukumbicilo we vihwani, hono mukuhabitila? Na ku wulawa lulo hanhu baho hakajendelela kucemwa 'Hanhu ha wukumbicilo we milungu yawo' sunga nalu.
30 Holeka luno, wuwalonjele Waisilayeli zono nghulonga ane nene Muwaha Mulungu, nghutya, alu nhawulenyi nanye mukwituma u wubi wuwulya du, wono sunga ne walezi wenyu wetumaga, no kuvihemba vihwani vyawo?
31 Sunga ne zuwali baha du, mwali kulavya vilavywa vivilya du, no kwiyavufya nyenye nyo weneco, so vihwani, mu nzila yo kuvilavizya i wana wenyu makumbiko, mu moto. Ninga hamba nhavivyo, 'nye nyo Waisilayeli, vyo mukali mukunyiziza du, ati ho mumanye zo wuhile wangu. Ninga mbeka nha vyono ane, yo nene Muwaha Mulungu, u Mulungu mumi, nane si nomulecenyi yaye, ho mumbuze cinji co cose.
32 Hambi nha mwadumula nghani, nha vyono anye mukusaka muwe nhe we makungugo ganji, cinye wanhu wono wekalaga mu ma'si ganji, muwe kuhemba mibici ne mabwe. Ninga mumanye nha vyono zizo, hamba si zolokwijelela lukulu yaye.”

U Mulungu kalipicizaga kanyhi kanenelaga

33 “Na mbeka ane nha vyono yo nene Muwaha Mulungu, mono nili Mulungu mumi, nghumusugulenyi nha vyono, nomutendelenyi anye mwi'kuwo lyangu, kwe vilungo vyo muwoko wangu, na kwe vilungo vyangu vyose.
34 Nane nomulajilenyi i vilungo vyangu vyono vinha, hamonga ne likuwo lyangu, kolo nghumukongholezenyi hamonga no kumugomolenyi ku wulawa mu zi'si zose kono mowa mwiyagazizwe.
35 Nane nomwinjizenyi mu ‘Nyika ya we makungugo.’ Na kuko ko colonganila nanye cidomile.
36 Na kuko ko nomuling'anizizenyi, nha vyono nawaling'aniza i walezi wenyu mu nyika ya Sinayi.” Vyo yakutya u Muwaha Mulungu.

37 “Nane nomudimenyi, no kumunozenyi mwibate cilagane cangu.
38 Nolowawusiza lukulu muli nyenye, i wabedegazi wose kanyhi i wetuma wubi. Nolowawusiza lukulu mu zi'si, mono wekaye luno, ninga si wolowuyila lukulu kanyhi mu yisi ya Isilayeli. Nanye baho nhondo ho molomanyila vyono ane yo nene Muwaha Mulungu.”

39 Alu Muwaha Mulungu yakutya, “Nye nyo Waisilayeli, alu munoze nha vyono nyenye weneco mukuwona vyowa viswanu. Mosuma kubita mukavitumamile vihwani vyenyu hono mwende. Ninga nghumusugulenyi nha vyono, zizo zilaza kuwa zapuluta, baho langa ho molowa munojeye muhulice 'ne, no kuleka kulyavufya litagwa lyangu lyono lyelile, so wulavizya vilavywa i vihwani vyenyu.
40 Nanye nyo wanhu wangu, nyo wanhu wa Isilayeli, wose molohemba nene du, mu yisi ya Isilayeli, mwi'tunda lyangu lyono lyelile, litunda litali muno lya mu yisi ya Isilayeli. Nane baho ho nolokwendezwa nanye, na kuko ko nologasaka ne makumbiko genyu, ne vilavywa viswanu, hamonga ne vipegwa vyenyu vyono vyelile.
41 Nane kolo nakondya kumuwusenyi mu zi'si zizo zono mowa muyagazizwe, no kumukongholelenyi wose hamonga, nhondo ho nolovibocela ne vilavywa vyenyu vinyanywe. Ne makungugo baho ho wolowonela nha vyono ane nelile.
42 Nane kolo nakondya kumugomolenyi ku yisi ya Isilayeli, yisi yono nehuwiza, nha vyono nowapela i walezi wenyu, nanye baho nhondo ho mulamanyila nha vyono ane yo nene Muwaha Mulungu.
43 Nanye baho nhondo, ho mologakumbucilila i makani genyu ge mawifyo, gono mwagetuma, na vyono mweyavufya nyenye nyo weneco. Na baho ho molokwisemuna nyenye nyo weneco, so makani mabi gose gono mwagetuma.
44 Nane kolo neza kwituma nhavivyo ku soko ye litagwa lyangu, baho nanye nyo Isilayeli, ho molomanya vyono ane yo nene Muwaha Mulungu. Nane si nghumutendelenyi zizo ku soko ye zinzila zenyu zimbi, hamba so metumo genyu mabi langa ku soko ye lilumbo lye litagwa lyangu.” Nhavivi vyo yakutya Muwaha Mulungu.

Moto wo lubavu lwa kutakama

45 U Muwaha Mulungu yakanonjela kanyhi yakatya,
46 “Gwe go munhu, wupituce ta, wulanje ku lubavu lwa kutakama. Wulonje ne yisi yiyo yo lubavu lulo lwa kutakama, wukawawilile i wanhu wono wekaye mu mbago ya Negebu.
47 Wuwalonjele wahulicize zono Muwaha Mulungu yakuwalonjela, yakutya. Lanje ta! Alu neza kwasa moto, no moto wuwo, wogaticimiza mabici gose gono gali muli'gwe, i mabici madece, sunga na gono ganyalile. Hamba hasina cinhu co cose cono cosuma kuwuzimya moto wuwo. No moto wuwo wotandala ku wulawilila ku lubavu lwa kutakama, sunga ko ku lubavu lwa kusumuka, ne wanhu wose, wogawona malambi go moto wuwo.
48 Nawo ho wowona no kumanya nha vyono yo nene Muwaha Mulungu yo niwupembeye moto wuwo, kanyhi na vyono hasina munhu wo wose mono kosuma kuwuzimya yaye.

49 “Ninga nane nghapitula nghatya, 'Gwe Muwaha Mulungu, ane hamba si nosuma kuzinoza zizo. Holeka wulece kunhuma nene nizinoze! Hambi wose nha wanhezaga vyono ane nongaga nhondo zose nghani za fumbo.”


Ezekiyeli 21

U mumaje wo Muwaha Mulungu

1 Muwaha Mulungu yakanonjela kanyhi yakatya,
2 “Gwe go munhu, wuyitacilaje Yelusalemi. Wuhatacilaje i hanhu hose hono wanhu wahembelaga. Wuyisugule yisi ya Isilayeli.
3 Hambi ane no Muwaha Mulungu, nha neza nili mulugu wenyu. Nowusola mumaje wangu, nimuwulajenyi nyenye wose, i waswanu ne wabi, wose hamonga.
4 Nowitumamila mumaje wangu niwawulaje wanhu wose, ku wulawilila ku lubavu lwa kusumuka, sunga ko ku lubavu lwa kutakama.
5 Ne wanhu wose womanya vyono yo nene Muwaha Mulungu, yo ninuye mumaje wangu, nane hamba si nowulecela lukulu.

6 “Gwe go munhu, alu weze kugula nha munhu mono nhumbula yakwe yitamile, yikusaka kubahuka so wusungu. Wugule ko wukalale, wukagulile i hanhu hazelu, hono wanhu wose wowa kukuwona.
7 Nawo kolo wakukuwuza wakutya, nhawule lu wukugula, nagwe wuwalonjele nha vyono, wukugula ku soko ye zinghani zono zikuza. Na kolo zinghani zizo zikuza, i zinhumbula zawo zolomema woga, ne miwoko yawo yolonyenyanjika, no wusugu wawo wose wolomalika, ne mafugamilo gawo golocicima. Ne lipango lilyo lyaza, lyakondya kufika.” Vyo yakutya Muwaha Mulungu.

8 Muwaha Mulungu yakanonjela kanyhi yakatya,
9 “Gwe go munhu, alu wuwile. Wuwalonjele wanhu zono ane no Muwaha Mulungu nghutya, Wuwatye nhavi; Mumaje wakondya kunolwa, kanyhi wakwemwa wakondya.

10 Wanolwa ho witume mulimo wo kuwulaga,

wakwemwa ho wumulice nhe muli.

Holowa hasina hamba nyemo,

hambi i wanhu wangu nha si waceleleye bwete,

ze suguzi hamba zo wulipicizwe.

11 No mumaje wukondya kunolwa

ho wuwikanizwe ku witumamilwe,

mumaje wunolwa no kukwemwa

ho wupegwe mu miwoko yo muwulaji.

12 Gwe go munhu, wulile wuzozize muno ko wusungu,

hambi u mumaje wuwu,

nha wuwikanizwe so wanhu wangu,

na so walongola wose wa Isilayeli.

Hambi wose nha wowulagwa,

hamonga ne wanhu wose wono wasigaye.

Ale, witowaje mu mhambaga yako ko wusungu.

13 Nane nghuwajela du wanhu wangu,

na hono waleme kunena,

ale, zinghani zizi zose zowaziza.

14 “Alu, 'gwe go munhu, wuwile. Wutowe maganza gako, nawo mumaje wowa kucinya kenji na kenji. Hambi mumaje wuwo, nha mumaje wono uwulagaga, kanyhi wo mumaje wo kogofya muno, no kuwulaga.
15 Wo mumaje wono wowanoza i wanhu wangu wawule vilungo no kwikobala. Nane nghugogofya i makaya gawo so mumaje wono wukung'alala nhe muli, wono wuwikanizwe ho wuwulaje.
16 Alu 'gwe go mumaje mukali, weze kutema kulume no kucekulu!

“Weze kutema ho hose du, mu nghango yo wupitucile wako akuno na akuno.
17 Nane nhavivilya, notowa maganza gangu, ne likuwo lyangu nhondo ho lyomalika. Vyo nghulonga nene no Muwaha Mulungu.”

U mumaje wo mutemi wa ku Babiloni

18 Muwaha Mulungu yakanonjela kanyhi yakatya,
19 “Gwe go munhu, alu wuwicile vitanjilo mu nzila zimbaha nyejete, zono u mutemi wa ku Babiloni kosuma kuzikola, akuno yadinghuye no mumaje wakwe. Ninga zinzila zizo zose, zeze zilawiliye mu yisi monga. Kanyhi wuwicile ne zimbawo ze vitanjilo ha nzila panda ye zinzila zizo.
20 Citanjilo cimonga comulajila mutemi i nzila yono yibitile sunga kwi'kaya lye Waamoli lya ku Laba. Ne citanjilo ciyagwe naco, comulajila i nzila yono yibitile ku yisi ya Yuda, kwi'kaya lya Yelusalemi lyono lizenjeywe magomawakali.
21 Hambi u mutemi wa Babiloni, nha yemile ha nzila panda, ho yayimanye nzila yono koyityatya, yakuzisinjisa zisonga zakwe mwi'pongolo, yakutowa cisangu, yakuviwuza vihwani vyakwe ho vimulonjele, yakulilanga litoga lye nimu yono yilavigwe so likumbiko.
22 Ale! Luno, yayibata songa mu muwoko wakwe wo kulume yono yandicigwe ‘Yelusalemi!’ Nayo songa yiyo yikumulonjela yabite yakawikanize vinhu vyo kugakumulila majinji, yazoze lizozo lyo wulugu, yakawice mu milango vinhu vyo kukumulila majinji, yawice matuta go muhangha, yahimbe ne makombo.
23 Ninga i wanhu wa mwi'kaya lya Yelusalemi, hamba si wozihuwilila lukulu zizi, ku soko yo wilumbanize no mutemi wa Babiloni wono wakondya kwilumbaniza nayo. Ninga i nghani yiyi yowa nha suguzi yono yowakumbuciza zo wubi wawo, kanyhi yowasugula nha vyono wolotekwa.
24 Na azi kanyhi zo nghulonga, ane no Muwaha Mulungu nghutya, U wubi wenyu wakondya kugubulwa calinze. Ne wanhu wose nha wakondya kumanya vyono muli wasutyi. Hambi nha mukuwulajila wubi wenyu mu metumo genyu gose. Holeka alu mweza muli wanhu wa kuling'anizwa du, nane luno nomutajilenyi mu miwoko ye walugu wenyu.

25 “Agwe go mulongola we wanhu wa Isilayeli, go mulongola u mwituma zimbi, lizuwa lyako, lizuwa lyo wulipicizwe wako we ndumilizo, likuza.
26 Nane no Muwaha Mulungu, mbeka nalonga du nakondya. Alu wuwuse citambala cako, wuvule no mukumbo. Hambi nha si zowa kanyhi nha vyono zali. Alu, wuwenulaje waciwa, watuje lilumbo! Wuwawusize lilumbo wose wono watemile!
27 Matongo nye, woyi hee matongo! Heya mbeka, nolinoza likaya liwe matongo. Ninga hamba nhavivyo, zizi zose si zokuza, sunga kolo yakondya kuza u mutemi mono nomuhagula, ho yaze yaliling'anize likaya. Hanyuma ho nimupele mwene.”

U mumaje, ne Waamoni

28 “Gwe go munhu, ale wuwile. Wuwalonjele zono ane no Muwaha Mulungu, nghuwalonjela wawo Waamoni, wono wakuwatovulanga i Waisilayeli. Wuwalonjelaje wutye.

‘U mumaje wawikanizwa ho wongomeze.

Wakondya kukwemwa ho wuwulaje,

wumulice nhe limuli.’

29 Wugubulilwe wono mukutya mukugubulilwa, wugubulilwe wuwo wa wulambi. Sunga no wuwizi wenyu wose wa wulambi du. Hambi anye nha muli wetuma zimbi du, ninga lizuwa lyenyu lyasejelela, lizuwa lyo wulipicizwe wenyu we ndumilizo. Hambi u mumaje nha wozidumulanga zisingo zenyu.

30 “Ale, wugomoleze mumaje mu njasulu yayo! Nane nomuling'anizizenyi i hanhu hono mwawumbilwa, mu yisi yono mwalelelwa.
31 Nagwe koliwona likuwo lyangu, lono lyeza kukuziza nhe malambi go moto. Nane nokuwika mu miwoko ye wanhu i wakali wono wali nzizila, wono wamanyile mulimo wo kongomeza.
32 Nagwe kokongomezezwa mu moto. Ne sakami yako, yomwajicila mu yisi yako. Na ku wulawa lulo, howa hasina kanyhi mono kolokukumbucilila lukulu.” Vyo yakutya u Muwaha Mulungu.


Ezekiyeli 22

U wubi we likaya lya Yelusalemi

1 Muwaha Mulungu yakanonjela kanyhi yakatya,
2 “Gwe go munhu, alu wule, wakondya kwiwikaniza wuliling'anize likaya lya Yelusalemi, lyono limemile wuwulaji wose? Ale, wulimanyizaje ze makani gayo gose ge mawifyo, gono litumaga.
3 Wulilonjele likaya zono nghulonga, ane no Muwaha Mulungu, wulilonjele vyono, alu vyono wakondya kuwawulaga wanhu wenji wa mwi'kaya lilyo, na vyono wakondya kwiyavufya go mweneco so wuhemba vihwani wako 'gwe Yelusalemi, holeka lizuwa lyako nalyo lyasejelela.
4 Hambi nha wuli musutyi ku soko yo wuwulaji wako, hamonga no wiyavufya wuli wene so vihwani vyono wavinoza, na ku soko yiyo, lizuwa lyako lyasejelela, ne lipango lyako lyafika! Na yiyi yo soko yono nghawaleka we makungugo ganji wakusimanje, ne we ma'si ganji nawo, wakusekanje.
5 I ma'si ge behi, hamonga ne ma'si go kutali, gose gakukusekanga, ku soko ye mikalile yako mibi.
6 Ne walongola wose we Waisilayeli, nha weyitumbiye weneco vilungo vyawo, kanyhi wawulaji.
7 Kanyhi mwi'kaya, musina mwana hamba yumonga mono yawahulice walezi wakwe. Mukuwakoca-koca i wajenzi, no kwitujila cambuzi kuli wacekulu wafwile, na ku wana wono wasina walezi.
8 Na kanyhi musina hamba cogopo kuli hanhu hono helile, hamba si mukuzibata ne zisabato yaye.
9 Na wanji wa muli wanhu wako, wendile makani kuwakoweleza makani wayawo wanji, no kuwabalalila ne zinghani zo wulambi, ho wawambuze wawulagwe mbula soko. Na wanji, wakulya makumbiko ge vihwani, na wanji waceye no wetumaga zo wusenyha wawo du.
10 Wanji nawo wa muli wene, wagonaga ne wacekulu we wasogo zawo. Na wanji wendile kuwebata wacekulu lono wali mu vilo vyawo vyo mwezi no kugona nawo kwe vilungo.
11 Na wanji wasenyha, na wanji nawo, wawalonzaga i walamu zawo we cicekulu, hamba walumbu zawo wono wahanjile wasogo zawo, hamba wanyina zawo.
12 Na wanji wa muli wanhu wako wakuwa kusolwa no kulipwa malipo, wawa kutumwa wabite wakawulaje munhu. Na wanji wakudaya ngomola so migawa yono wandugu zawo Waisilayeli wawakopile, hanyuma wawa kwigolifya muno so cambuzi cono cikulawa kuli wandugu zawo. Wene wakondya kusemwa'ne.” Vyo yakutya no Muwaha Mulungu.

13 “Alu, lanje, nane nocitowela hamhelo, i cambuzi cono wucitujile mu zinzila zo wuhizi no wuwulaji.
14 Alu 'gwe mwene wukwihwesa nha vyono kosuma kuwa no wukangalile, hamba kuwa ne vilungo vyo kwinulila muwoko wako, ane kolo nakondya kukunozeza zizo hongo? Ane no Muwaha Mulungu vyo nghutya, kanyhi noyifisiliza du.
15 Ane noweyagaza i wanhu wako, weyagalile mu ma'si 'gose na mu makungugo, nane nogakongojeza ne metumo gako mabi.
16 Na mu nzila yiyo, ne makungugo gose gokubedegala. Ninga agwe ho kolomanyila vyono, ane yo nene Muwaha Mulungu.”

Lityanilo lyo Mulungu lyo kwezeza

17 Muwaha Mulungu yakanonjela kanyhi yakatya,
18 “Gwe go munhu, alu Waisilayeli nha weza kanyhi wasina cambuzi kanyhi kuli'ne. Wehwana ne cuma cono cisina cambuzi, nha vyono shaba, hambe bati, hamba cuma, hamba lisasi, vyono vyalekwa, lono fweza yakondya kukwemwa mu moto.
19 Holeka luno ane no Muwaha Mulungu, nghuwalonjela nghutya nhavi, nawo vyo wenha, wasina cambuzi nha vivyo. Alu nane nowategula ku Yelusalemi wose hamonga,
20 nha vyono wakongholezaga mabwe ge fweza, ne shaba, ne cuma, ne lisasi, ne mabati, wavitajila mwi'tyanilo, ho vifugutizwe no moto no kuvyenjedula. Nhavivyo nane vyo nomukongholezezenyi mwi'kuwo lyangu na munhunduji yangu, nimwenjedulenyi.
21 Heya mbeka, nowakongholela wose ha Yelusalemi, niwapembele mu moto, niwenjedule ne likuwo lyangu.
22 Nawo wokwenjedulilwa mumo mu Yelusalemi, nha vyono fweza yenjedulagwa mu moto. Nawo baho nhondo, ho wolomanyila vyono, mbeka ali lyo likuwo lyo Muwaha Mulungu.”

U wubi we walongola we Waisilayeli

23 U Muwaha Mulungu yakanonjela kanyhi yakatya,
24 “Gwe go munhu, wuwalonjelaje i Waisilayeli nha vyono yisi yawo lu, yaweza nyavufywe, holeka nane lu, noyilipiciziza mwi'kuwo lyangu.
25 Hambi i walongola wayo nha wehwana zisimba zono zikolomelaga zinimu zono gakondya kuziwulaga. Hambi nha weza wawulaga wanhu, wakusola mapesa menji, hamonga ne vinhu vyo wuguzi wukamu. Na mu nzila ye wanhu wawulaji, wono wawapelaga pesa ho wawulaje wanhu, nalu wakondya kuleka wacekulu wenji wono wafwiliywe ne walume zawo.
26 Nawo i wakohani, wakondya kubena lilajilizo lyangu, sunga nalu, hamba si wakuvicelelela kanyhi i vinhu vyono vyelile yaye. Hamba si wakusuma kuwika wisimajile we vinhu vyono vyelile, ne vinhu vyono si vyelile. Hamba si wakusuma kwijiza wanhu, ho wasume kutemanula i vinhu vyono vyavufywe, ne vinhu vyono si vyavufywe. Na kanyhi wayibedegala ne Sabato. Na yiyi yo yikuleka sunga ne wanhu wa Isilayeli hamba si wanyogopile 'ne yaye.
27 Nawo i wetumami we selekali, wehwanile maminzi, gono gahawangaga zinimu zono gakondya kuziwulaga. Wakuwulaga wanhu, wene ho wagolipe.
28 Nawo i wawizi, wazihundice zo wubi wuwu, wehwana ne wanhu wono wahiliwaga swakala mwi'jinji. Wawonaga zo wugubulilwe wo wulambi, no kuwila zo wulambi du. Wene weyitezaga nha vyono wakuganula nghani yo Muwaha Mulungu, na kumbe nane no Muwaha Mulungu ho si niwatumile yaye.
29 Wakuhiza ko wusugu sawo ya wanji, no kuwaboka kwe vilungo. Wakuwatendela vibi i waciwa no kuwewonela i wajenzi.
30 Nane nghalanga-langa, ho nhuje munhu muli wene mono kosuma kulizenga lijinji, na mono kosuma kwima i hanhu hono majinji gamwaguce, ho yasume kuyitaza yisi, lono nane neza kuyongomeza yisi kwe likuwo lyangu. Ninga si namutuga yaye hamba monga.
31 Holeka ku soko yiyo, nane nowamwajila likuwo lyangu, niwakongoje mu moto, ku soko ye metumo gawo.” Vyo yakutya Muwaha Mulungu.


Ezekiyeli 23

I wahinza wetuma wubi

1 Muwaha Mulungu yakanonjela kanyhi yakatya,
2 “Gwe go munhu, kwali na wana we cicekulu wejete.
3 Nawo lono wali wakali wadodo, wekalaga ku Misili. Wakawuhodanga wunyacipale wawo, wakawa malaya.
4 U muwaha yacemwaga Ohola, nha cinji yo Samaliya. Nayo u mwanakwawe, yacemwaga Oholiba, nha cinji yo Yelusalemi. Nawo vyono wejete, wakawa wangu wakandelela wana walume ne wacekulu.
5 Ninga Ohola, hamba yawe yali wangu, hamba nhavivyo yajendelela du no wumalaya wakwe. Yaketipiza muno ne wendece wakwe wa ku Ashuli.
6 I wasilikale wono wali wavwalile myenda yihwanile, ye liwala lyo lugandu, i wanhu we lilumbo, wono wena witumami we lilumbo liwaha. Na wawo wose wali wazelelo du wapanda fwalasi.
7 Yali malaya mono yasenyhaga ne wetumami wose we Ciashuli. No wusenyha wakwe, wukamwambuza yakeyavufya so wuhemba vihwani vye Waashuli.
8 Nayo yajendelela no wumalaya wakwe, nha vyono yali yenha du kuko ku Misili, kono yawucila no wunyacipale wakwe. Hambi nha ku wulawa mu wuhinza wakwe, i walume nha wagonaga nayo, wakamutendela nhe wacekulu wamalaya.
9 Holeka ku soko yiyo, nane nghamuwika mu miwoko ya wawo Waashuli i wendece wakwe, wono yali viyendile nawo.
10 Nawo wakamuvula myenda yakwe, yakekala yali cipwi, wakawasola i wanangwe we cilume na we cicekulu, u mwene wakamuwulaga ko mumaje. Ne wacekulu wakawa wakulonga i zakwe du hanhu hose.

11 “Ninga hamba nhavivyo, u mwanakwawe Oholiba, nayo yaziwona zizi, ninga nayo yakacila kuwa malaya muno sinha vyono yali yenha Ohola.
12 Nayo nhavivilya, yali yena masulumizi, yawasulumilaga i wanhu we lilumbo, i wetumami we Ciashuli, i wasilikale wono wali wavwalile myenda yihwanile ye liwala lyo lugandu, sunga ne wasilikale wawalya i wazelelo wono wejelaga nha wazelelo waswanu.
13 Nane nghamuwona vyono yakondya kwiyavufya, na wose vyono wejete, wakawa wabi wose, nghologo monga.

14 “Oholiba yakacila muno muno kwiyinjiza mu makani go wusenyha. Yakakwegwa muno ne zihwani ze wetumami wawaha wa Babiloni, zono zali nghakatwe mwi'jinji, hiliwe liwala lidunghu muno.
15 Wono wali wawopwe misipi mu viwino vyawo, ne vipombelo vye mawala mu mitwe yawo.
16 Nayo Oholiba yali yeza kuwawona du, hobaho baho yakebatwa no wusulumizi, yakawa kutuma wanhu wabite ku Babiloni.
17 Nawo wanya Babiloni, hobaho wakawa kuza kusenyha nayo. Wakasenyha nayo muno, no kumwavufya muno muno. Sunga hanyuma nhondo, wakawa kumuleka.
18 Yakegubula cipwi cakwe calinze, ne wanhu wose nhondo wakamanya nha vyono mwene musenyha muno muno. Holeka nane, nhondo nghawa kumuleka, nha vyono nali nakondya kumuleka u muwahawe Ohola.
19 Nayo yakacila muno konjeza zo wusenyha wakwe, yakakumbucila vilo vyo wuhinza wakwe, lono yetumaga metumo go wumalaya mu yisi ya Misili.
20 Yakamema wusulumizi muno muno wo wusenyha, yakawa kuwasulumila i walume wono wena miwili miwaha nhe miwili ye zindogowe, wono walavyaga mbeyu yo lulelo nha ye fwalasi nume.”

21 Nhavivi agwe Oholiba, vyo wawukumbucila wusenyha wono wetumaga ha wuhinza wako, lono wali ku Misili, lono walume wedawalilaga vitombo vyako, sunga no wunyacipale wako, wukawa kuwuka.

Wulipicize wo Mulungu kuli Oholiba

22 “Alu agwe Oholiba, azi zo Muwaha Mulungu yakukulonjela, yakutya nhavi. Alu, mwekatala na wawo wendece wako. Ninga 'ne nowanoza wakukolelwe 'kuwo, nowaleta wakupilime mbavu zose.
23 Nowaleta Wababiloni wose, hamonga ne Wakalidayo, ne wanhu walume, wono wolawa ku Pekodi, na Showa, na ku Kowa, hamonga ne Waashuli wose. Nowakongholela hamonga i wazelelo waswanu, wono wena lilumbo liwaha mu witumami wawo, hamonga ne wetumami walongola, ne wasilikale wapanda fwalasi, wono wena milimo ye lilumbo liwaha mu mulimo wawo.
24 Waze wakuhome ku wulawilila ku lubavu lwa kusumuka, wolokuza ne lipuga liwaha lye wasilikale, ne matuka go wulugu, hamonga ne magali. Wolowa wena ne zingula zawo, ne zikofiya ze cuma mu mitwe yawo, nawo wolokupilima mbavu zose. Nane nokukombola mu miwoko yawo, nawo wokuling'aniziza mu wityatye wo wulajilize wawo vyono winha.
25 Ku soko agwe Oholiba, mbeka wanghoza muno likuwo, holeka nane, nowaleka wawo, wakutendele mu nghango ye likuwo lyawo vyono lyowa. Ninga wumanye vyono kolo wakuza, wolokudumula mhula yako, hamonga ne makutu gako, hanyuma ho wawawulaje sunga ne wanago. Na mbeka, wowasola i wanago we cilume na we cicekulu wawakongoje womi mu moto.
26 Wolovidemulanga vivwalo vyako, wasole hamonga no wihambo wako wo wulenzi.
27 Nane baho nhondo, ho nolowudumizya lukulu wusulumizi wo wusenyha wako, hamonga ne makani gako ge cikomaza, gono wetumaga ku wulawilila lono wali wukali ku Misili. Nagwe ku wulawilila baho, nhondo si kolovinugulila kanyhi meso, i vihwani, hamba kwihwesa kanyhi za ku Misili.”

28 Azi kanyhi zo Muwaha Mulungu yakulonga yakutya, “Ane nokulavya kuli wanhu wono wuwasulile, kanyhi wono mwakondya kwileka nawo.
29 Nawo vyono wakusulile, holeka wovisola vinhu vyako vyose, vyono wavisawila mu vilungo vyako, wolokuvula myenda yako, wakulece wuli cipwi, ne zinghani zako zo wumalaya wako, nazo zolokwijela calinze. Alu u wusulumizi wako hamonga no wusenyha wako,
30 vyo vikuleteye zizi zose. Hambi nha wali wuli malaya muwaha wa muli makungugo gose agwe Oholiba, sunga nhondo wukawa kwiyavufya gwegwe go mweneco ne vihwani vyawo.
31 Hambi nha wukazityatya zinghanzo zo muwahawo Ohola, holeka nane nokung'wesa cikombe cicilya du co wulipicizwe, nagwe wucing'we.”

32 Muwaha Mulungu yakutya nhavi,

“Nagwe Oholiba,

kong'wela du mu cikombe co muwahawo Ohola,

naco ho ciwaha muno kanyhi citali.

Ne wanhu wose wokwizwajiza no kukubedegala,

naco cikombe cimemile muno.

33 Ne cikombe cico co muwahawo Samaliya,

i cikombe co woga no wuciwa,

cokunoza wugale muno, wuwe no wukalale.

34 Heya kocing'wa hamba si kosigaza cinji mugati yaco,

kovitafunya ne vibada vyayo,

ne matombo gako kogazewanga.

Vyo nalonga ane no Muwaha Mulungu.”

35 Azi nazo zo Muwaha Mulungu yakulonga yakutya, “Alu vyono agwe wakondya kusemwa'ne, wanhumbila mugongo, nagwe lu kotuga magazo ku soko yo wusulumizi wako hamonga no wumalaya wako.”

U wulipicize wo Mulungu kuli Ohola, na kuli Oholiba

36 Nayo Muwaha Mulungu yakanonjela kanyhi yakatya, “Gwe go munhu, wule, wakondya kwiwikaniza ho wuwaling'anize Ohola na Oholiba? Ale wuwalonjelaje ze makani ge mawifyo gono wali kwituma.
37 Hambi nha wali kwituma makani go wusenyha, ne makani go wuwulaji. Wali kusenyha ne vihwani, no kuwawulaga i wana wawo weneco wono wandelela'ne. Wali kuwalavya makumbiko i wanangu kuli vihwani vyawo.
38 Kanyhi hamba si makani gago du gali wene gono wali kwituma. Langa wayavufya sunga ne Nyumba yangu, wakazibena ne zisabato zangu, zono nali nakondya kuzamula.
39 Na mwi'zuwa li lilya du lyono wawawulajila i wanangu, no kuwalavya makumbiko kuli vihwani, lyo lizuwa lilyo kanyhi, lyo wenjilila na mu Nyumba yangu no kuyavufya!

40 “Wakawalalika wanhu kenji na kenji, ku wulawa kutali muno, waze. Nawo wanhu wakaza. Nawo Ohola na Oholiba, wakehovuga, wakeyola ne lanji mu meso gawo, wakehamba no wihambo.
41 Wakekala mu magoda maswanu, na ha mwande yawo, wakatenjelwa meza yono yali yimemile vinhu viswanu muno, hamonga no wuvumba, ne mafuta gono nali niwapelile.
42 No winji we mazi ge wanhu wono wali wali mu wusangalale, gakahulikwa i mazi ge lipuga lye wanhu wono waletwa ku wulawa ku nyika. Nawo wakawavwika Ohola na Oholiba bangili mu miwoko yawo, hamonga ne vipombelo viswanu mu mitwe yawo.
43 Nane baho, nghawa kulonga nili wene mu nhumbula yangu nghutya, Alu lanje vyono wamwitumamilaga u mucekulu malaya, mono aluno yasakala ku soko yo wusenyha wakwe.
44 Hambi nha wabitaga kuli wamalaya wawa kali kenji muno, Wabitaga kuli Ohola, na kuli Oholiba, wawa i wacekulu wasenyha.
45 Holeka i wanhu we ling'ani, wowaling'aniza mu ning'aniza yono yiwanojeye i wacekulu wasenyha, na mu ning'aniza yono yiwanojeye i wacekulu wawulaji. Hambi nha wali kwituma mulimo wo wusenyha, sunga ne miwoko yawo yihodanjice ne sakami.”

46 Azi kanyhi zo yakulonga u Muwaha Mulungu yakutya, “Langa, ta, nane noleta lipuga lye wanhu, waze wawahungucile, no kuwaboka vinhu vyawo.
47 Ne lipuga lilyo lye wanhu, lyowatowanga ne mabwe, no kuwawulaga kwe mimaje yawo. Wawawulaje ne wana wawo, we cilume na we cicekulu, no kuzipembela zikaya zawo.
48 Nane nowupezezya lukulu wusenyha mu yisi yawo, i wacekulu wose ho wafundwe, walece kuzityatya kanyhi zinghani zo wusenyha, nha vyono wetumaga.
49 Ninga nanye Ohola na Oholiba, ane nomulipicizenyi ku soko yo wusenyha wenyu, na ku soko yo wubi wenyu wo kuhemba vihwani. Nanye baho ho momanyila vyono ane yo nene Muwaha Mulungu.”


Ezekiyeli 24

Sufuliya yono yina nghangaga

1 Na mwi'zuwa lye likumi, lya mu mwezi we likumi, wa mu mwaka we licenda, wa mu wuhamywe wetu, u Muwaha Mulungu yakanonjela yakutya,
2 “Gwe go munhu, alu wulyandikaje lizuwa lili, hambi lizuwa lili, nha lye 'zuwa lyono umutemi wa Babiloni, kolokuziza yayipilime Yelusalemi.
3 Holeka i wanhu wangu wono wakondya kumbedegala, nghusaka wuwalonjele fumbo yiyi, yono ane no Muwaha Mulungu nghuwatya,

Wutelece sufuliya mu moto,

wuyimemye malenga twaa!

4 Wuyitajile mugati yayo zimhonje ziswanu ze zinyama,

zinyama ze viwoko ne vima,

wutajilize hamonga ne zinyama ziswanu zono wuhaguye,

zono zihangahanjile ne mafupa.

5 Ninga witumamilaje zinyama ze ngholo nhelevu,

hanyuma, wuyifwanghanize ne zingwagu nyinji sufuliya yiyo.

Wugalece malenga gahemule,

gahemuse mafupa hamonga ne zinyama zayo.”

6 Azi kanyhi zo Muwaha Mulungu yakulonga yakutya, “Woyi hee! Gwe go likaya lyo wuwulaji! Hambi nha wehwana sufuliya yono yina nghangaga, yono si yosumika hamba ku wukwemwe yaye. Ale, wunimule mhonje monga monga ya nyama mugati yayo, sunga halece kusigala hamba mhonje monga yaye.
7 Hambi mwi'kaya, nha mwali mowuli wuwulaji, ne sakami ye wanhu, hamba si yamwajika mu muhangha yaye, hono hogali manghundi ho yifucilice, langa yamwajika mucanyha ye litalawe.
8 Nane nghawa kuyileka sakami yiyo mucanyha ye litalawe, ho yilece kwihundika, ho likuwo lyangu likolele no kulipiciza.”

9 Azi nazo kanyhi zo Muwaha Mulungu yakulonga yakutya, “Woyi, hee! Gwe, 'gwe 'kaya lyo wuwulaji! Ane nane nokonjeleza zingwagu ziwe nyinji.
10 Lete ngwagu nyinji! Wufwanghanize moto! Telece nyama! Hemuse muhuzi! Takamize mafupa!
11 Hanyuma wuyijelece mu moto yiyo sufuliya ye shaba, yono yisina cinji mugati yayo, wuyilece yipye sunga yidunghuwale. Nawo wavufwe wayo, hamonga ne nghangaga yayo, vyowa vyakondya kukongojela, nayo yowa yezwa kanyhi,
12 hamba nghangaga yose yilece kuwucila lukulu mu moto.
13 Gwe Yelusalemi, i metumo gako go wusenyha, gakondya kukwavufya. Nane hamba niwe najela kukweza, ninga hamba nhavivyo vyo wukali si nela yaye. Nane manyile nha vyono si kokwelela lukulu, sunga langa kolo wakondya kuwuwona wukali we likuwo lyangu.
14 Vyo nghutya ane no Muwaha Mulungu. Nha vyono cidaha caza, nane nokwituma. Nane hamba si nowunenela wubi wako, hamba kukuwonela lusungu, hamba si nokuleka yaye. Langa nokulipiciza du ku soko ye metumo gako gono wetuma.” Vyo yakutya u Muwaha Mulungu.

U wufwe wo mugolece wo muwizi Ezekiyeli

15 U Muwaha Mulungu yakanonjela kanyhi yakatya,
16 “Gwe go munhu, ane nokuwusiza lwa kamonga u munhu mono wumwendile muno. Ninga wulanjilile, wulece kulila, hamba kulavya mahozi yaye.
17 Hamba cililo cako cilece kuhulikwa na yunji. Kanyhi wulece kuciwopola ne cipombelo cako, hamonga ne malatu gako ho ciwe nha citanjilo co wufwilwe wako. Hamba wulece kwigubiciza ku wusu, hamba kulya zindigwa nha zono walizaga wanhu wono wali mu wusungu wo wufwilwe.”

18 Ale, nane nghalongana ne wanhu hakali mitondo, ne lizuwa lyali lyeza kuswa, cinyamihe, u mugolece wangu yakawa kufwa. Nane wali weza kwela mitondo yaco, nghawa kwituma nha vyono u Muwaha Mulungu yandajiliza nitume.
19 Ninga nawo i wanhu wakambuza wakutya, “Alu nhawule wukwituma nhavivi?”

20 Nane nghapitula nghawalonjela nghatya, “U Muwaha Mulungu kanonjela nha
21 vyono, nimulonjelenyi anye nyo Waisilayeli wose, nghani azi, vyono u mwene yakutya nhavi, Anye mwidunhumulilaga i vilungo vya mu Nyumba yangu. Mwendile kubita kuyilanga no kuyiwona, ninga u Muwaha Mulungu koyavufya. Sunga ne wazelelo wa mu zimhuga zenyu, wono wolowa wakali wasigaye mumo mu Yelusalemi, wolowulajilwa wose ku wulugu.
22 Ale, nanye baho ho mulanozeza zizi zono nghuzinoza 'ne. Hambi nha si mologubiciza wusu wenyu, hamba kulya zindigwa nha zono walizaga wanhu wono wali mu wusungu wo wufwilwe.
23 Hamba si molojenda musina cipombelo mu mitwe yenyu, hamba si molojenda musina malatu mu migulu yenyu, hamba si mololila, hamba kwikunguza yaye. Langa wose molonyeta ku soko yo wubi wenyu, kanyhi molowa kugula munhu na muyagwe.
24 Hambi ane Ezekiyeli nha yo nolowa citanjilo kuli'nye, nanye molokwituma zose nha vyono nene nozize. Nayo Muwaha Mulungu yakutya nha vyono, lulo kolo zejela, nanye ho molomumanyila nha vyono u mwene mbeka yo Muwaha Mulungu.”

25 U Muwaha Mulungu yakutya nhavi, “Alu agwe go munhu, ane nowawusiza lukulu yiyo Nyumba yangu, yono yali kuwapela nyemo no widunhumule, yono wali wendile kuyilanga no kuyiwona. Kanyhi nolowasola ne wana wawo we cilume na we cicekulu.
26 Na mwi'zuwa lilyo lono niletumila zizi, yuyo munhu mono kolo kuza yapone mu wongomele wuwu, u munhu yuyo yo kolokuziza, yaze yakulonjele nha vyono ziweye.
27 Nagwe mwi'zuwa lilyo, ho kologomolelelwa kanyhi i vilungo vyako vyo kulonga, vyono wali wakondya kuvyajiliza. Nagwe ho kololongana no munhu yuyo. Na mu nzila yiyi, nagwe mo kolowela citanjilo kuli wanhu wa Isilayeli. Nawo baho nhondo, ho wolomanyila vyono ane yo nene Muwaha Mulungu.”


Ezekiyeli 25

Uwuwililwe we wana wa Amoni

1 Muwaha Mulungu yakanonjela yakatya,
2 “Gwe go munhu, wuwalonjelaje wanhu wa Amoni.
3 Wuwalonjeje vyono wahulicize zono nghuwalonjela, ane no Muwaha Mulungu nghutya nhavi, Anye mwendezwaga kuyiwona Nyumba yangu nyavufywe, no kuyiwona yisi ya Isilayeli yakondya kunozwa yitongo, no kuwawona i wanhu wa Yuda, wakusolwa wakutegulwa ku wuhamywe.
4 Alu, vyono nanye mwali kuzisangalalila zizo, holeka nane nowaleta we makungugo, go lubavu lwa ku nyika ya kucilima, waze wamutemenyi. Nawo kolo wakuza, wosita zinghambi zawo mu muno mono yisi yenyu, wekale mumo. Wolya ne mikalo, wong'wa ne mele, i vinhu vyono vyali vinojeye viwe vyenyu, muvitumamile nyenye.
5 Nane nolinoza likaya lya Laba, liwe hanhu ha wuwiko we zingamiya, ne yisi yose ya Amoni, hawe hanhu ha wuwiko we zingholo. Nanye ho musume kumanya nha vyono yo nene Muwaha Mulungu.

6 “U Muwaha Mulungu kanyhi yakuzilonga azino yakutya, Anye mwatowaga maganza, hamonga no kuzumha-zumha so nyemo. Mukayibedegala yisi ya Isilayeli.
7 Holeka ku soko ya zizo, nane nomuwicenyi mu miwoko ya we makungugo ganji, waze wamuhizizenyi no kumusolenyi mateka. Nane nomongomezezenyi lukulu, mulece kuwa kanyhi likungugo hamba hadodo, hamba kuwa na yisi yono kanyhi yowa yenyu nyo weneco. Nanye baho ho momanyila nha vyono yo nene Muwaha Mulungu.”

Uwuwililwe we yisi ya Mowabu

8 U Muwaha Mulungu yakutya, “Vyono Mowabu yikutya yisi ya Yuda, yihwanile zinghungugo zinji zose.
9 Ale, nane nogaleka i makaya gono gayipilime no kuyidima Mowabu mu mipaka yayo, gose ho gahomwe, hamonga ne makaya gose matambule ga Beti Yeshimoti, Baali Meyoni, hamonga ne likaya lya Kiliyatayimu.
10 Na kanyhi, nogaleka i makungugo, gono gekaye ku nyika ya ku lubavu lwa kucilima, gaze gayisole yisi ya Mowabu, hamonga ne yisi ya Amoni. Ne likungugo lye Wamowabi, hodu hobaho liwe kuduma.
11 Nane noyilipiciza Mowabu, nawo ho womanyila vyono ane yo nene Muwaha Mulungu.”

Uwuwililwe we yisi ya Edomu

12 U Muwaha Mulungu yakutya, “I wanhu wa Edomu, vyono wawanozeza ko wukali i wanhu wa Yuda, no kuwalipiciza, ne nghani yiyo, likawa yisutya yiwaha kuli wene Waedomu.
13 Nane ku soko yiyo, holeka noyilipiciza Edomu, nowawulaga wanhu wose hamonga ne vilimu. Noyinoza Edomu yiwe nyhiwa, ku wulawilila kwi'kaya lya Temani, sunga ko kwi'kaya lya Dedani, ne wanhu wose wowulajilwa ku wulugu.
14 Nawo i wanhu wangu Waisilayeli, wolowalipiciza ko wukali Waedomu ku soko yangu, no kuwanoza Waedomu wawuwone wukali we likuwo lyangu. Nawo Waedomu baho ho wolomanyila u wukali wo wulipicize wangu vyono winha.” Vyo yakutya u Muwaha Mulungu.

Uwuwililwe we yisi ye Wafilisiti

15 U Muwaha Mulungu yakutya nhavi, “Vyono i Wafilisiti, wetuma nghani yo wilipicize, kuli walugu wawo we vilo vinji vyono vyapuluta, wakawongomezeza mwi'kuwo lyawo.
16 Holeka nane lu, noyihoma yisi ye Wafilisiti, niwawulaje wanhu wose, halece kusigala hamba munhu monga. Nowongomeza wanhu wose wono wowa wakali womi mu yisi yo wuhihi ye Wafilisiti.
17 Nowalipiciza ko wulipicizwe wuwaha muno, nowatacila ko wukali muno. Nawo woliwona likuwo lyangu vyono linha. Nhondo ho womanya vyono ane yo nene Muwaha Mulungu.”


Ezekiyeli 26

Uwuwililwe we likaya lya Tulo

1 Na mwizuwa lilongozi lyo mwezi mulongozi, mu mwaka we likumi na monga, wa mu wuhamywe wetu, u Muwaha Mulungu yakanonjela yakatya,
2 “Gwe go munhu, ayi yo nghani yono yikuleka wanhu wa mwi'kaya lya Tulo wakuyisekanga Yelusalemi wakutya, ‘Ahee, nhondo Yelusalemi yibenanjika! Ne vilungo vyayo vyo wusuluza alu nhondo vyamalika! Hamba si cokwisumaniza kanyhi nayo!’

3 “Alu, azi nane nene no Muwaha Mulungu, zo nghukulonjela agwe go likaya lya Tulo, nha vyono ane neza nili mulugu wako. Nowaleta makungugo menji kuli'gwe, waze wakuhome, nawo kolo wakuza, wolokuziza ne cihumbi, nhe mawingo ge lilenga.
4 Kolo wakuza, wologamwagulila lukulu i majinji ge likaya lya Tulo, wayigwise ne minala yayo yose. Nane nologafwagulila lukulu manghundi gose gawuce, niyinoze yiwe nhe'talawe lili bwete.
5 Nawo i wanimula zisomba mwi'lenga, wolonyaziza zinyavu zawo mucanyha yayo. Vyo nghutya ane nene Muwaha Mulungu. Ne makungugo woloyiteka Tulo.
6 Wawawulaje i wanhu wose wono wali mu makaya gayo mu yisi nghavu. Nayo Tulo, baho ho yolomanya vyono, ane yo nene Muwaha Mulungu.”

7 Muwaha Mulungu yakutya, “Nomuleta Nebukadineza u mutemi muwaha mono yacizize watemi wose, u mutemi wa ku Babiloni, yayihome Tulo. Nebukadineza kolokuza ne lipuga yiwaha muno lye wasilikale, ne zifwalasi, ne matuka go wulugu, hamonga ne wasilikale wapanda fwalasi, yakulawilila ku lubavu lwa kusumuka.
8 Ne wanhu wose wono wekaye mu makaya gono gali mu yisi nghavu, wolowulajilwa mu wulugu. I walugu wolohimba makweme, hamonga no kuzenga magomawakali, wakunozeze ne magagala, wokuzenjela lijinji lya ngula, wakupilime mbavu zose.
9 Wologamwagulanga majinji gako ne matinde, no kuyikumulanga minala yako ne vyuma vizito.
10 Ne manghundi gono golowa kutimulwa ne zifwalasi zawo zi nyinji, golokufuniciza. Ne misindo ye mazozo ge wapanda zifwalasi, hamonga ne magali go wulugu gono gakukwegwa ne zifwalasi, gologasinjisa majinji gako, kolo yeza kwinjila mu milago yako, nha vyono wanhu wenjilaga mu kaya yono yibenigwe hanji.
11 Nawo i wapanda zifwalasi wawo, wolowa kukola ne cihumbi mu zinzila, wakuwawulaga wanhu wako kwe mimaje yawo. Ne masumbili ge vilungo vyako, gose gologwiswa sunga ho hasi.
12 Ne walugu wako, wolowusola wugoli wako wagawane, hamonga ne vinhu vyako vyose vyo wusuluza. Nawo wologakumulanga majinji gako, no kuzimwagulanga zikaya zako zono zinojeze muno. Kanyhi, wologasola i mabwe gayo, ne zimbawo zayo, ne zikokoto zayo, wavigumile vyose mwi'lenga.
13 Nane nologadumya i mazi ge zinyimbo zako, hamonga ne mazi ge masumbi gako, hamba si golohulikwa kanyhi yaye.
14 Nololileka litalawe lili bwete, i hanhu hono wanimula 'somba wowa kwanika zinyavu zawo. Ne likaya, si lilazenjelwa lukulu kanyhi yaye. Vyo nghutya ane no Muwaha Mulungu.”

15 U Muwaha Mulungu, yakali yena zinji kanyhi zono yakulilonjela likaya lya Tulo, yakutya, “Nagwe kolo wasumwa, i wanhu wako wono wekalaga mu mwalambalo we lilenga, wolokogofywa muno ne cililo ce wayawo wono wakuwulagwa.
16 Ne watemi wose we zinghungugo zono zikaye mu mwejelele we lilenga, wolowuka mu magoda gawo go wutemi. Wolovula ne vivwalo vyawo vye citemitemi, wolokwikala hasi mu muhangha, akuno wakucicima muno. Hambi nha wolokogofywa muno, wakwizina ku soko ya zizo zono zikuweleye, kanyhi hamba si wolosuma kwiyimila ho walece kujendelela kucicima.
17 Nawo wolokwimbila u lwimbo lwe maziko gako, wawe kutya,

Ale, lanjenyi ta ze likaya lye lilumbo

vyono lyahodanjika!

Ne zingalawa zalyo zose zahamywa mwi'lenga.

Hambi i wanhu we likaya lili,

nha wali walitemile lilenga.

Wakawa cogofyo kuli wose

wono wekalaga mu mwalambalo we lilenga.

18 Nalu mwi'zuwa lyo wugwe wako,

vizingwa vikucicima sunga ne wanhu wose wono wekalaga mumo,

wakutinuswa muno so wuhodanjice wako.”

19 U Muwaha Mulungu yakutya, “Nane nokunoza wuwe kaya yo wambuce, wuwe nhe makaya ge matongo, gono si wekalaga wanhu. Nghugubicize ne lilenga liwaha kusi ku cisolo.
20 Nghutegule, nghufise sunga ko ku yisi ye wafu, wukekalane ne wanhu wono wekalaga mono yinze baho katali. Kanyhi nolokunoza wikale ku yisi yono yili kusi muno, i hanhu hono hena wambuce cibitilila, wikale hamonga ne wafu. Nagwe nhondo ho si koloyiwuyila kanyhi nhojo yako, no kwikala kanyhi hanhu hako, mu yisi ye womi.
21 Nane nolokunoza wuwe cinhu ca kogofya, na wuwo, wo wolowa wo mudumo wako. Ne wanhu walakulondola muno, ninga hamba si wulejelela lukulu.” Vyo yakutya u Muwaha Mulungu.


Ezekiyeli 27

Lwimbo lwe maziko ge likaya lya Tulo

1 Kanyhi u Muwaha Mulungu yakanonjela yakatya,
2 “Gwe go munhu, alu wimbe u lwimbo lwe maziko ge likaya lya Tulo,
3 i likaya lyono lizenjigwe mu mwalambalo we lilenga, lyono linozanaga wusuluza ne wanhu wono wekaye mu myalambalo yose ye lilenga. Wuyilonjele Tulo zono u Muwaha Mulungu yakuzilonga wutye,

Agwe Tulo, wevunilaga zo wuswanu wako,

4 Ne mipaka yako yili mugati ye lilenga.

Ne wazenji wako wono wakuzenga,

wakakuzenga wukawa nha ngalawa swanu muno.

5 Wetumamila zimbawo zo musonobali

ku wulawa mwi'tunda lya Helimoni.

Hamonga no mubici wo muhagata,

ku wulawa Lebanoni, yo wakakunozeza misijiti yako.

6 Wakayisola i mibici ye minghola ya ku Bashani,

wakakunozeza makasiya go kujendezya ngalawa zako.

Na hasi ye zingalawa zako,

wanoza ne misonobali yono yikalawa Kupulo,

yono mihiliwe no wulembo wo lubali lwa nhembo.

7 Ne mitote yako yo kwambila mbeho mu ngalawa,

yali ya myenda ya bafuta.

Yali mitalazwe ne bafuta ya Misili,

ho yiwe vipelu kuyiwona, ku wulawilila kutali.

Ne mapaziya gako,

gali manoze no mwenda muswanu we liwala lyo lugandu,

yono yikulawa ku vizingwa vya Kupulo.

8 Ne wanhu wono webataga mitela,

yo kujendezya ngalawa, wali wo wanhu,

wono wali walawile mu makaya ga Sidoni na Alivadi.

Ne wanhu wako we mahala mu Tulo,

wali wo watonji wako.

9 Ne waselemala wono wanozaga zingalawa,

wali wo wanhu wanghula muno, wono wala wa ku Babiloni.

Ne watonji we zingalawa zose za mwi'lenga,

wanozaga wusuluza ne maduka gako.

10 “Ne wasilikale, walawaga ku Pelisiya na Ludi,

na Libiya, wawo wose,

wakwitumamilaga gwegwe mwi'puga lye wasilikale wako.

Wakazitumbika zingula zawo hamonga ne zikofiya zawo ze cuma,

mu zinyumba ze wasilikale wako.

Wo wanhu wono wakuletela lukumyo luwaha.

11 Ne wasilikale wono walawa ku Alivadi, wakagadima majinji gako. Ne wanhu wono walawa ku Gamadi, nawo wakayidima minala yako. Nawo wakazitumbika zingula zawo mu majinji gako. Kanyhi wo wanhu wono wakonjelezeza wuswanu wako.

12 “Wukanoza wusuluza ne yisi ya Hisipaniya, wukatuga fweza, na cuma, na bati, wukavitumamila mu vinhu vyako.
13 Kanyhi wukanoza wusuluza ne yisi ye Wagiliki, na Tubali, na Mesheki, wawo wo wakukwawila, nagwe wukagula kuko wawanda ne viya vya shaba.
14 Wukakawa vinhu vyako kwe zifwalasi zo kukwega matuka go wulugu, ne zifwalasi zo kupanda i wasilikale mu wulugu, hamonga ne zinyumbu ku wulawa ku Beti-togalima.
15 Ne wanhu wa Lodesi, nawo wazaga kukwawa kuli'gwe, sunga ne wanhu wenji du wa mu mwalambalo we lilenga, nawo wakuletelaga mbali za nhembo, hamonga ne mibici yo mupingo, ho mukawane ne vinhu vyako.
16 Nawo i wanhu wa Siliya, wagulaga kuli'gwe vinhu vinji du vyo wusuluza, hamonga ne vilimwa vyako. Wene wakakuletela zumaladi, ne myenda yo lugandu, ne milimo yo wutalaze, ne myenda ye bafuta swanu, ne malijani, ne vibwe vidunghu vyo wuguzi wukamu, ho wakawe ne vinhu vyako.
17 Sunga ne yisi ya Yuda na Isilayeli, nawo nhavivyo, wakakuletela wuhemba no wuci, ne mafuta, no hati ye mhunjile swanu, wakakawa ne vinhu vyako.
18 I wanhu wa ku Damesiki, nawo wakagula vinhu vinji du vyo wusuluza, hamonga ne vilimwa vyako, wene wakawa kukawa ne divayi yono yalawaga ku Heliboni, hamonga no wukunzeje we zingholo wono walawaga ku Sahali.
19 Wene nawo, wakuletelaga cuma cityane, hamonga ne vinhu vye mhunjile swanu vyo kuhatila mu mboga, wawa kukawa ne vinhu vyako.
20 Nawo wanya Dedani, wakuletelaga myenda yo wuguzi wukamu yo kutandika mucanyha ye zifwalasi, wawa kukawa ne vinhu vyako.
21 Nawo i wanhu wa ku Alabiya, hamonga ne watemi wa ku yisi ya Kedali, wakuletelaga vinhu vinji du vyo wusuluza, hamonga ne zinyana ze zingholo, ne zinduwilu, ne zimhene, wawa kuzikawa ne vinhu vyako.
22 Nawo i wasuluza wa ku Sheba, na Lama, wakakuletela vibwe vyo wuguzi wukamu, na zahabu, hamonga ne hati yono yina mhunjile swanu muno muno.
23 Nawo we makaya ga Halani, na Kaneti, na Edeni, ne wasuluza wa ku Sheba ne makaya ga Ashuli, ne Cilimadi, wose wawo, wazaga kuli'gwe ku wukwawi.
24 Wakakuguziza myenda miswanu muno yo wulenzi, myenda yo lugandu, yono yina no wutalaze, hamonga ne mazuliya maswanu gono gena mawala hayo hayo, gono gali mawopwe viswanu ne milejehe.
25 Ne vinhu vyako vyono wakwawaga, vyadinghulilwaga mu zingalawa zimbaha muno za Hisipaniya.

Agwe, wali wihwanile ngalawa yono yili mwi'lenga,

Yono yidinghuye mitegulo mizito muno muno.

26 Na lono i watonji wako we zingalawa

wakondya kukufisa kutali mu ciligati ye lilenga,

i mbeho ya kucilima yokubenanjiziza mu ciligati ye lilenga.

27 Baho nhondo, sawo yako yose yo wukwawi,

hamonga ne wetumami wako wose wa mu ngalawa,

ne watonji we ngalawa,

ne waselemala wono wayinoza ngalawa,

ne vinhu vyako vyose vyo wukwawi,

ne wasilikale wose wono wowa wali mumo mu ngalawa,

ne vinhu vyako vyose,

vyolokwajililila lukulu mwi'lenga,

mwi'zuwa lyo wongomele wako.

28 Ne zimhwani,

zolobocelela i mazi ge cililo ce watonji wako we ngalawa,

lono ngalawa yikudodomela.

29 “I wetumami ne watonji we zingalawa,

wolotima wose,

wolobita weme mu yisi nghavu.

30 Na wose wolokulilila ko wusungu wuwaha,

akuno wakwimwajila manghundi mu mitwe yawo,

no kwigalangaza mu mavu.

31 Woloyiceta ne mitwe yawo ku soko yako,

wavwale ne magunila,

akuno wakulila ko wusungu wuwaha.

32 No kukwimbila lwimbo lwe maziko gako, wakutya,

‘Hambi nani kanyhi mono kowa yehwanile likaya lya Tulo,

Likaya lya Tulo lyono aluno lyanyamazwa mu ciligati ye lilenga?’

33 Na lono vinhu vyako vyo wukwawi vyakolaga

mwi'lenga wawamosyaga wanhu we makungugo gose.

Hambi i watemi, nha wakagolifywa no winji we sawo yako,

na so wugoli wako.

34 Alu, lanje, wabenecela mu ciligati ye lilenga.

Wakondya kudodomela sunga mu cisolo ce lilenga.

Nalu i vinhu vyako hamonga ne wetumami wako wose,

vyakondya kwajilila mwi'lenga.

35 “Ne wanhu wose wono wekaye mu zimhwani, wakucicima so zono zikutujile. Sunga ne watemi wawo wose, wenjilwa no woga, wakununghala na mu wusu wawo.
36 Sunga ne wakwawi wa mono'nze yose, wakondya kogofywa na zono zikwijeleye, nalu wakukuhoweleza wakutya. Hambi lu zili hayi, hamba si kolokwijela kanyhi cibitilila.”


Ezekiyeli 28

U wuwililwe wo mutemi we yisi ya Tulo

1 Muwaha Mulungu yakalongana kanyhi nane yakatya,
2 “Gwe go munhu, alu, wumulonjelaje u mutemi we yisi ya Tulo, zono ane no Muwaha Mulungu nghumulonjela nghutya nhavi,

Alu nha vyono wukwikumya,

no kwiwona mu nhumbula yako

nha vyono agwe wuli mulungu.

Nalu ti, vyono wukwiwona nha wuli mulungu,

holeka wikaye mwi'goda lyo wutemi,

mu ciligati ye lilenga.

Heya, kosuma nhawule kwihwesa wuli mulungu,

kumbe nagwe ho wuli munhu du,

hamba si wuli mulungu yaye.

3 Kanyhi agwe,

wukwihwesa nha vyono wina mahala menji si nha Danyeli,

na vyono hasina nghani yo yose ye cinyele,

yono yowa hundikwe hali'gwe.

4 Na kanyhi wukwihwesa nha vyono,

agwe wina mahala, na kanyhi wuli munghula,

holeka ti wuketujila sawo nyinji,

hamonga na zahabu ne fweza.

5 Wukwihwesa kanyhi nha vyono so mahala gako,

wanoza wusuluza no kwitujila cambuzi ciwaha muno.

Wedunhumula muno so wugoli wako!

6 “Alu, 'lece nane lu nghulonjele,

ane mono yo nene Muwaha Mulungu,

nghukutya nhavi, Alu vyono agwe wedunhumula,

no kwihwesa vyono nagwe weza wina mahala nho mulungu,

7 Nane lu nokutumila i wanhu wakali,

wono wasina lusungu, waze wakuhome.

Nawo kolo wakuza,

wovyongomezeza lukulu i vinhu vyako vyose i viswanu,

vyono wavitujila mu wunghula ne mahala gako.

8 Nawo wokuwulaga wakugumile mu cisolo ce lilenga.

9 Alu wule, kojendelela du kwiyitya agwe wuli mulungu,

sunga na baho lono weza kukuwulaga?

Ninga wumanye nha vyono, agwe,

kowa vyo wuli munhu du kuli mono yakukuwulaga,

hamba si kowa nha wuli mulungu yaye.

10 Kanyhi kolofwa nha wuli 'mbwa,

mu miwoko ye wanhu wajenzi,

wono wasina hamba mulungu yaye.

Ane yo nene Muwaha Mulungu mono nghuzilajiliza zizi.”

U wugwe wo mutemi we yisi ya Tulo

11 U Muwaha Mulungu yakanonjela kanyhi yakatya,
12 “Gwe go munhu, wumulilile ko wusungu u mutemi wa Tulo, so zinghani zono zomwagana. Wumulonjele nha vyono, ane no Muwaha Mulungu nghumutya nhavi,

Agwe wali wuli musambo wo mugoloka.

Kanyhi wali wumemile mahala,

hamonga no wusikame wo wuswanu!

13 Hambi nha wekalaga mu Adeni,

busitani yo Mulungu,

wavwalaga vibwe vye mawala gose vyo wuguzi wukamu,

ne yakuti, ne alimasi, ne belulo, na zumaladi,

na shohamu, na yashibu, ne yakuti-samawi,

na zumaladi ne bahalamani, ne zahabu.

Na vyose vivyo,

wakunozeza gwegwe mwi'zuwa lyono wawumbilwa.

14 Nghakuwicila malayika wo wogofye,

ho yakudime.

Hambi agwe nha wekalaga mwi'tunda lyangu lyono lyelile,

wukawa kujendela mu ciligati ye mabwe go moto.

15 Ne mikalile yako,

yali miswanu ku wulawilila mwi'zuwa lyono wawumbilwa,

sunga mwi'zuwa lyono wanziza kwituma wubi.

16 Nagwe, wali wuli mwipiganzi muno,

mu mulimo wo kugula no kuguza.

Ne nghani yiyi, yikakutegula ku wuhizi wo wituma wubi.

Na ku soko yiyo,

holeka nane nghakuswaswaniza wuwuce mwi'tunda lyangu lyono lyelile.

Nayo malayika mono yakudimaga,

yakakuwinjila kutali, ku wulawa mu mabwe go moto.

17 Agwe wedunhumulilaga wuswanu wako,

ne lilumbo lyako likakunoza witume nghani zo wulele.

Holeka ku soko ya zizo, nane nokugwisa ho hasi,

nghulece wuwe suguzi kuli watemi wanji.

18 Na ku soko yo wukwawi wako,

wukahatajila wavufywe i hanhu hako hono helile.

Na ku soko yiyo,

nane nghakupembela mu moto wukatakamilila lukulu.

Na wose wono wowa kukulanga,

wowona nha vyono weza mavu du.

19 Sunga ne makungugo gose wono wazaga ho wakuwone,

wakondya kogofywa na zono zikwijeleye,

nalu wakukuhoweleza wakutya,

Hambi lu, zili hayi, hamba si kolokwijela kanyhi cibitilila.”

U wuwililwe we likaya lya Sidoni

20 Muwaha Mulungu yakanyitya,

21 “Gwe go munhu, wulimanyizaje likaya lya Sidoni.

22 Wuwalonjele i wanhu wono wekalaga mwi'kaya lilyo,

zono ane no Muwaha Mulungu nghuwalonjela nghutya nhavi,

Ane no Muwaha Mulungu nili mulugu wenyu anye wanhu wa Sidoni,

ne wanhu wose wolonghumya nene,

so zono nomutendelenyi.

Nawo baho ho wolomanyila

nha vyono yo nene Muwaha Mulungu,

kolo nakondya kwilajila nha vyono ane nelile,

mu nzila yo kuwalipiciza

i wanhu wono wekaye muli'gwe Sidoni.

23 Hambi nha nolokuletela matamwa,

noloyinoza sakami yimwajice mu zinzila zako zose,

ne wanhu wako wolowulagwa.

Nagwe ho kolomanyila nha vyono ane yo nene Muwaha Mulungu.”

Yisi ya Isilayeli yolomoteswa

24 Muwaha Mulungu yakatya, “Hasina kanyhi nghungugo hamba monga, muli zono ziyipilime yisi ya Isilayeli, zono zayitendelaga Isilayeli vibi, yono kanyhi yosuma kuwa nhe mamiya ne mabijili, gono goyitamisa kanyhi Isilayeli. Nawo ho womanya vyono ane yo nene Muwaha Mulungu.”

25 Muwaha Mulungu yakatya, “Nane nowagomola kanyhi Waisilayeli, ku wulawa mu makungugo, mono nali niweyagazize. Ne makungugo gose ho womanya nha vyono ane nelile. Nawo i wanhu wa Isilayeli, wolokwikala mu yisi yawo weneco, mu yisi yono namupela u mwitumami wangu Yakobo.
26 Nawo wolowa kwikala mumo wapolile. Wolozizenga zikaya zawo, no kulima migunda ye mizabibu. Nane nolowalipiciza i wekalani wayawo wose, wono wawatendelaga vibi, ninga Waisilayeli wolowa wapolile du. Nawo nhondo baho ho wolomanyila vyono ane yo nene u Muwaha Mulungu, u Mulungu wawo.”


Ezekiyeli 29

U wuwililwe we yisi ya Misili

1 Na mwi'zuwa lye kumi na nyejete, lya mu mwezi we likumi, wa mu mwaka we likumi, wa mu wuhamywe wetu, u Muwaha Mulungu yakalongana nane yakatya,
2 “Gwe go munhu, wumulonjelaje u mutemi wa Misili, nha vyono yisi ya Misili yolipicizwa.
3 Wumulonjele nha vyono u Muwaha Mulungu yakumulonjela zizi u mutemi we yisi ya Misili, yakutya, ‘Ane nili mulugu wako, agwe go lizoka liwaha, mono wugonile mugati ye likolongo. Mono wukwiyitya nha vyono likolongo lya Nile lyako, na vyono ati yo gwegwe mono walinoza.
4 Alu nane notajila ndobogo za cuma mu zinzagasa zako, hanyuma nizinamatize zisomba za mu makolongo muli'gwe. Hanyuma niwe kukukwega ku wulawa mwi'kolongo lya Nile, hamonga ne zisomba zose zono zowa zikunamatiye.
5 Niwe kukutaga mu nyika, hamonga ne zisomba zizo. No muwili wako wogwa hasi, no kulekwa baho du, howa hasina munhu mono kowuzika. Langa nozilecela zindeje, hamonga ne malimu ga mu mbago go gakulye.’
6 Na baho, i wanhu wose wa mu yisi ya Misili, ho womanyila vyono ane yo nene Muwaha Mulungu.” Na kanyhi u Muwaha Mulungu yakutya, “Anye nyo Wamisili, i Waisilayeli, wali wamwitumbilenyi nyenye vyono mowapela wutazi, ninga kumbe ho si mukwiliye cinji yaye, ciba ne nghome ye ligwami si nha'nye.
7 Hambi nha wali weza kumwegamilenyi, anye mukawa kubeneka, no kuwacinyanga mu mawega gawo gose, no kuwapela wusungu wuwaha mu migongo yawo.
8 Holeka aluno, nane no Muwaha Mulungu, nghumulonjelenyi nha vyono, ane nowatuma wanhu, waze wamuhomenyi ne mimaje. Nawo kolo wakuza, wowawulaga i wanhu wose, hamonga ne mitugo zose.
9 Nayo Misili, yolosigala yili bwete du musina cinji. Nagwe go yisi ya Misili, baho ho wulamanyila vyono ane yo nene Muwaha Mulungu.

“Hambi agwe nha wali kutya, likolongo lye Nile lyako, ati kanyhi yo gwegwe mono walinoza.
10 Holeka ane nili mulugu wako, kanyhi nili mulugu we likolongo lya Nile. Nane noyinoza yisi ya Misili yiwe nyika no wambuce du, ku wulawilila kwi'kaya lya Migidoli ku lubavu lwa kusumuka, sunga ko kwi'kaya lya Aswani lyono lili ku lubavu lwa kutakama, sunga ko ku mupaka we yisi ya Kushi.
11 Na kanyhi, holowa hasina munhu mono kolokola mumo, hamba nimu yo yose. Molowa musina cinhu co cose cono cilekala mumo, sunga yize yipulute myaka makumi mane.
12 Nane noloyinoza Misili yiwe yo yisi yono yina wambuce muno si nhe zi'si zose za mono'nze. Ne makaya gose ge yisi ya Misili, golokwikala matongo mwi'daha lya myaka makumi mane. Nolowasola Wamisili, niweyagazize kutali ako na ako kuli zinghungugo zinji, wakekalane ne wanhu wanji.”

13 Muwaha Mulungu yakutya, “Na kolo yeza kupuluta yiyo myaka makumi mane, nhondo ho nolowakonghola kanyhi Wamisili ku wulawa mu makungugo, kono nali niweyagazize.
14 Nize niwalece wekale ku Patilosi ku lubavu lwa kutakama ye yisi ya Misili, i hanhu hono walelelwa. Ninga kuko, wolowa wena wutemi wono wusoceye muno muno,
15 hamba si wologatemela lukulu kanyhi i makungugo ganji yaye. Nolowanoza wawe wanhu wono wasina lilumbo, wono si wolowa ne vilungo vyo kulisuma no kulitema kanyhi likungugo linji yaye.
16 Sunga ne Waisilayeli nawo, hamba si walawetumbila kanyhi i Wamisili so wutazi wo wose. Ne nghani yiyo yolowamanyiza Waisilayeli, nha vyono kumbe yali nghani 'mbi muno yono wetuma, yo kuwetumbila wawo Wamisili. Na baho nawo Waisilayeli, ho wolomanyila vyono ane yo nene Muwaha Mulungu.”

U mutemi Nebukadineza yo koloyitema Misili

17 Na mwizuwa lilongozi, lya mu mwezi mulongozi, wa mu mwaka we makumi mejete na mupunghati, wa mu wuhamywe wetu, u Muwaha Mulungu yakalongana nane yakatya,
18 “Gwe go munhu, Nebukadineza u mutemi wa Babiloni, yawetumamila wasilikale wakwe yakayihoma Tulo. Yawadinghuza wasilikale wakwe mitegulo mizito muno muno, sunga ne mitwe yawo yikakulunjika yikatuga mihalu, ne mawega gawo gakakubuka. Ninga u mutemi, hamonga ne wasilikale wakwe, si wapegwa co cose so mulimo wono wetuma.
19 Holeka nane no Muwaha Mulungu nghutya nhavi, Alu noyisola yisi ya Misili, nimupele mutemi Nebukadineza. Nayo koyihoma, yasole mateka vinhu vyose, ne sawo yose ya Misili, gawe go malipo kuli wasilikale wakwe.
20 Nomupela Misili gawe go malipo go mulimo wakwe, hambi i wasilikale wakwe, nha wanyitumamilaga nene. Ane no Muwaha Mulungu vyo nghutya.

21 “Na kolo zikwijela zizi, ane nowanoza i wanhu wa Isilayeli wawe ne vilungo. No kukuleka nagwe Ezekiyeli, wusume kuwalonjela Waisilayeli, nawo wokuhulika zono wukuwalonjela. Nawo ho wamanye vyono ane yo nene Muwaha Mulungu.”


Ezekiyeli 30

U Muwaha Mulungu koloyilipiciza Misili

1 U Muwaha Mulungu yakalonga kanyhi yakatya,
2 “Gwe go munhu, wuwawilile, wuwalonjele zono ane no Muwaha Mulungu nghulonga, wuwalonjele wuzozize wutye,

I lizuwa lye nghakamo likuza!

3 Lizuwa lyasejelela,

lizuwa lyono u Muwaha Mulungu kolokwitumila zizo zose.

Lizuwa lye mavunde,

no wugaye kuli makungugo.

4 Kolowa na wulugu mu yisi ya Misili,

hamonga no wugaye wuwaha mu yisi ya Kushi.

Na mumo mu Misili, wanhu wenji wolowulagwa,

sunga ne ma'si menji golotekwa no kulekwa matongo du.

5 “No wulugu wuwo, wolowawulaga ne wasilikale wose wono wazimwe ku wulawa Kushi na Lidiya, na Libiya, na Alabiya, na Kubu, sunga na muli wasilikale wa mu wanhu wangu.”
6 Muwaha Mulungu yakutya, “I wanhu wose wono vilunzize ne yisi ya Misili, wono wakulawa ku Migidodi, ku lubavu lwa kusumuka, hamonga na wono wakulawa ku Aswani, ku lubavu lwa kutakama, wawo wose wolowulajilwa ku wulugu. Ne lipuga lye wasilikale lyono yisi ya Misili yilitumbiye muno, nalyo lyokongomezwa lyose. Vyo nghutya ane no Muwaha Mulungu.
7 Ne yisi ya Misili, yolowa yo yisi yono yina wambuce wuwaha muno, si nhe ma'si gose ga mono'nze. Ne makaya, nago golokwikala matongo du.
8 Nane kolo nakondya kuyipembela moto yisi ya Misili, no kuwawulaga wose wono vilunzize nayo, baho nhondo, ho wolomanyila vyono ane yo nene Muwaha Mulungu.

9 “Ne lizuwa lilyo kolo lyaza, ne yisi ya Misili yakondya kongomezwa, nane nolotuma wanhu wazize mu zingalawa, wawatinuse no kuwogofya i wanhu wa Kushi. Ne lizuwa lilyo mbeka likuza!”
10 U Muwaha Mulungu yakutya, “Nane nolomutuma Nebukadineza u mutemi wa Babiloni, yaze yawuwusize lukulu u wugoli wose we yisi ya Misili.
11 U mwene kolo yakuza, kolokuza hamonga ne Wasilikale wakwe i wakali, wono wasina lusungu, waze wayihodanje yisi. Woloyihoma Misili ne mimaje yawo. Ne yisi yose yolomema mivimba yose.
12 Nololinyaza likolongo lya Nile, no kuyiwika yisi ya Misili, mu miwoko ye wanhu wabi. Ne wanhu wajenzi, woloyongomeza yisi yose. Vyo nghutya, ane no Muwaha Mulungu.”

13 U Muwaha Mulungu yakutya, “Nolovihodanjila lukulu vihwani vyose, hamonga ne milungu yose yo wulambi yono yili mu Memufisi. Na holowa hasina hamba munhu monga, mono koloyitema kanyhi yisi ya Misili, hambi nha nolowogofya i wanhu wose.
14 Noloyihodanga yisi ya Misili ku lubavu lwa kutakama, nilipembele moto ne likaya lya Zowani, lyono lili ku lubavu lwa kusumuka. Nize nililing'anize ne likaya lya Tabesi, i likaya liwaha.
15 Nololinoza Pelusiyumu, i ligomawakali liwaha lye yisi ya Misili, liwuwone u wukali we likuwo lyangu. Nolowuhodanjilila lukulu u wugoli wose wa Tabesi.
16 Noloyipembela moto yisi ya Misili, na Pelusiyumu i ligomawakali liwaha, lyolowa mu wusungu wuwaha muno. Ne majinji ge likaya lya Tabesi, golomwaguka, gaze gagwe ho hasi. Ne likaya nalyo lyolosolwa ne lihwazi lye malenga.
17 Ne wazelelo wose wa mu makaya ga Oni na Pi-beseti, wolofwila mu wulugu, ne wanhu wanji wose, wolotekwa mateka.
18 Ne citi colodinda mu'si, kolo nakondya kuwanyenyanga vilungo i Wamisili, no kuwawusiza lukumyo lono wedunhumulilaga. Ne livunde lyoloyigubika yisi yose ya Misili, ne wanhu wayo wose wa mu makaya gose, wolosolwa mateka.
19 Nane kolo nakondya kuyilipiciza yisi ya Misili mu nzila yiyi, nawo ho womanya vyono ane yo nene Muwaha Mulungu.”

U mutemi wa Misili, yakuwusizwa vilungo vyo wutemi

20 Na mwi'zuwa lyo mupunghati, lya mu mwezi mulongozi, wa mu mwaka we likumi na monga, wa mu wuhamywe wetu, u Muwaha Mulungu yakanonjela yakatya,
21 “Gwe go munhu, ane nawubena u muwoko wa Falawo u mutemi wa Misili. Kanyhi hasina hamba munhu monga mono yabita ho yakamuwope ne bandeji, hamba kumutajila miti yaye, ho wupone, wutuje vilungo vyo kuwibatila mumaje kanyhi.
22 Holeka luno, azi zo yakulonga u Muwaha Mulungu, yakutya, nhavi, Ane nili mulugu wa Falawo u mutemi wa Misili, holeka nomubena i miwoko yakwe yose vyono myejete, u muwoko muswanu, kanyhi na wuwulya wono wali wubenece, no mumaje wolagala mu muwoko wakwe wugwe hasi.
23 Nane noweyagaziza Wamisili mu yinze yose.
24 Hanyuma niyinoze miwoko yo mutemi we Babiloni yiwe ne vilungo, nimupele no mumaje wangu yebate mu miwoko yakwe. Ninga i miwoko ya Falawo u mutemi wa Misili, noyibena, nayo kokugula no kufwa ha mwande yo mulugu wakwe.
25 Na heya, mbeka, u mwene Falawo, nomunyetya, no kumwinula u mutemi wa Babiloni. Nane kolo namupela mumaje wangu, nayo kolo yakuwusonha ku lubavu lwa Misili, baho nhondo i wanhu wose, ho wolomanyila vyono ane yo nene Muwaha Mulungu.
26 Noloweyagaza i Wamisili, weyagalile mu yinze yose. Nawo ho waze wamanye vyono ane yo nene Muwaha Mulungu.”


Ezekiyeli 31

Yisi ya Misili, yikwihwananizwa no mubici wo Muhagata

1 Na mwi'zuwa lilongozi, lya mu mwezi we kadatu, wa mu mwaka we likumi na monga, wa mu wuhamywe wetu, u Muwaha Mulungu yakanonjela yakatya,
2 “Gwe go munhu, wumulonjelaje u mutemi wa Misili hamonga ne wanhu wakwe wose, wumutye nhavi,

Alo agwe wihwanile nani mu vilungo vyako?

Hamba nghwihwananize na cinhu cilo?

3 Agwe, nghuwona wihwanile no mubici

wo muhagata wa ku Lebanoni,

wono wina manhambi gono gena mubeho muswanu,

U mubici mutali muno wuficile ko ku mavunde.

4 Hakawa ho gali ne malenga go kuwuwilusa.

Na kusi yayo, vikolongo vyo kuwulisa.

Navyo vikawa vikumwajila malenga

i hanhu hono mubici wali wucindice.

No kutegula malenga mu mabici gose ga mu mbago.

5 Nawo vyono wamwajilwaga viswanu,

holeka wukatalimha muno sinhe mibici yose.

Ne manhambi gayo gakakangala no kutalimha muno.

6 Ne zindeje ze mawala gose,

zikazenga manhuli mucanyha ye manhambi gayo.

Zinimu za mu mbago,

nazo zikalelela wana wayo mu mubeho wayo.

Ne makungugo ga kono'nze,

nago gakaceha mu mubeho wayo.

7 No mubici weneco, wali muswanu nye!

Wali mutali, wina manhambi matali.

Ne milela yayo,

yikawa yinjiye kusi muno muno

mu muhangha yikulonda vikolongo vye malenga.

8 Hasina mubici wo muhagata hamba monga

mu mugunda wo Mulungu,

wono wosuma kwihwananizwa nawo.

Hamba hasina musonobali wo wose

wono wina manhambi gono gehwanile gago,

hamba mubici wose wulya.

Na hakawa hasina mubici muswanu muno,

wono wihwanile wuwo mu mugunda wo Mulungu.

9 Ne manhambi gayo gono gali gakopile muno muno,

gakawunoza mubici wukawa muswanu muno.

Sunga ne mibici yose yono yali yili mumo

mu mugunda wo Mulungu

yikawuwonela wivu.

10 “Alu ane no Muwaha Mulungu, nokulonjela zono zolowijelela u mubici wuwo wono wawiluka, wukafika ko ku mavunde. Vyono, mu nghango yono wacilaga kuwiluka, yo nghango kanyhi vyo wacilaga kwidunhumula so wutali wayo.
11 Holeka nane lu nawulema, nawuleka wusolwe no munhu muwaha wa muli we makungugo. Nayo kowulipiciza mubici wuwo mu nghango yo wubi wayo.
12 Ne wanhu wakali muno wa muli we makungugo, wono wasina lusungu, wolowudumula wawugwise hasi. Ne manhambi gayo, hamonga ne vinhambi vyayo vyologwila mu matunda gose, na mu malolo ga mu'si. Ne makungugo gose gono gekalaga mu mubeho wayo, golowuka.
13 Ne zindeje zolokuza zizenje manhuli gazo mu mubici wuwo wono wugwiye. Nazo zinimu za mu mbago, zologakanyanjila manhambi gayo.
14 Holeka ku wulawa lulu, sunga mu vilo vyono vikuza, hasina kanyhi mubici wo wose, hamba wumwajilwe viswanu muno malenga, wono wulawiluka wutalimhe kanyhi no kufika ko ku mavunde nha vyono wali u mubici wuwu. Hambi i mibici yose nha yofwa, nha vyono wanhu wafwizaga, no kwilunza na wono wakubita ku yisi ye wafu.”

15 Na zizi zo yakulonga u Muwaha Mulungu yakutya, “Na mwi'zuwa lono u mubici weza kubita ku yisi ye wafu, nane noganoza i malenga gono gali hasi mu muhangha, gawugubice, ciwe co citanjilo co kuwulilila. Nologemila makolongo, hamonga ne vikolongo, ho vilece kuhuluma kanyhi. Hambi u mubici nha wolowa wakondya kunyala. Nololeta citi ciwaha, mu matunda ga Lebanoni, niyinyaze mibici yose ya mu mbago.
16 Nane kolo neza kuwutegula ku yisi ye wafu, i lizi lyo wongomele wayo, wogacicimya makungugo. Ne mibici yose ya Adeni, i mibici miswanu, ne mibici mimwajile viswanu ya Lebanoni, yono nayo yakondya kubita ku yisi ye wafu, nayo yokwendezwa du no wongomele wayo.
17 Nayo vyabitana hamonga no mubici wuwo, sunga ko ku yisi ye wafu, vikekalane na wawo wono wakondya kuwulagwa. Na wose wono wali wekaye mu mubeho wayo haciligati ye makungugo.

18 “U mubici wuwo, yo Falawo u mutemi wa Misili, mono hamba ne mibici ya mu Adeni, si yatalimha yaye no kunojeza. Ninga aluno, nagwe kotagwa ku yisi ye wafu, hamonga ne mibici ya Adeni. Nagwe kokwilunza ne wanhu wono si wamumanyile Mulungu, hamonga na wono wawulagwa ku wulugu. Vyo yakutya u Muwaha Mulungu.”


Ezekiyeli 32

U mutemi wa Misili, yakwihwananizwa ne mamba

1 Na mwi'zuwa lilongozi, lya mu mwezi we likumi na nyejete, wa mu mwaka we likumi na nyejete, wa mu wuhamywe wetu, u Muwaha Mulungu yakanonjela yakutya,
2 “Gwe go munhu, wubite wukamukaje Falawo u mutemi wa Misili, wumulonjele zono nghumutya. Ane nghumutya nhavi, Agwe go mutemi wa Misili wukwiwona nha wuli simba muli makungugo. Ninga hamba nhavivyo, vyo winha mamba du mu malenga wukuzumhya malenga mwikolongo. Kanyhi kugatibulaga malenga ga mu makolongo ne magulu gako sunga gawa nhope, no kugahodanga makolongo.
3 Ninga wumanye! Kolo makungugo menji gakwihanguliza hamonga, ane nokwibata mu lwavu lwangu, niwalonjele wakunimule wakutegule ku nhumbi ye likolongo.
4 Nane nolokubamiliza hasi, nghulece baho, nizilece zindeje, hamonga ne zinimu zose za mono'nze, zize zikulye.
5 Nolowiyagaziza muvimba wako, mu matunda na mu malolo.
6 Noloyimwaga sakami yako, yihulumile mu matunda, yivimemye ne vikolongo.
7 Nane kolo nakondya kukongomeza, nolowugubika wulanga no kuzihundika zinyelezi. Nalyo lizuwa lyolokwihundika mu avunde, nawo mwezi si wolosana yaye.
8 Nolovizimizya lukulu vimuli vyose vya ku wulanga, no kuyileka yinze yenyu yiwe mu wutitu. Vyo nghutya ane no Muwaha Mulungu.

9 “Ne makungugo gose, wologazika muno, kolo nghuwalonjela zo wongomele wako mu zi'si zono wukali namanyika yaye.
10 Na zono nghukutendela agwe, mbeka zowatinusa wanhu wenji. Nane kolo nghuwukamanga u mumaje wangu mucanyha, mbeka i watemi wolocicima so woga. Na mwi'zuwa lyono agwe wulagwila, mbeka wose wolocicima so woga ku so wumi wawo weneco.”
11 Nayo u Muwaha Mulungu, yakumulonjela Falawo u mutemi wa Misili, yakutya, nhavi, “U mumaje wo mutemi wa Babiloni wolokuziza.
12 Nane nolowaleka i wasilikale wakali muno, wono wali nzizila, wono wakulawa mu makungugo ganji, wakuzize, wawawulaje wanhu wako kwe mimaje yawo. Nawo wolowongomeza i wanhu wako wose, hamonga ne vinhu vyako vyose vyono kwidunhumulilaga.

13 “Kanyhi nozikopolanga ne zing'ombe zako, ha nhumbi ye malenga menji. Na ku wulawilila baho, howa hasina kanyhi wanhu wono wogatibulanga malenga, hamba ng'ombe yaye.
14 Langa nogaleka malenga gako mose, gowa gatulile, gasunjile kanyhi maswanu, ne makolongo gako nhondo, gowa kuhuluma gatulile. Vyo nghutya ane no Muwaha Mulungu.
15 Nane kolo nakondya kuyileka yisi ya Misili mu wambuce, kanyhi yihodanjice, i wanhu wose wono wekalaga mumo, nhondo ho wolomanya nha vyono, ane yo nene Muwaha Mulungu.
16 Ne suguzi yiyi, yowa lo lwimbo lwe maziko. I wacekulu we zinghungugo zose wolowa kulwimba, wakuyililila Misili, hamonga ne wanhu wayo wose. Vyo nghutya, ane no Muwaha Mulungu.”

I yisi ye wafu

17 Na mwi'zuwa lye likumi ne'hano, lya mu mwezi mulongozi, wa mu mwaka we likumi na nyejete, wa mu wuhamywe wetu, u Muwaha Mulungu yakanonjela yakatya,
18 “Gwe go munhu, wuwalililaje liwumbi lyose lye wanhu wa Misili. Wuwategule ku yisi ye wafu hamonga ne wanhu we zinghungugo zinji, wono wali vilungo.
19 Wuwatezaje nhavi,

‘Alu, nye, mukwihwesa nha vyono muli waswanu

mucizize i wanhu wanji lo?’

Nanye vyo mobita du ku yisi ye wafu

mukekalane hamonga na wono si wamumanyile Mulungu.

20 “Nawo i wanhu we yisi ya Misili, wologwa hamonga ne wanhu wono wowulagwa no mumaje mu wulugu. Hambi u mumaje, nha wakondya kuwikanizwa ho wuwawulaje wose.
21 I vitung'ati wawaha, wono wakondya kulongola ku yisi ye wafu, wono wehoma ku lubavu lwe Wamisili, wolowabocela i Wamisili kuko ku yisi ye wafu, wawe kutya, ‘I Wamisi, nawo wakondya kuza kukuno, alu wagonile wanyamaye twii, hamonga na wono wawulagwa mu wulugu, wono si wamumanyile Mulungu!’

22 “Ashuli nayo yili baha, yipilimwe ne vitenjelo vye wasilikale wayo. Na wose wawa, wo wawulagwa ku wulugu,
23 ne vitenjelo vyawo vili kusi muno muno ku yisi ye wafu. Ne wasilikale wayo wose wo wafwila mu wulugu, ne vitenjelo vyawo vicipilime citenjelo cakwe. Ninga ha mwamulizo, ho wayogofyaga muno yisi ye womi.

24 “Nayo Elamu yili bahano, nayo yipilimwe ne vitenjelo vye wasilikale wayo. Na wose wo wafwila ku wulugu, wakafwa si wamumanyile Mulungu, wakabita ku yisi ye wafu. Lono wali wakali womi, wawogofyaga wanhu muno muno, ninga aluno, woyi hee! Wakondya kugona wafwiye, hamonga ne cevu cawo.
25 Elamu nayo yigonile hasi hamonga na wono wawulajilwa ku wulugu, sunga ne vitenjelo vye wasilikale wayo, vyose viyipilime. Na wose hamba si wamumanyile Mulungu na wose wawulajilwa mu wulugu. Na mu vipango lono wali wakali womi, wawogofyaga wanhu muno muno, ninga aluno, woyi hee! Wakondya kugona wafwiye hamonga ne cevu cawo, hamonga na wose wono wawulajilwa ku wulugu.

26 “Nayo Mesheki na Tubali, nazo zili baha, nazo zipilimwe ne vitenjelo vye wasilikale wazo. Na wose wo wanhu wono si wamumanyile Mulungu yaye, na wose wo wawo wono wawulajilwa ku wulugu. Baho katali wawogofyaga i womi.
27 Ninga wene wali weza kufwa, si wazikwa mu nzila ye lilumbo, nha vyono wazikwa i wayawo vitung'ati we myaka ye katali, wono watima ku yisi ye wafu akuno wena ne vitwalo vyawo. Wakazicilizwa hamonga ne vitwalo vyawo, i mimaje, wakehegama mu mitwe yawo, nazo zingula zawo, zikajelekwa mu miwili yawo. Na mbeka, i vitung'ati wawa, lono wali womi, wawogofyaga muno muno i womi.

28 “Ayi yo soko yono yololeka i Wamisili wolobeneka wagone hamonga na wono si wamumanyile Mulungu wono wawulagwa ku wulugu.
29 Nayo Edomu yili baho, hamonga ne watemi wayo ne walongola wayo. Nawo wali wasilikale we nghakamo muno, ninga aluno wagonile mu yisi ye wafu, hamonga ne wanhu wono si wamumanyile Mulungu wono wawulagwa ku wulugu.

30 “Ne watemi wose wa ku lubavu lwa kusumuka, nawo wali baho, hamonga ne Wazidoni. Nawo wawo ha lulongozi, wali wali vilungo wono wogofyaga wanhu, ninga aluno wakondya kufwa ne cevu cawo, hamonga na wono wawulagwa ku wulugu, wose lu wagonile wacehile kanyhi hamba si wamumanyile Mulungu. Wahanga cevu na wono wakubita ku yisi ye wafu.”
31 Muwaha Mulungu yakutya, nhavi, “U mutemi wa Misili kolo yakuwawona wanhu wono wawulagwa ku wulugu, kosinyhimala hamonga ne wasilikale wakwe.
32 Ane namunoza Falawo u mutemi wa Misili yawogofye i womi wose. Ninga mwene hamonga ne wasilikale wakwe wose, aluno wowulagwa wazikwe hamonga ne wanhu wose wono si wamumanyile Mulungu, wono wawulagwa ku wulugu.” Vyo yakutya u Muwaha Mulungu.


Ezekiyeli 33

U Mulungu yakumuwika Ezekiyeli yawe mulindilizi

(Eze 3:16-21)

1 U Muwaha Mulungu yakanonjela yakatya,
2 “Gwe go munhu, wuwalonjele wanhu wako wawe wamanyile, zono zolokwijela kolo nghuleta wulugu mu yisi. Holeka i wanhu we yisi yiyo, wasole munhu monga wa muli wene, wamuwice yawe mulindilizi wawo.
3 Kolo yakuwawona walugu wakuza, mwene yatowe ndulele, yawasuguze wanhu.
4 Na kolo munhu yakuyihulika ndulele, nayo ho yalece kuyicelelela, na hono walugu waze wamuwulaje, yowa nghani yakwe mweneco, hambi nha keyambuza mweneco.
5 Kafwa so masutya gakwe mweneco, vyono si yawuceleleye wusugulwe wono yasuguywe ne ndulele. Ku soko hono yali yawucelelele wusugulwe, hamba si yali yafwe yaye.
6 Ninga nayo u mulindilizi, hono yawawone walugu wakuza, nayo ho yalece kuyitowa ndulele, ne walugu waze wamuwulaje yumonga wa muli wanhu wawa, u munhu yuyo mono kowulagwa, ayo kowa yafwa no wubi wakwe. Langa nane nomuwamba yuyo mulindilizi, nimulipicize so lifwa lyo munhu yuyo.

7 “Holeka agwe go munhu, ane nghukunoza agwe wuwe mulindilizi we nghungugo ya Isilayeli, ku wulawa lulu. Holeka ku wulawa lulu, weze kuwasugula wanhu kuli zono nowa kukutuma wuwasugule.
8 Na hono nghulonjele nha vyono u munhu ayo kofwa hono yalece kupituka no kuwuleka wubi wakwe, nagwe wikale si wumusuguye yaye yapituce, nayo lu ho yafwile mu wubi wakwe. Ale nane nokuwamba gwegwe, nghulipicize ku soko ye lifwa lyo munhu yuyo.
9 Ninga langa hono wuwe kumusugula u munhu mubi, nayo hamba kuhulika yaye, ayo langa kofwila mu wubi wakwe, nagwe kowa wusina wuhoswe wo wose.”

U munhu na lwakwe

10 Muwaha Mulungu yakanonjela kanyhi yakatya, “Gwe go munhu, wuwalonjele Waisilayeli kali kenji du. Hambi wene nha wakulonga wakutya, ‘U mutegulo wo wubi wetu ne masutya getu, wacilemela, alu ho copona wule?’
11 Ninga alu agwe, wuwalonjele vyono ane no Muwaha Mulungu, mono nili Mulungu mumi, mbeka si nghuwa kwendezwa kuwona u munhu mubi yakufwila mu wubi wakwe. Langa nyendile yapituce yawulece wubi wakwe yekale mumi. Holeka anye nyo wanhu wa Isilayeli, alu mupituce, mulece kwituma u wubi wenyu wono mwitumaga. Alo nhawulenyi mukusaka kufwa anye nyo wanhu wa Isilayeli?

12 “Gwe go munhu, alu, wuwalonjele i Waisilayeli nha vyono, u munhu muswanu, hono yetume wubi, i ziswanu zono yali kwituma, hamba si zomuponya yaye. Nayo u munhu mubi, hono yalece kwituma zo wubi wakwe, ayo si kolipicizwa yaye. Na hono u munhu muswanu yanze kanyhi kwituma wubi, ayo hamba si kopona ng'o.
13 Ane nosuma kumwihuwiza wumi, u munhu muswanu, ninga nayo hono yanze kwihwesa nha vyono i ziswanu zono yetuma katali zo zomutaza, hanyuma ho yanze kwituma zo wubi, yamanye nha vyono ane si nozikumbucila yaye i ziswanu zakwe zono yali yetuma. Langa mwene kofwa du ku soko yo wubi wakwe.
14 Heya, nosuma kumulonjela munhu mubi, nha vyono, agwe kofwa. Ninga hono yanene wubi wakwe no kwituma zo mugoloka na ze ling'ani,
15 nha cinji, hono yagomole cinhu cono yapinjicilwa, kanyhi yamugomolele munhu sawo yakwe yono yakopa, hamba cono yahiza, na hono yalece kwituma kanyhi wubi, yatyatye u wulajilize wo wumi, u munhu yuyo si kofwa yaye, langa kowumipa.
16 Nane nomunenela u wubi wakwe wose wono yetumaga, nayo kokwikala mumi, vyono lu yakwituma zo mugoloka na ze ling'ani.

17 “Kanyhi i wanhu wako, watezaga nha vyono ati ane si nitumaga ze ling'ani aa, ng'o! Langa i wene, wo wetumaga zono si zililing'ani yaye.
18 Holeka u munhu we ling'ani, hono yalece kwituma ze ling'ani, ho yanze kwituma zo wubi, munhu yuyo kofwa so wubi wakwe.
19 Nayo u munhu mubi hono yalece kwituma wubi, no kwituma zo mugoloka na ze ling'ani, yuyo kowuponya wumi wakwe.
20 Ninga anye nyo Isilayeli, mukali vyo mukutya du, vyono zono ane nghwituma si zili ling'ani yaye. Ninga nane nomuling'anizizenyi mu metumo genyu.”

I zinghani zo wugwe we likaya lya Yelusalemi

21 Na mwi'zuwa lye'hano, lya mu mwezi we likumi, wa mu mwaka we likumi na nyejete, wa mu wuhamywe wetu, u munhu yunji mono yehemya yakulawa ku Yelusalemi, yakanyiziza yakanonjela yakatya, nha vyono likaya lya Yelusalemi lyahoma.
22 Ninga nane, mu mapango ge cinyamihe, lono u munhu yuyo mono yehema yali yakali si nanyiziza, ane nane, nali nakondya kuviwona i vilungo vyo Muwaha Mulungu muli 'ne. Na lono u munhu yuyo mono yehemya yanyizizaga i mitondo yaco hakali mitondo, u Muwaha Mulungu, yali yakondya kumhela i vilungo vyo kusuma kulonga.

U wubi we wanhu

23 U Muwaha Mulungu yakanonjela yakatya nhavi,
24 “Gwe go munhu, i wanhu wono wekaye mu matongo ge makaya ga mu yisi ya Isilayeli, wakutya, ‘Abulahamu, yali yo munhu mono yali yali wene du mono yapegwa yisi yiyi yose. Alu ase nha cili wenji, holeka ne yisi yiyi yose yetu.’

25 “Ninga lu agwe wuwalonjele wutye, Anye nyo Isilayeli, mulizaga nyama hamonga ne sakami yayo. Kanyhi muhembaga vihwani. Kanyhi muli wawulaji wa wanhu. Alu nghanici, yono yoleka sunga mwihwese nha vyono, yisi yiyi yenyu nyenye?
