26 Anye nha mwitumbilaga i mimaje yenyu du. Ne metumo genyu gose nha metumo ga cevu du. Nanye wose muli wasenyha. Alu cono coleka sunga mwihwese nha vyono yisi yiyi yenyu, cicici cene lo?

27 “Ninga wuwalonjelaje nha vyono, ane no Muwaha Mulungu, mono nili Mulungu mumi, nghuwasugula nha vyono mbeka, i wanhu wose wono wekaye mu matongo ge makaya gose, wose wowulagwa. Nawo wono wekaye mwi'hala, wololigwa ne zinimu za mu mbago. Nawo wono wehundice mu matunda na mu mapango, wolowulagwa ne matamwa.
28 Nane noloyileka yisi yose yina wambuce wuwaha muno, novidumya ne vilungo vyono wedunhumulilaga. Ne matunda ga mu yisi ya Isilayeli, golowa gena wambuce wuwaha, hamba munhu wo wose si kolowa kusuma kukola mumo.
29 Nane kolo nakondya kuwalipiciza i wanhu so wubi wawo, no kuyinoza yisi yiwe wambuce, baho nawo nhondo, ho wolomanyila nha vyono, ane yo nene Muwaha Mulungu.”

U witumwe we zinghani zo muwizi

30 Muwaha Mulungu yakatya, “Gwe go munhu, i wanhu wako, wakuduwila zinghani zako, lono wetang'anila mu majinji ge likaya, hamba mu milango ye zikaya zawo. Wakulongana munhu na muyagwe wakutya, ‘Nye, alenyi cibite cikahulicize zinghani zono zilawile kuli Muwaha Mulungu.’
31 Nawo baho wawa kwikonghola wenji du ha mwande yako ho wahulice zono kolonga, ninga hamba si wakwituma zono wukuwalonjela wetume. Wakulonga zinghani mu milomo yawo du, ninga vyo wajendeleye du no wituma zimbi.
32 Na langa, ta! Agwe lu kuli wene weza kotya lwimbo luswanu muno, lwa munhu mono yena yizi lyono likuwendeza, mono yakusuma kutowa lisumbi viswanu, wakuzihulika zinghani zako, ninga hamba si wakuzituma yaye.
33 Ninga kolo zinghani zako zakondya kufisilizwa, na mbeka vyo zowa, ale nhondo, nawo baho ho wolomanya vyono kumbe mbeka u muwizi yali yali hamonga nawo muli wene.”


Ezekiyeli 34

Kuli ze wadimi, we wanhu wa Isilayeli

1 Muwaha Mulungu yakanonjela yakatya nhavi,
2 “Gwe go munhu, wuwalonjele no kuwawilila, i wadimi wose we yisi ya Isilayeli, wuwamanyize zono ane no Muwaha Mulungu nghuwalonjela nghutya, Woyi hee, anye nyo wadimi wa Isilayeli! Hambi nha mwiyilisaga nyo weneco du, hamba si mukuzilisa zingholo yaye.
3 Hambi nha mung'wezaga mele, no kuvwala i myenda yo wukunzeje we zingholo, akuno mukuziwulaga no kuzilya zingholo zono zijinile.
4 Ninga hamba si muzitondowozaga yaye zono zisoceye, hamba kuzilagula, zono zitamigwe, hamba kuziwopa zono zina nghomele, hamba kuzikongholeza zono ziyagaye ako na ako, hamba kuzilondola zono zajiliye. Langa anye muzikunhulangaga du.
5 Alu, zingholo vyono zali zeza zisina mudimi, zikeyagala caka, ne malimu ga mu mbago gakaziwulaga no kuzilya.
6 Na ku soko yiyo, zingholo zangu zikeyagala mu vigongo vitali, na mu matunda. Zikeyagalila mu zisi zose za kono'nze. Na hali hasina hamba munhu mono yakuziwuza, hamba kuzilondola.

7 “Holeka anye nyo wadimi, muhulicize lu zono ane no Muwaha Mulungu nghumulonjelenyi, nghutya nhavi,
8 Na mbeka, anye nyo wadimi, yowa viswanu hono muhulicize ane no Muwaha Mulungu mumi. Hambi zingholo zangu nha zebatwa ne malimu ga mu mbago, zakondya kuwulagwa no kuligwa nago, vyono zali zisina mudimi. Nawo i wadimi wangu, si wajela yaye hamba kuzilondola kose. Wene wakawa kwiyidima weneco du, hamba si zingholo yaye.
9 Holeka muhulicizaje lu zono nghumulonjelenyi anye nyo wadimi.
10 Alu nane nghumulonjelenyi nha vyono, ane no Muwaha Mulungu neza nili mulugu wenyu. Noziwusa zingholo zangu mu miwoko yenyu, hamba si nolomukombolenyi kanyhi ho muzidime yaye. Na nyenye nyo wadimi, hamba si mojendelela kwiyilisa nyo weneco kanyhi. Langa noziponya zingholo zangu mu milomo yenyu, zilece kuwa kanyhi ndigwa yenyu.”

U mudimi muswanu

11 “Nane no Muwaha Mulungu, nghumulonjelenyi nha vyono, alu nozilanjilila zingholo zangu nene no mweneco, no kuzidima.
12 Nozidima no kuzilanjilila viswanu, nha vyono mudimi kazilanjililaga zingholo zakwe, zono zali zakondya kwiyagala ako na ako, kawa kuzikonghola hamonga kanyhi. Nane nozigomola zose ku wulawa hanhu hose hono zali ziyagaliye, mwi'zuwa lyo wutitu, ne magayo mawaha.
13 Noziwusa mu wulawa mu zi'si zo wujenzi, nizikongholele hamonga, nizigomole ku yisi yazo zeneco. Nolozitonga sunga nizifise mu matunda ne vikolongo vye malenga, vya mu yisi ya Isilayeli, nane nolozilisa mu wudimo wuswanu.
14 Nolozidimila mu wudimo wuswanu, mucanyha ye litunda litali na mu malolo, mono mogali mahanze ge dedede, mu yisi ya Isilayeli.
15 Nane nomweneco, yo nolowa mudimi we zingholo zangu, nolozilondolela ne hanhu ho wuceho. Vyo nghutya ane no Muwaha Mulungu.

16 “Nolozilondola zono zajiliye, nizigomole, na zono ziyagaye caka, noloziwopa bandeji, zono zina nghomele, noloziponya na zono zitamigwe. Langa zingholo zono zijinile zina ne vilungo, azo zose nolozongomezeza lukulu. Hambi ane nha yo nene u mudimi mono ketumaga ze ling'ani.
17 Alu ane no Muwaha Mulungu, nghumulonjelenyi anye nyo litewa lyangu nghutya nhavi, ane nolomuling'anizenyi nyenye wose, munhu na lwakwe, nimutemanulenyi i waswanu wawuce muli wabi. Nazo zingholo nizikome ziwuce mu zimhene.
18 Ninga hamba nimulisenyi mu mahanze maswanu, yowa nha nghani ya bwete, hambi nha mogakanyanjila du ne migulu yenyu, i mahanze gono gowa gakali gasigaye. Nha vyono mwang'wa i malenga maswanu, no kugatibula gono gali gasigaye.
19 Ne zingholo zangu zinji, zikulya i mahanze gono anye mwagakanyanjiza hasi ne migulu yenyu, no kung'wa i malenga gono mwagatibulanga.

20 “Holeka alu ane no Muwaha Mulungu, nghumulonjelenyi, nha vyono noling'aniza haciligati ye zingholo zono zijinile na zono zigandile.
21 Hambi nye nha mwazikoma zingholo zono zitamigwe, mukaziwinjila kutali ne litewa.
22 Ninga'ne noziponya zingholo zangu, hamba si nozileka muzitendele vibi kanyhi, nane noziling'aniza mutugo no mutugo, nizikome ziswanu muli zimbi.
23 Nozipela mutemi mono yehwanile mutemi Dawudi, yo yawe mudimi wazo. Nayo kozilanjilila muno.
24 Nane no Muwaha Mulungu, nowa Mulungu wawo. No mutemi mono kowa yehwanile u mwitumami wangu Dawudi, yuyo yo kowa yo mutemi wawo. Nene vyo nghutya.
25 Kanyhi nonoza cilagane nawo, cilagane co mulamu. Kanyhi noziwusiza ne malimu gose makali mu'si, zingholo zangu ho zikale zipolile mu mawimbi, no kugona mu zimbago.

26 “Nane nolozimotya, nizilece zikale zilipilime litunda lyangu lyono lyelile. Kanyhi nolozimotya ne mvula, mwi'daha lyono zikuyihila.
27 Ne mibici nayo, yololela mikalo, nayo migunda, yololavya nhwanga mbaha, ne wanhu wose wolokwikala wapolile mu yisi yawo weneco. Nane kolo nakondya kugadumula manyolo gono gawawopile wanhu wangu, no kuwawika wawe welecele ku wulawa mu miwoko ya wono wali wawawicile wuwanda, baho nhondo nawo, ho wolomanyila vyono ane yo nene u Muwaha Mulungu.
28 Nawo we zinghungugo zinji, hamba si wolowateka kanyhi. Nago malimu ga mu mbago, hamba si golowawulaga no kuwalya yaye. Hambi nha wowa wekaye wapolile, hasina yunji wo wose mono kowogofya kanyhi.
29 Nane nolowapela migunda yono yina moso mwinji, noloyiwusiza lukulu ne nzala mu'si. Ne wanhu we zinghungugo zinji, hamba si wolowasekanga kanyhi yaye.
30 Ne wanhu wose nhondo ho wolomanyila nha vyono nghuwadima wanhu wa Isilayeli, hambi nha wanhu wangu. Vyo nghutya, ane no Muwaha Mulungu.
31 Holeka anye nyo ngholo zangu, nyo litewa lyangu, mono nghumulisenyi, anye muli wanhu wangu, nane yo nene u Mulungu wenyu.” Vyo yakutya u Muwaha Mulungu.


Ezekiyeli 35

U wulipicize wo Mulungu kuli yisi ya Edomu

1 U Muwaha Mulungu yakanonjela yakatya,
2 “Gwe go munhu, wuyilonjelaje yisi ya Edomu.
3 Wuwalonjele wanhu zono ane no Muwaha Mulungu nghuwalonjela nghutya nhavi:

Ane nili mulugu wenyu, anye nyo matunda ga Edomu!

Nolomulecenyi mu wambuce wuwaha.

4 Nologaleka makaya genyu matongo.

Ne yisi yenyu yolowa mu wambuce.

Nanye nhondo baho, ho molomanyila vyono

ane yo nene Muwaha Mulungu.

5 “Anye nyo wanhu wa Edomu, yo nyenye mono mwali walugu wawaha we wanhu wa Isilayeli. Sunga mukawambuza i wanhu wa Isilayeli wakawulagwa vibi, mwi'daha lye magazo gawo, mwi'pango lye ndumilizo lyo wulipicizwe wawo ku soko yo wubi wawo.
6 Na mbeka, vyono ane yo nene Muwaha Mulungu, u Mulungu mumi, anye Waedomu langa mofwa du, hamba si mosuma kwiponya yaye. Hambi nha muli wasutyi wawaha wo wuwulaji, holeka nanye i lifwa lyomwaganenyi du.
7 Nane noyinoza yisi ye matunda ya Edomu yiwe wambuce, kanyhi niwawulaje wose wono wowa kola mu yisi yiyo.
8 Nologagubika matunda gose ne mivimba, ne miwili ye wanhu wose wono wowa kuwulagwa mu wulugu, yolovigubika vigongo hamonga ne malolo.
9 Nane nolokunoza wulekwe cibitilila, na holowa hasina munhu hamba monga, mono kolokwikala kanyhi mu makaya gako. Nagwe go yisi ya Edomu, baho nhondo, ho wulamanyila nha vyono ane yo nene Muwaha Mulungu.

10 “Hambi agwe nha wukutya ti, gago i makungugo mejete, Yuda na Isilayeli, hamonga ne yisi yawo, vyose vyako yo sawa yako. Hamba yiwe ane no Muwaha Mulungu, yo nene Mulungu wawo ku wulawa na katali.
11 Ninga luno mbeka, nha vyono yinha, ane yo nene Muwaha Mulungu, u Mulungu mumi, nane nokulipiciza gwegwe so likuwo lyako, no wivu wako, hamonga no lusulo lwako, kuli wanhu wangu. Nawo i wanhu wangu, nhondo ho wolomanya nha vyono nghukulipiciza ku soko ye metumo gono wawatendela wene.
12 Nagwe Edomu ho komanya nha vyono nene Muwaha Mulungu, hulika ze maligo gako gose, gono wali kugaliga i matunda ge yisi ya Isilayeli, wukutya, alu gahodanjika, na vyono ati gakondya kutajilwa mu miwoko yenyu, mugatece.
13 Nane nakondya kuzihulika zinghani zako ze cinyono-nyono, zono wukunonga'ne, ko widunhumuzi wuwaha.”

14 Nayo u Muwaha Mulungu yakutya nhavi, “Ane nokuleka mu wuciwa, sunga ne wanhu we yinze yose wawusangalalile u wugwe wako.
15 Nha vyono nagwe wawusangalalilaga u wutekwe we wanhu wangu Waisilayeli. Holeka i matunda ge yisi ya Seyili, hamonga ne yisi yose ya Edomu, yolowa nyika. Na mu nzila yiyo, wanhu wose ho wolomanyila vyono ane yo nene Muwaha Mulungu.”


Ezekiyeli 36

Wumoteswe wo Mulungu, kuli yisi ya Isilayeli

1 U Muwaha Mulungu yakatya, “Gwe go munhu wulonje ne matunda ga Isilayeli, wugalonjele nha vyono ganojeye gayihulicize nghani,
2 yono ane no Muwaha Mulungu, nghusaka nigalonjele. Hambi i walugu we yisi ya Isilayeli, nha wakwitopa wakutya, ‘I matunda gago ge katali nha geza getu co weneco!’
3 Alu wuwawililaje, wuwalonjele zono ane no Muwaha Mulungu nghuwalonjela nghutya, Lono i makungugo i mekalani ne Waisilayeli, wagasolela no kugateka i matunda ge yisi ya Isilayeli, i makungugo gose gakawa kuwaliganga Waisilayeli.
4 Ninga aluno muhulicize zono ane no Muwaha Mulungu nghumulonjelenyi anye nyo matunda ne vigongo, nanye nyo mivwilo ye malenga, ne malolo, na kuli'nye nyo matongo, na kuli'nye nyo makaya gono mwatekwa no kusolwa ne makungugo gono gapilime.

5 “Alu nane no Muwaha Mulungu, nghulonjela mwi'kuwo lyangu liwaha, kuli makungugo gose gono gapilime yisi ya Isilayeli, na ko muno kuli yisi ya Edomu. Wono waketemya mu yisi yangu yiwe wutemi wawo, so nyemo ye zinhumbula zawo, wakahasola ne hanhu ho wudimilo wayo.
6 Holeka luno, wuyiwililaje yisi ya Isilayeli, wugalonjele matunda ne vigongo, ne mivwilo ye malenga, hamonga ne malolo gose, wuvilonjele zinghani zono ane no Muwaha Mulungu nghuzilonjela mwi'kuwo lyangu, ku soko ye makungugo gono gali wanhu, kuwaliga, no kuwakoza cevu wanhu wangu.
7 Nane no Muwaha Mulungu, nghwilahiza nha vyono, i makungugo gose gono gayipilime yisi ya Isilayeli, golokozwa cevu.
8 Ninga mucanyha ye matunda ga mu yisi ya Isilayeli, i mibici yolotula kanyhi mahamha, no kumulelelenyi mikalo, anye nyo wanhu wa Isilayeli. Hambi nha mweza behi ku wuze kono kwenyu.
9 Na langa, ta! Ane nili ku lubavu lwenyu, nane nghusaka sunga nize mbone yisi yenyu yikulimwa, no kuhadikwa mbeyu.
10 Nane nolokonjeza liwalo lyenyu muwe wenji muno. Molokwikala mu makaya gono gaweza matongo, mugazenje kanyhi i makaya gose gono gamwaguka.
11 Nane nolokonjeleza liwalo lye wanhu, hamonga ne mitugo. Nanye molokwinjipa muwe wenji muno kuciza nha vyono mwali. Kanyhi molotuga wana wenji muno. Nane nolomulecenyi mwikale du mu yisi yiyo, nha vyono mwekalaga katali. Nolomumotyeni kuciza halulongozi. Vyo yakutya u Muwaha Mulungu.
12 Nolomugomolenyi 'nye nyo wanhu wangu Isilayeli, muze mwikale kanyhi mu'si, nayo yolowa yisi yenyu nyo weneco. Nayo yisi hamba si yolowaleka kanyhi i wana wenyu waze wagazice ne lizala.

13 “Ane no Muwaha Mulungu nghukutya nhavi, ‘I wanhu wakukutya agwe go yisi, ati, wuli mulya wanhu, na kanyhi ati weza wuli muwulaji we wanhu we nghungugo wulawa lulu,
14 agwe si kowa kanyhi wuli mulya wanhu, hamba muwulaga nghungugo yako. Vyo nghutya ane no Muwaha Mulungu.
15 Ne yisi si yolohuliciza kanyhi zo wusekwa ne makungugo, hamba kuwawona wanhu wakuyizwajiza kanyhi yisi yenyu. Ne yisi hamba si yolohizizwa kanyhi i wana wayo. Vyo nghutya ane no Muwaha Mulungu.’ ”

I mikalile mipya ye Waisilayeli

16 Muwaha Mulungu yakanonjela yakatya nhavi,
17 “Gwe go munhu, lono Waisilayeli wali wakali wekaye mu yisi yawo, wene wakeyavufya mu nzila ye metumo gawo. Nghagawona metumo gawo gali mavufywe nho mucekulu mono yali mu vilo vyakwe vyo mwezi.
18 Holeka nane nawaleka wawuwone u wukali we likuwo lyangu, ku soko yo wuwulaji wawo wono wetuma mu yisi, na ku soko yo wiyavufye wawo so wuhemba vihwani.
19 Nane nghawalipiciza ku soko ye zinzila zawo zimbi ze metumo gawo. Holeka nghawa kuweyagaziza mu zi'si zinjenzi.
20 Nawo ho hose hono wabita, wakajendelela du kulikoza cevu, litagwa lyangu lyono lyelile. Sunga ne wanhu wakawa kuwatya, ‘Lanjenyi ta! Awa wo wanhu wo Muwaha Mulungu, ninga alu wakondya kuwuka mu yisi yakwe.’
21 Na yiyi nane yo yikuleka nghulikalalila litagwa lyangu lyono lyelile, vyono wene Waisilayeli, i hanhu hose du hono wakubita, wakulikoza cevu litagwa lyangu.

22 “Holeka alu, wuwalonjele wawo Waisilayeli, zono ane no Muwaha Mulungu nghuwalonjela nghutya nhavi, Alu zono nozituma, si nozituma ku soko yenyu anye nyo Waisilayeli, aa ng'o! Langa nozituma ku soko ye litagwa lyangu lyono lyelile, lyono anye mwakondya kulikoza cevu hanhu hose hono mukubita.
23 Ninga nane kolo neza kuwalajila we makungugo zo wuwaha wo wele we litagwa lyangu, i litagwa lyono lyatajililwa cevu muli wene, nawo baho ho womanyila vyono ane yo nene Muwaha Mulungu. Vyo nghutya, ane no Muwaha Mulungu. Ane nomwitumamilenyi nyenye du, ho niwalajile makungugo, nha vyono ane nyelile.
24 Nomuwusenyi 'nye mu wulawa mu ma'si, na mu makungugo gose, no kumugomolenyi sunga ko ku yisi yenyu.
25 Nomumizizenyi malenga maswanu, nimwezenyi mu wavufywe wenyu, ku soko yo wuhemba vihwani wenyu, hamonga ne makani menji du mabi gono gamwavufyajenyi.
26 Nomupenyi nhumbula mhya no muhe mupya mugati yenyu. Nomuwusizenyi nhumbula ye 'bwe yono yisina cihuliko muli nyenye, nomupelenyi nhumbula ye cihuliko.
27 Nane nomutajilenyi muhe wangu mu zinhumbula zenyu, nayo komupelenyi vilungo ho musume kuwutyatya wulajilize wangu, no kugebata malajilizo gangu gose, gono namupelenyi.
28 Nanye ho mosuma kwikala mu yisi yono nawapela i walezi wenyu. Nanye mowa wanhu wangu, nane nowa Mulungu wenyu.
29 Nane nomuponyenyi mu wavu wenyu wose. Nowulajiliza wuhemba wuwe winji muno, ho mulece kutuga nzala hamba hadodo.
30 Kanyhi nociziliza mikalo ye mibici, hamonga no winji we vilimwa vya mu migunda yenyu, ho halece kuwa ne lizala, lyono lyosuma kumupelenyi cevu ha meso ge makungugo.
31 Holeka nanye mokumbucila zinzila zenyu zimbi, ne metumo genyu gono si maswanu, nanye mokwiwona cevu, ku soko yo wubi wenyu hamonga ne mawifyo genyu.
32 Ho yimanyice kuli'nye, anye nyo Waisilayeli, nha vyono ane si nghwituma zizi ku soko yenyu nyenye, aa, ng'o! Langa nghwituma nghani zizi, anye ho mukolwe soni no kuwona cevu ku soko ye makani mabi gono mwali kwituma. Vyo nghutya ane no Muwaha Mulungu.”

33 U Muwaha Mulungu yakutya nhavi, “Nane kolo nakondya kumwezenyi mu wubi wenyu wose, baho nhondo, ho nolomulecenyi mwikale mu makaya genyu kanyhi, mugazenje kanyhi ne matongo gayo.
34 Ne wanhu wose wono wazoweye kukola mu migunda yenyu, wayiwonaga nha vyono yali yisopile ne maselegwa, yawa nha si migunda kanyhi. Ninga ane nomulecenyi kanyhi muyilime.
35 Ne wanhu wose kanyhi wotya, nye, muyilangaje ta yisi ayi yono yali yeza nha mbago, alu kumbe yehwana nhe busitani ya Adeni. Alu mulanje ta, sunga ne makaya gono gali gasakala, gambuka, geza matongo, alu wanhu wekaye, wagazenjela sunga ne magomawakali.
36 Nhondo baho sunga ne wanhu we makungugo wono wakali womi wono wayipilime Isilayeli, nawo womanya vyono ane no Muwaha Mulungu, yo nene mono nigazengaga kanyhi, i makaya gono geza matongo, no kuhadika kanyhi vilimwa mu migunda yono yali yalekwa. Yo nene Muwaha Mulungu mono nakondya kwihuwiza nha vyono nozituma zizi, na mbeka nozituma.”
37 U Muwaha Mulungu yakutya nhavi, “Nane nolowaleka Waisilayeli walombe kanyhi wutazi kuli'ne. Nolowaleka wonjelezece mwi'walo lyawo, wawe wenji nhe 'tewa lye zingholo.
38 Ne makaya nago gono gali geza matongo, nhondo golomema wanhu, nha vyono Yelusalemi baho katali, yali yimemile zingholo, zono wazilavyaga makumbiko mu vilo vye zisikukuu. Nhondo nawo ho wolomanyila vyono ane yo nene Muwaha Mulungu.”


Ezekiyeli 37

I lilolo lye mafupa gono ganyalile

1 Nghaviwona vilungo vyo Muwaha Mulungu, no muhe wakwe wukanhegula kwi'lolo lyono lyali limemile mafupa.
2 Yakanghoza behi nago, yakamhilimya mbavu zose. Nane nghawona, mwali mogali mafupa menji muno muno, kanyhi gose ganyalile.
3 Yakanonjela yakatya, “Gwe go munhu, wule, mafupa gaga gosuma kuwumipa?”

Nane nghapitula nghatya, “Gwe go Muwaha Mulugu, agwe yo gwegwe du mono kozimanya zizo!”

4 Nayo Muwaha Mulungu yakanyitya, “Ale, lu, wugawilile mafupa gago. Wugalonjele mafupa gaga gono ganyalile, gayihulicize i nghani yo Muwaha Mulungu.
5 Wugalonjele nha vyono, ane no Muwaha Mulungu nghugalonjela nghutya nhavi, lanjenyi ta, ane notajila wehele muli'nye nanye mowumipa.
6 Nane nomupelenyi matimba hamonga ne nyama, hanyuma nimugubicenyi ne nghuli mucanyha yayo. Nomutajilenyi wehele muli'nye, nanye mowumipa. Nanye ho momanya vyono, ane yo nene Muwaha Mulungu.”

7 Ale, baho, nane nghawa kuwila nha vyono nalonjelwa. Na lono nawilaga, nghahulika lizi likulila kotya kayamba, ne mafupa gakanza kwilunza yifupa ne 'fupa liyagwe.
8 Na lono nali nghali nanjile, hobaho, i mafupa gakawa kugubikwa ne matimba hamonga ne nyama, na hanyuma yayo, gakawa kugubikwa ne nghuli. Ninga hamba nhavivyo mwali mukali musina wehele mu miwili yiyo.

9 U Mulungu yakanonjela yakatya, “Gwe go munhu, aluno, wuyiwililaje mbeho. Wuyilonjele mbeho, nha vyono u Muwaha Mulungu yakukutya nhavi, alu wuze ku wulawa mu zimbavu zose vyono yine, wukayehelele i miwili yiyi yono yifwiye ho yiwumipe.”

10 Baho nane nghawa kuwila nha vyono nalonjelwa. No wehele wukawa kuyinjila i miwili yiyilya. Ne miwili yikawumipa, wakenuka wakema wima. Nawo wali wenji muno muno, wono wali wakwiliye kunoza lipuga yiwaha lye wasilikale wo wulugu.

11 U Muwaha Mulungu yakanyitya, “Gwe go munhu, i wanhu wa Isilayeli, wehwanile ne mafupa gagano. Hambi wene nha wakutya vyono, cakondya kunyala, nalu ceza cisina witumbilo, kanyhi cafwila lukulu!
12 Holeka wuwawililaje i wanhu wangu wa Isilayeli, wuwalonjele nha vyono, ane no Muwaha Mulungu, novidigula vitenjelo vyawo, niwawuse mumo mu vitenjelo, niwategule sunga ko ku yisi ya Isilayeli.
13 Nane kolo nakondya kuvidigula vitenjelo, mono i wanhu wazicigwe, no kuwalavyaniza kunze, nawo baho ho womanyila vyono, ane yo nene Muwaha Mulungu.
14 Nane notajila wehele muli wene, niwomifye, niwalece wekale mu yisi yawo. Nawo ho womanya vyono, ane yo nene Muwaha Mulungu. Nihuwiza nha vyono nozituma zizi. Na mbeka nozituma. Vyo nghutya ane no Muwaha Mulungu.”

Yuda na Isilayeli mu wutemi wumonga

15 U Muwaha Mulungu yakanonjela kanyhi yakatya nhavi,
16 “Gwe go munhu, wusole cibici cimonga, wucandice mucanyha yayo zinghani azino, ‘U wutemi wa Yuda.’ Na hanyuma kanyhi wusole cibici cinji, naco wucandice mucanyha yayo zinghani azino, ‘U wutemi wa Isilayeli.’
17 Hanyuma wuvisole vibici vivyo vyose vyono vyejete, wuvihanze, wuvibate hamonga ku sonje yavyo, vijele nha kotya cibici cimonga.
18 Na kolo i wanhu wako wakukuwuza wakusaka wamanye nhunizo yayo nyhici.
19 Wuwalonjele nha vyono, ane no Muwaha Mulungu, nocisola cibici cono candicigwe Isilayeli, nicihanze hamonga ne cibici ciyagwe cono candicigwe Yuda. Nane novinoza viwe cibici cimonga nicibate mu muwoko wangu.

20 “Holeka luno, wuvibataje vibici vivyo vyejete mu muwoko wako, i wanhu wakuwone vyono wuvibate.
21 Hanyuma wuwalonjele nha vyono, ane no Muwaha Mulungu, nowakonghola wanhu wangu mu wulawa mu makungugo gose kono weyagaliye. Nowakonghola niwawice hamonga, niwagomole sunga ko ku yisi yawo.
22 Nane nowalunza wose wawe nghungugo monga du mu'si, mu matunda ga Isilayeli. Nawo wolowa na mutemi yumonga, mono kowatema wanhu wose hamonga. Nawo hamba si wolokwigawa kanyhi wawe makungugo mejete, hamba wutemi wejete.
23 Hamba si wolokwiyavufya weneco so wuhemba vihwani vye cevu, hamba kwiyinjiza mu wituma wubi yaye. Nane nolowawusiza lukulu mu zinzila zo wubi, na mu zinzila zono zosuma kuwambuza wangaluce'ne. Noloweza, nawo wolowa wanhu wangu, nane nolowa Mulungu wawo.
24 No mutemi mono yehwanile Dawudi u mwitumami wangu, yuyo yo kolowa mutemi wawo. Nawo wose wolowa wali hamonga hasi yo mutemi monga. Kanyhi wogebata ne malajilizo gangu ko wihuwilwa.

25 “Nawo wolokwikala mu yisi yono namupela mwitumami wangu Yakobo, i yisi yono wekalaga walezi wawo. Nawo wolokwikala mu yisi yiyo cibitilila, wene ne wana wawo, hamonga ne wana we wana wawo. No mutemi mono yehwanile u mwitumami wangu Dawudi, yuyo yo kolotema muli wene cibitilila.
26 Nane nolonoza cilagane co mulamu hamonga nawo, cowa cilagane ce cibitilila. Nowatandaza no kuwonjeleza wonjelezece wawe wenji. Noloyiwika ne Nyumba yangu mu yisi yawo, nayo yolokwikala mumo cibitilila.
27 Nane nolokwikala baho hamonga nawo, Ane nolowa Mulungu wawo, nawo wolowa wanhu wangu.
28 Nane kolo nakondya kuyiwika Nyumba yangu yikale muli wene cibitilila, hobaho i makungugo ho golomanyila vyono, yo nene Muwaha Mulungu, mono niwahaguye i wanhu wa Isilayeli, wawe wanhu wangu.”


Ezekiyeli 38

U mutemi Gogu yakwitumamilwa no Mulungu

1 Muwaha Mulungu yakanonjela yakatya, nhavi,
2 “Gwe go munhu, wumulonjelaje Gogu, u mutemi we zinghungugo za Mesheki na Tubali, mu yisi ya Magogu. Wumulonjele,
3 kanyhi wumumanyize nha vyono ane no Muwaha Mulungu, nili mulugu wakwe.
4 Holeka yamanye nha vyono nomupitula, nimutajile ndobogo mu zinzagasa zakwe, nimulavye nimukwejenhele kutali, hamonga ne mapuga gakwe ge wasilikale. I wasilikale wakwe, hamonga ne zifwalasi, wono si wavwalile myenda ye cisilikale, nawo wowa mhuga mbaha muno. Ne wasilikale wawo wose, wowa wadinghuye hamonga ne vitwalo vyawo, nha cinji, zingula, hamonga ne mimaje yawo.
5 Nawo i wanhu wa Pelisiya na Kushi hamonga na Libiya, wolowa wali hamonga nayo. Na wose wawo wolowa wadinghuye hamonga ne vitwalo vyawo, zingula ne zikofiya ze cuma.
6 Sunga ne wanhu vitung'ati we zi'si za Gomeli na Beti-togalima ku lubavu lwa kusumuka, wawo wose wowa hamonga nayo, hamonga ne wanhu wenji du wono wololawa mu zinghungugo zinji.
7 Holeka wumulonjele nha vyono, yimunojeye yewikanize hamonga ne mapuga gose ge wasilikale, wono wowa wali hasi yo wulindilizi wakwe.
8 Na kolo yakondya kupuluta myaka minji, ane nolomulajiliza, yabite yakayihome yisi, yono i wanhu wowa wakongholwa no kugomolwa hamonga, ku wulawa mu zinghungugo zinji, nawo luno wekaye wapolile wasina kanyhi woga wo wulugu. Kanyhi kologahoma ne matunda ga Isilayeli, gono gali gakondya kulekwa no kutuga wambuce vilo vinji muno, i hanhu hono aluno i wanhu wekaye wapolile.
9 Nayo mutemi Gogu ne lipuga lyakwe lye wasilikale, hamonga ne wanhu wenji wono wololawa mu makungugo menji, woloyihungucila yisi nhe mbeho mbaha yo mukukunzo, kanyhi wolotandala mu yisi yose nhe mavunde.”

10 Ne zinghani zizi, zo Muwaha Mulungu yakumulonjela Gogu yakutya, “Ne mapango gago kolo gakondya kuza, agwe Gogu kolokwanza kwihwesa zo wuhile wubi du mu nhumbula yako.
11 Hambi nha kolowa kwihwesa zo kuzihoma du, i zi'si zono zisina vilungo, zono i wanhu wayo wekaye wapolile, mu makaya gono gasina magomawakali, hamba milango.
12 Koloviteka vinhu hamonga ne wanhu wayo, wono wekaye mu makaya gono katali, gali matongo du. Wono wakondya kwikonghola ku wulawa mu makungugo. Nalu wakondya no kusawa mitugo ne zisawo zinji, nalu wekaye haciligati ye yinze.
13 Ne wanhu wanya Sheba na Dedani, hamonga ne wasuluza wa mu vitumbi vya kutakama ya Hisipaniya, wolokuwuza wawe kutya, ‘Alu mwene 'gwe Gogu, kuza akuno hamonga ne mapuga gako ge wasilikale ho wutece mateka lo? Yikwijela nha vyono wuhile wako, wutuje fweza, na zahabu, ne mitugo, hamonga ne zisawo zinji zose, wuvikunyhe wubite navyo vyose.’ ”

14 Hobaho u Muwaha Mulungu yakawa kunhegula, mbite nghamulonjele Gogu zose zono yasakaga yamulonjele. Nane nghamulonjela Gogu vyono u Muwaha Mulungu yakutya nhavi, “Alu vyono i wanhu wangu wekaye wapolile, nagwe lu wubite
15 ku wulawa ku yisi yako, ku lubavu lwa kusumuka kutali, wubite hamonga ne lipuga yiwaha muno lye wasilikale vitung'ati, wono wapandile fwalasi, wono wakulawa mu zinghungugo zose.
16 Nanye mowahoma i wanhu wangu Isilayeli, nhe libeho liwaha lyo mukukunzo lyono likudumula yisi. Na kolo lipango lyakondya kuza, ane nokutegula wukayihome yisi yangu, ho wuwamanyize we makungugo, wamanye vyono nene nani, na kanyhi wuwamanyize zo wele wangu, mu nzila yono nghwitumila muli'gwe.
17 Hambi agwe Gogu, nha yo gwegwe mono nalongaga i zako ku wulawa katali, mu nzila ye wetumami wangu i wawizi wose we Waisilayeli, na vyono na mu vilo vyono vikuza, nokwenda nize ndete munhu yayihome Isilayeli.” Vyo yakutya u Muwaha Mulungu.

Gogu yakulipicizwa no Mulungu

18 U Muwaha Mulungu yakutya, “Mwi'zuwa lyono Gogu kolowahoma we yisi ya Isilayeli, nane nolowa mukali muno muno.
19 Nane nghulonjela mwi'kuwo lyangu likali, nha vyono, mwi'zuwa lilyo, holowa ne 'tulo yiwaha muno mu yisi ya Isilayeli.
20 Ne zisomba zose, ne zindeje ne zinimu zose zimbaha ne zindodo, sunga ne wanhu wose du wono wekaye mono'nze, wolocicima so woga, ha meso gangu. Ne matunda nago gologwa hasi, na hono henuce, holotenjelemulwa, ne majinji gose gologwa hasi.
21 Nane nolomogofya Gogu, ane no Muwaha Mulungu. Ne wanhu wakwe wolokwipitucila wawe kwihoma ne mimaje yawo weneco wose munhu na muyagwe.
22 Nane nolomulipiciziza Gogu mu nzila ye matamwa na mu ma'fwa. Nolotonyeza mvula yono yine 'beho na ye mabwe mawaha muno muno, no moto, ne cibiliti, muli mwene ne lipuga lye wasilikale wakwe, hamonga ne makungugo gose, gono gowa gali hamonga nayo.
23 Na mu nzila yiyo, mo nowamanyiziza we zinghungugo zose nha vyono, ane yo nene Muwaha Mulungu, kanyhi nelile. Nawo nhondo, baho ho womanyila vyono, ane yo nene Muwaha Mulungu.”


Ezekiyeli 39

Gogu yakusumwa

1 Muwaha Mulungu yakanonjela yakatya nhavi, “Gwe go munhu! Wumulonjelaje Gogu, u mutemi we zinghungugo za Mesheki na Tubali, wumumanyize Gogu nha vyono, ane nili mulugu wakwe.
2 Nomupitusa, nimulongoze, nimusole ku wulawa ku lubavu lwa kusumuka kutali, nimutegule sunga ko ku matunda ga Isilayeli.
3 Nane nowutoweza wupinde wakwe mu muwoko wakwe wo kucekulu wugwe hasi, ne zisonga zakwe, nozitoweza mu muwoko wakwe wo kulume, nazo zigwe hasi.
4 Nayo Gogu, ne lipuga lyakwe lye wasilikale, hamonga na wose wono wali kumutaza, wogwa hasi mu matunda ga Isilayeli wakondya kufwa wose. Ne mivimba yawo, nozilecela zindeje ziyilye, hamonga ne malimu ga mu mbago.
5 Nawo wogwa mwi'hala wafwiye. Vyo nghutya ane no Muwaha Mulungu.
6 Nolokwasa moto mu yisi ya Magogu, na mu mhwani yose, mono wanhu wekalaga wasina magaziko. Ne wanhu wose nhondo, ho womanya vyono ane yo nene Muwaha Mulungu.
7 Nane nolinoza litagwa lyangu lyono lyelile, liwe limanyice mu wanhu wangu Waisilayeli. Hamba si nololileka kanyhi litagwa lyangu likozwe cevu yaye. Na mu nzila yiyo, i wanhu we makungugo, ho wolomanyila vyono ane yo nene Muwaha Mulungu, Ane nili Mulungu mono nelile we Waisilayeli.”

8 Muwaha Mulungu yakutya nhavi, “Ne lizuwa lyono nalonga, lyeza behi kuza.
9 Ne wanhu wono wekaye mu makaya ga Isilayeli, wololawaniza kunze, wavikonghole vitwalo vyono vilecigwe, ho viwe ngwagu. Wolozisola zingula no wupinde, ne zisonga, ne migoha, hamonga ne zinhumbi, wazipemhele mu moto. Navyo vivyo, vyowakwila kuwa ngwagu zono zokwila myaka mupunghati.
10 Nawo wolowa wasina wanjile wa ngwagu, kanyhi wolowa wasina soko yo kutenya ngwagu mwi'hala, hamba kutema mabici mu mbago, hambi nha wowa wakondya kutuga vitwalo vyono vilecigwe, navyo vyo vyolowakwila so ngwagu. Nawo wolowa teka i wanhu wono wawateka wene, wawasolele ne vinhu vyawo.” Vyo yakutya, u Muwaha Mulungu.

I maziko ga Gogu

11 U Muwaha Mulungu yakatya, “Kolo zizi zikwijela, nane nolomupela Gogu ciwanza, hono kowa kuzika i wafu kuko mu yisi ya Isilayeli, mwi'lolo lya wono wakukola lya ku lubavu lwa kucilima, ye lilenga lyo Mwino. Nayo Gogu mweneco, hamonga ne Wasilikale wakwe, wolozikwa kuko, ne lilolo lilyo, lyolocemwa, ‘I lilolo lye wasilikale wa Gogu.’
12 Waisilayeli wolowa wena mulimo wo kuzika i mivimba myezi mupunghati, no kuyiwika kanyhi yisi yiwe yelile.
13 Ne wanhu wose wa mu yisi, wolowataza Waisilayeli mulimo wuwo wo kuzika mivimba, nawo wolopegwa lilumbo ku soko yo mulimo wuwo, mwi'zuwa lyangu lyo wusume. Vyo nghutya ane no Muwaha Mulungu.
14 Na kolo yeza kupuluta i myezi yiyo mupunghati, wolohagulwa wanhu wanji, wayipilime yisi yose, walanje i mivimba yono yowa yikali yisigaye yigwiye hasi, wayizice, ho wayezeze lukulu yisi.
15 Na lono wowa kubita ako na ako, mu yisi yose, na hono wawone mafupa ga munhu gali hasi, ale, wawicile baho citanjilo, i wazika wanhu ho wasume kuhawona, no kugasola mafupa gaga, wabite wakagazice mwi'lolo lye wasilikale wa Gogu.
16 (Behi na baho, howa na cikaya cono cocemelwa litagwa lilyo lye Lilolo lye wasilikale wa Gogu.) Na mu nzila yiyo, yisi yowa yakondya kwezwa kanyhi.”

17 Muwaha Mulungu yakanonjela kanyhi yakatya, “Gwe go munhu, alu zicemaje zindeje zose hamonga ne zinimu zose ku wulawa hanhu hose, zilye likumbiko lyono nghuziwikaniziza. Yolowa ndigwa nyinji mu matunda ga Isilayeli, hono zosuma kulya nyama, zing'we sunga ne sakami.
18 Nazo zolya i miwili ye wasilikale, no kung'wa i sakami ye wawaha wa mono'nze. Na wawo wose wowa kuwulagwa nha vyono ziwulagwaga zingholo, hamba zinyana ze zingholo, hamba zimhene, hamba zing'ombe zono zijinile muno.
19 Nane lono neza kuwawulaga i wanhu wawa wose nhe makumbiko, nazo zindeje hamonga ne zinimu, zolya nyama zono zijinile, mu nghango nha vyono zosuma zeneco. Kanyhi zoying'wa sakami sunga zigale.
20 Na mu meza yangu, nolozitenjela nyama ye zifwalasi na ye wapanda fwalasi, hamonga na ye wasilikale vitung'ati, hamonga ne wanhu wose wo wulugu. Vyo yakutya u Muwaha Mulungu.”

Wuwuye we Waisilayeli kuwulawa ku wuhamywe

21 U Muwaha Mulungu yakatya, “Ane nowanoza i wanhu we makungugo, wayiwone nhojo yangu, niwalajile nha vyono nosuma kuwufisiliza wuhile wangu kwe ling'ani.
22 Ne Waisilayeli nawo ku wulawa lulo, ho wolomanyila nha vyono ane yo nene Muwaha Mulungu, u Mulungu wawo.
23 Sunga ne makungugo gose ho womanya, nha vyono i Waisilayeli wahamywa ku soko yo wubi wono wasutizya'ne. Na vyono nane nghawaleka, ne walugu wawo wakawasuma no kuwawulaga mu wulugu.
24 Nane nghawalipiciza nha vyono yiwanojeye ha nghango ye masutya gawo no wubi wawo. Hodu nghawa kuwaleka.”

25 U Muwaha Mulungu yakatya, “Ninga aluno hodu, nowawonela lusungu i wana wa Yakobo, i wanhu wa Isilayeli, niwamotye kanyhi. Hambi nha nghuliwonela wivu i litagwa lyangu lyono lyelile.
26 Nawo kolo wekala wapolile mu yisi yawo, na hawe hasina kanyhi mono kowogofya, baho nhondo ho wolosemwela lukulu ze cevu cawo, na vyono wali wangaluka.
27 Nane lu nolowagomola wanhu wangu ku wulawa mu ma'si gono wekalaga i walugu wawo, ho niwalajile makungugo menji, nha vyono ane nelile.
28 Nawo i wanhu wangu ho wolomanya nha vyono ane yo nene Muwaha Mulungu, u Mulungu wawo. Nawo walosuma kuzimanya zizi, ku soko yo nene mono nawehamya wabite ku wuwanda, kuli we makungugo. Kanyhi yo nene mono nghuwakonghola no kuwenjiza kanyhi mu yisi yawo weneco. Na hamba si nomuleka yunji wo wose yaye, yasigale kuko.
29 Nane nolowetila zo muhe wangu i wanhu wangu Waisilayeli, kanyhi hamba si nolowalecela lukulu ng'o. Vyo nghutya, ane no Muwaha Mulungu.”


Ezekiyeli 40

U WUGUBULILWE WE NYUMBA YO MULUNGU YA HANYUMA

(40:1–48:35)

Ezekiyeli yakusolwa no kutegulwa ku Yelusalemi

1 Na mwi'zuwa lye likumi, lya mu mwamulizo wo mwaka, mu mwaka we makumi mejete ne'hano, wa mu mapango gono cali cakondya kuhamywa. Na mu mwaka we likumi na yine, ku wulawa lono Yelusalemi yatecelwa. Ale, mwi'zuwa lilyo, nghaviwona vilungo vyo Muwaha Mulungu, yakandeta kuko.
2 Na mu wugubulilwe wangu, u Mulungu yasola, yakanhegula ku yisi ya Isilayeli, yakambika ku cizuli ce litunda litali muno muno. Na ha mwande yangu nghawona makaya menji, gono gejelaga nhe kaya yiwaha.
3 Yakasola yakasejeleza ho behi. Baho, nghamuwona munhu mono yang'alalaga nha shaba. Munhu yuyo, yali yemile behi no mulango, yebate mu muwoko wakwe cipimo co kupimila ca citambala ca bafuta, hamonga ne nghome yo kujelela,
4 Hanyuma yakanonjela yakatya, “Gwe go munhu, alu ta, wulanje kwe meso gako, wuzikamise muno muno mu nhumbula yako zose zono nghukulajila. Hambi zizi nha zo zilecile agwe nghukuleta baha. Hambi nha wowa wo mulimo wako wo kuwalonjela i wanhu wa Isilayeli nghani zose zono koziwona bahano.”

U mulango wa kucilima

5 Nane nali neza kulanga, nghawona Nyumba yo Mulungu, nzenjelwe lijinji, lipilime mbavu zose. U munhu yuyo, yakayisola nghome yakwe yo kujelela, ne nghome yiyo, yali yina wutali wa mita yidatu, yakalipima lijinji. Nalyo lijinji likawa lina wutali wo kubita kucanyha, mita yidatu, no wugazi wayo, mita yidatu nhavivilya.
6 Hanyuma kanyhi yakawa kubita ku mulango wono wali wulanjile kucilima yakapanda zingazi sunga ha kacanyha, na baho ha kacanyha, yakawupima u mulango zifulemu zayo.
7 Na ku mhilimo yayo, kwali na cipuluto, cono cali cina nyumba yidatu zo wulindilizi mbavu zose. Ne zinyumba zizo zose, zali zina wugazi no wutali wa mita yidatu yidatu mbavu zose. Ne cilejeho co wulawa nyumba na nyumba, cali ca wugazi wa mita nyejete na nusu. Na ku mhilimo ye zinyumba zizo, kwali kanyhi na cipuluto cono cali no wugazi wa mita yidatu, naco cali co cipuluto cono cali cibitile sunga ko ku nyumba yono yali vidomile ne Nyumba yo Mulungu.
8 Nayo u munhu yuyo, yakabita yakayipima nyumba yiyo. Yakayiwona yina wutali wa mita yine.
9 Ne nyumba yiyo yali yili ku ndumilizo yo mulango wono wali wuli behi ne Nyumba yo Mulungu. Na ha mwande hadodo, hali holili yijinji lyono lyali lina wugazi wa mita monga.
10 Ne zinyumba zizi zose zo wulindilizi, zono zali nzengwe ku mhelo ayi na ayi ye cipuluto, zose zali ziling'aniye u wuwaha wayo mbavu zose. Ne majinji gayo gose gali gena wukangazi wono wiling'aniye mose.

11 Hanyuma yayo u munhu yuyo, yakawupima wugazi we cipuluto co kwinjilila ha mulango, naco cali cina wugazi wa mita mutandatu na nusu. No wutali wo mulango, wali mita yihano.
12 Na ha mwande ye zinyumba zo wulindilizi, hali holili yibupa lyo wutali wa centimita makumi mahano, no wukangazi wayo centimita makumi mahano nhavivilya. Ne zinyumba zizo, u wugazi wa mugati, zose zali ziling'aniye, zali zina wugazi wa mita yidatu yidatu kose kose.
13 Hanyuma kanyhi yakawupima u wutali we cilejeho co wulawa mwi'jinji lye nyumba ayi, sunga kwi'jinji lye nyumba nzagwe, yono yili ku nhumbi ye cipuluto co kwinjilila ha mulango, naco cilejeho cico, cali cina mita yikumi na nyejete wugazi wayo.
14 Yakayipima kanyhi ne nyumba yono yali yili mu nzila, yono yinjiye mwi'kumbo, nayo yikawa yina wugazi wa mita yikumi.
15 No wutali we nzila yono yinjiye ku mulango, ku wulawa mwi'jinji lya kunze, lyo mulango we nyumba ye ndumilizo, wali mita makumi mejete ne'hano.
16 Hali ho gali madilisha mu majinji ga kunze ge zinyumba zizo, hamonga na mu majinji ga mugati ye zinyumba. Kanyhi hali na mitende mikakatwe mu majinji ga mugati gono gali vidomile ne cipuluto.

I likumbo lya kunze

17 Ale, hobaho u munhu yuyo, yakawa kundeta sunga ko kwi'kumbo lya kunze. Na kuko, nghawona kwali ko zili nyumba zinji zono zali zizenjigwe, mhapicizwe mwi'jinji lya kunze. Na ha mwande yazo, hali na hanhu hono hali hasindiywe mabwe hasi,
18 gono gali matandazwe hadeha, gapilime likumbo. Ne likumbo lilino lya kunze, lyali lizenjigwe hasika muno si nhe likumbo lya mugati.

19 Kanyhi hali ho wuli mulango wunji wono wali wuli ha kacanyha muno, wono wali wo wokwinjilila kwi'kumbo lya mugati. U munhu yuyo, yakawupima u wutali wo wulawa ku mulango awu, sunga ko ku mulango muyagwe, yikawa wutali wa mita makumi mahano.

U mulango wa ku lubavu lwa kusumuka

20 Hanyuma u munhu yuyo, yakawupima mulango wo lubavu lwa kusumuka wo kwinjilila ku luwa kwa ya kunze.
21 Ne zinyumba zose yidatu yidatu zo wulindilizi, zono zali zizenjigwe mbavu zose ze cipuluto, hamonga ne majinji gayo, sunga ne balaza yo kwambila lubeho, vyali vihwanile vyose na vya ku mulango mulongozi wakulubavu lwa kucilima. No wutali wo mulango, wali mita makumi mejete ne 'hano, no wugazi wayo, mita yikumi na nyejete na nusu.
22 Balaza yo kwambila lubeho, hamonga ne madilisha, ne mitende yayo mikakatwe, vyose vyali vihwanile du na vya ku mulango wa ku lubavu lwa kucilima. Langa hali ho zili, ngazi mupunghati, zono zali zibitile ku mulango. Ne balaza yo kwambila lubeho, yali yili ku ndumilizo yayo vidomile ne luwa ye kaya.
23 Na lilyo likumbo ne luwa yiyo ya kugati, yali yina mulango wono wudomile ku mulango we kejete, ku lubavu lwa kusumuka, wihwanile no mulango wo lubavu lwa kucilima. Nayo u munhu yuyo, yakawupima wutali wo wulawa ku mulango awu, sunga ko ku mulango muyagwe, yakatuga wutali wa mita makumi mahano.

U mulango wa ku lubavu lwa kutakama

24 Hanyuma kanyhi u munhu yuyo yakasola, yakanhegula ku lubavu lwa kutakama, nako kuko, nghawona mulango wunji kanyhi. Nayo yakagapima i majinji gayo ga mugati, hamonga ne balaza yayo yo kwambila lubeho, navyo vyose vyali viling'aniye du nhe viyawo.
25 Mwali mogali ne madilisha, mu zinyumba zizi za kono mulango wuwu, gono gehwanile na gono gali gali mu milango yinji yose. No wutali wo mulango wuwo, wali wuficile mita makumi mejete na yihano, no wugazi wayo, mita yikumi na nyejete na nusu.
26 Hali ho zili ngazi mupunghati zono zali zibitile ku mulango wuwo, ne balaza yayo yo kwambila lubeho, nayo yali yili ku ndumilizo, vidomile ne luwa. Baho naho, hali ho yili mitende mikakatwe mu majinji ga mugati, gono vyali vidomile ne cipuluto.
27 Na baha naho, hali ho wuli mulango wono wubitile ku luwa ya mugati. Nayo munhu yuyo, yakawupima wutali wo wulawa ku mulango mulongozi, sunga ku mulango wuwu we kejete, nawo wukawa wutali wa mita makumi mahano.

Luwa ya mugati, no mulango wo lubavu lwa kutakama

28 U munhu yuyo, yakasola kanyhi, yakanghoza ku mulango wo lubavu lwa kutakama, yakanyinjiza mu luwa ya mugati. Yakawupima mulango, nawo i vipimo vyayo, vikawa viling'aniye du ne vipimo vye milango yinji yose ya mwi'jinji lya kunze.
29 Ne zinyumba zayo zo wulindilizi, ne balaza yayo yo kwambila lubeho, hamonga ne majinji ga mugati, vyose vyali cipimo cimonga du na vye milango yinji yose.
30 Na mu zinyumba za ku mulango wuwu, nazo zali zina ne madilisha gayo nha vivilya. No wutali wayo, wali wuficile mita makumi mejete ne 'hano, no wugazi wayo wali mita yikumi na nyejete na nusu.
31 Ne balaza yayo yo kwambila lubeho, yali vidomile ne luwa, ne mitende yono yali mikakatwe mu majinji ga mu cipuluto. Na hali ho zili ngazi munana zono zali zibitile ku mulango wuwu.

Luwa ya mugati, no mulango wa ku lubavu lwa kucilima

32 Hanyuma kanyhi, u munhu yuyo, yakasola yakanghoza ku mulango wa ku lubavu lwa kucilima, yakanyinjiza mu luwa ya mugati. Nayo yakawupima mulango, nawo wukawa wiling'aniye vipimo vyayo ne milango yinji yose.
33 Nha cinji, i zinyumba zayo zo wulindilizi, ne balaza yayo yo kwambila lubeho, hamonga ne majinji gayo ga mugati, vipimo vyayo vyose, vikawa viling'aniye ne vipimo vya mu milango yinji yose. Hali ho gali madilisha gapilime, sunga na mu balaza yo kwambila lubeho nhavivilya. No wutali wayo wali wuficile mita makumi mejete ne'hano, no wugazi wayo, nawo wali wuficile mita yikumi na nyejete na nusu.
34 Nayo balaza yo kwambila lubeho, yali vidomile ne luwa ya mugati. Nayo mitende, yali mikakatwe mu majinji ga mu cipuluto. Na hali ho zili ngazi munana zono zali zibitile ku mulango wuwu.

Luwa ya mugati, no mulango wa ku lubavu lwa kusumuka

35 Na hanyuma kanyhi u munhu yuyo yakawa kusola, yakanhegula ku mulango wo lubavu lwa kusumuka. Nayo yakawupima mulango, nawo wukawa viling'aniye cipimo cayo ne milango yinji yose.
36 Nako kuko, kwali ko zili zinyumba zo wulindilizi, ne majinji gayo makakatwe, ne balaza yayo yo kwambila lubeho, hamonga ne madilisha gapilime mose. Ne vipimo vyayo vyose, vikawa viling'aniye du ne vipimo vye milango yinji yose. Nha cinji wutali wayo wali wuficile mita makumi mejete ne'hano, no wugazi wayo wali mita yikumi na nyejete na nusu.
37 Ne balaza yayo yo kwambila lubeho, yali vidomile ne luwa ya mugati. Nayo mibici ye mitende, yali mikakatwe mu majinji gose ga mu cipuluto. Kanyhi hali hozili ngazi munana zono zali zibitile ku mulango wuwu.

I mazengo ge zinyumba, zono zali nzenje ku mulango wa ku lubavu lwa kusumuka

38 Na ku luwa ya kunze, hali ho zili nyumba zinji nzengwe zipapicizwe mwi'jinji ku lubavu lwa kusumuka yo mulango. Ne milango yayo, yadigulilwaga mugati ye balaza yo kwambila lubeho, yono yali vidomile ne luwa. Baho ho wazikwemelaga zi mitugo ze makumbiko ge vilavywa vinyanywe.
39 Na ha balaza yo kwambila lubeho, hali ho zili meza yine. Nazo zali mbice nyejete ku lubavu aluno, ne zinyejete nazo, ku lubavu aluno baho ha balaza. Nawo, mu zi meza zizo, mo waziwulajilaga zimitugo ze vilavywa vye makumbiko, hamba ze vilavywa vinyanywe, hamba ze vilavywa so wubi, hamba ze vilavywa vyo mulamu.
40 Na kunze ye balaza yiyo, hali ho zili naho meza yine, zono zihwanile zinyawo. Nazo zali mbice ku mulango wo lubavu lwa kusumuka, nyejete ku lubavu aluno lo mulango, ne zinyejete nazo ku lubavu luyagwe lo mulango.
41 Nazo hamonga zali meza munana, zono u mulimo wazo, wali wo kuziwulajila zimitugo zono zalavywaga makumbiko. Nazo zali mbice meza yine, kugati ye balaza yo kwambila lubeho, ne zi yine nazo, zali mbice hanze mugati ye luwa.
42 Kanyhi hali na meza yine zinji za mabwe maseswe, zono zali mbice mugati ye zinyumba, zono wali wazenjile wazipapicize mwi'jinji lyo mulango wa mugati. Ne zimeza zizo, zali no wutali wa Sentimita makumi mahano, na mucanyha yazo, zali na wugazi wa sentimia makumi mupunghati na yihano kose kose. Ne zimeza zizo, wazitumamilaga mulimo wo kunozelezeza i vilavywa vinyanywe. Ne vitumamilwa vyose vyono wetumamilaga mulimo wo kuwulajila mitugo ye makumbiko, vyose vyawikwaga mucanyha ye zimeza zizo.
43 Ne zindobogo zali nhumbice mwi'jinji zipilime zimeza zizo. Ne nyama yose yono walavyaga mu makumbiko, wayijelekaga mu zimeza zizo.

44 Hanyuma kanyhi, u munhu yuyo, yakanhegula mu luwa ya mugati. Na mumo mwali na nyumba nyejete, mumo mu luwa, ne milango yayo yali yili mwazi. U mulango we nyumba monga, wali wulanjile ku lubavu lwa kutakama, vidomile no mulango wo lubavu lwe sumuka. Nawo mulango we nyumba nzagwe, wulanjile ku lubavu lwa kusumuka, vidomile no mulango wa ku lubavu lwa kutakama.
45 U munhu yuyo yakanonjela nha vyono, i nyumba yo mulango wono wulanjile kutakama, yo nyumba ye wakohani wono wetumaga mu Nyumba yo Mulungu,
46 ne nyumba yo mulango wono wulanjile ku lubavu lwa kusumuka, yo nyumba ye wakohani wono wetumaga ha cikumbicilo. Ne wakohani wawa, wose wo wana wa Zadoki, wa mu mhuga ya Lawi, nawo wo wali wakomboligwe du, wamwitumamile u Muwaha Mulungu.

Luwa ya mugati, ne lizengo lye Nyumba yo Mulungu

47 Hanyuma u munhu yuyo, yakayipima luwa ya mugati, nayo yikawa no wugazi wa mita makumi mahano mahano, kose kose. Nyumba yo Mulungu yali yili ku lubavu lwa kumwezi, na ha mwande yayo, ho hali ho cili cikumbicilo.
48 Hanyuma kanyhi yakasola yakawa kunyinjiza mu balaza ye Nyumba yo Mulungu. Yakawupima mulango, nawo wukawa wina wutali wa mita nyejete na nusu, u wugazi wayo mita mupunghati. Nago majinji go mulango, gali gena wukangazi wa mita monga na nusu mbavu zose.
49 Naho baho, hali hozili ngazi zono zibitile ku balaza yo kwambila lubeho, zono zali zina wugazi wa mita yikumi no wukangazi wayo, mita mutandatu. Na ha mulango, hali ho gali masumbili mejete, nago gali mahimbile ha mulango, limonga ku mhelo ayo, ne liyagwe ku mhelo ayino yo mulango.


Ezekiyeli 41

1 Hanyuma u munhu yuyo kanyhi, yakawa kusola, yakanyinjiza mu nyumba ya ha ciligati, yono yikucemwa, i Hanhu Hono Helile. Yakawa kuhapima Hanhu Hono Helile. Naco cipuluto, hakawa no wutali wa mita yidatu ku wubita hasi,
2 na mita yihano u wugazi wayo. Naco cipuluto cali cizenjeligwe majinji akuno na akuno, mbavu zose, gono gali gena wukangazi wa mita nyejete na nusu. Hanyuma kanyhi, yakayipima ne nyumba yeneco, nayo yikawa yina wutali wa mita makumi mejete, no wugazi wayo, mita yikumi.

3 Hanyuma kanyhi yakenjila ku nyumba ya mugati muno muno. Yakacipima cipuluto cico cono cibitile mu Nyumba yo Mulungu. Naco cali cina wutali wa mita monga du ku wubita hasi, no wugazi wa mita yidatu. Naco cali cizenjeywe kose kose, i majinji gono gena wuwaha wa mita yidatu na nusu.
4 Hanyuma kanyhi yakayipima nyumba yeneco. Nayo yikawa yina mita yikumi mugati mbavu zose. Ne nyumba yiyi, yali yili ku mwande ye nyumba ya haciligati. Na hanyuma u munhu yuyo yakanyitya, “I Hanhu bahano, ho Hanhu Hono Helile Helile muno.”

I zikaya zono zali nzengwe mhapicizwe ne majinji ge Nyumba yo Mulungu

5 U munhu yuyo, yakalipima lijinji lye Nyumba yo Mulungu, yawone wugazi wayo. Nalyo lyali lina wugazi wa mita yidatu. Na mwi'jinji lilyo lyose lyono lipilime Nyumba yo Mulungu, mwali movili vinyumba vidodo vidodo, vyono vyali vina wugazi wa mita nyejete nyejete, mugati yavyo.
6 Ne vinyumba vivyo, vyali vizenjigwe vina golofa yidatu yidatu. Na golofa zose zali zina vinyumba makumi madatu madatu. Ne lijinji lya kunze lye Nyumba yo Mulungu, lyali lizenjigwe mu nzila yo wisimajile wo wukangazi we lijinji lyayo mu zigolofa zose. Nha cinji, lijinji lyo musinji, lyali likangaye muno, ne golofa yono yikatyatya, lijinji likacepulwa wukangazi wayo, nhavivi hadodo hadodo sunga ko ku golofa ye kadatu ya kucanyha. Wazenga nhavivyo vinyumba vivyo ho vituje wupapicizwe, ninga vilece kwilunza ne lijinji lye Nyumba.
7 Ninga hono wukugalanga majinji ge Nyumba yo Mulungu, kudeha yayo, gejelaga gose nha gena wukangazi wono wiling'aniye sunga ko kucanyha yayo. Kanyhi mumo mwi'jinji lya kunze lye Nyumba yo Mulungu, hali hazenjigwe ngazi nyejete kunze ye vinyumba, zono zetumamilwaga ku wulawa hasi no kubita ku golofa ya hagati, sunga ko ku golofa za kucanyha kundumilizo.

8 Nane nghawona vyono Nyumba yo Mulungu yali yina sakafu yono yali yizenjigwe yinuligwe hacanyha hadodo yipilime Nyumba yo Mulungu, nayo sakafu yiyo wali wo musinji we zigolofa zizilya zidatu zono zali mhapicizwe mu Nyumba yo Mulungu. No wutali wo musinji we zigolofa zizo, vyali viling'aniye no wutali wo musinji we zigolofa zizo, vyali viling'aniye no wutali wo mulejehe wo kupimila nha cinji mita yidatu.
9 Wukangazi we majinji ga kunze ye vinyumba vya mugati, wali mita nyejete na nusu. Ne cilejeho cono casigala, cali cilejeho ce vinyumba vya mu mhelo, vyono vyali vipilime Nyumba yo Mulungu,
10 ne zinyumba ze walindilizi, zali zina wugazi wa mita yikumi ku wupilime Nyumba yo Mulungu mbavu zose.
11 Ne zinyumba zizo zose zali zina mulango wumonga wumonga wono wali wulanjile ku lubavu lwa kusumuka lwe Nyumba yo Mulungu, no mulango wunji kanyhi wumonga, wali wulanjile ku lubavu lwa kutakama. Kanyhi nghawona i hanhu hono wahanozize nha balaza, hakawa hatendemaye viswanu, hono hali hapilimwe Nyumba yose yo Mulungu, naho hali hena wugazi wa mita nyejete na nusu ku wulawa mwijinji lye Nyumba yo Mulngu. No muhangha wono wali wawutenjelemuye, wali we lundo lya wutali wa mita yidatu ku wulawa hasi yayo, wukawa wiling'aniye ku wulawa hasi, no wutali wo musinji we Nyumba yo Mulungu. Na haciligati ye balaza yiyo ne mazengo gono wekalaga i wakohai, hali hocili cilejeho co wugazi wa mita yikumi ku wulawa mu majinji ge Nyumba yo Mulungu.

I lizengo lya ku lubavu lwa kumwezi

12 Na ha mwande hadodo, ku lubavu lwa kumwezi lwe Nyumba yo Mulungu, hali ho lili yikaya yiwaha, lyo wutali wa mita makumi mane ne'hano no wugazi wayo, wali mita makumi madatu ne 'hano. Ne majinji galyo gose, gali gena wukangazi wa mita nyejete na nusu.

I vipimo vyose vye lizengo lye Nyumba yo Mulungu

13 Hanyuma kanyhi u munhu yuyo, yakayipima i Nyumba yo Mulungu, ku lubavu lwa kunze, nayo yikawa no wugazi wa mita makumi mahano. Na ku mhilimo ye Nyumba yo Mulungu, u wugazi we cilejeho cicilya, ku wulawa mwi'jinji lye Nyumba yo Mulungu, sunga ko kwi'zengo lyono lyali lili cidaha hadodo, ku lubavu lwa kumwezi, wugazi wali mita makumi mahano nhavivilya.
14 Kanyhi no wugazi we ciwanza ce Nyumba yo Mulungu, hono wuhanze no wugazi we cilejeho cicilya, wali wuficile wugazi wa mita makumi mahano mbavu zose.
15 Hanyuma kanyhi yakalipima lizengo ku lubavu lwa kumwezi, yakatuga wutali wa mita makumi mahano nhavivilya hamonga ne vimweleda vya mu mbavu zose.

Lizengo lye Nyumba yo Mulungu vyono lyali linha

I balaza ya mu Nyumba yo Mulungu yono wambilaga lubeho, hamonga ne Hanhu Hono Helile, hamonga ne Hanhu Hono Helile, Helile muno muno,
16 Na mumo mu majinji gose, mwali mutowelwe mbawo swanu muno, ku wulawa hasi, sunga mo mu madilisha. Nago madilisha gakadindwa.
17 Na mugati ye majinji ge Nyumba yo Mulungu, ha kacanyha ye milango, mwali mukakate
18 mibici ye mitende hamonga ne makelubi. Nayo mibici ye mitende yali mikakate vityatizye ne makelubi, nha cinji, mutende, hanyuma yikelubi, mutende, hanyuma yikelubi, vipilime majinji gose, ge nyumba. Nago makelubi gose gali gena wusu wuli hejete hejete.
19 U wusu wono wali wihwanile wusu wo munhu, wali wulanjile ku mubici wo mutende, wo lubavu aluno, no wusu wono wali wihwanile wusu wa simba, nawo wali wulanjile u mubici wo mutende, wo lubavu aluno. Wali wakakate nhavivyo du wapilimye lijinji lyose,
20 ku wulawilila hasi ha sakafu, sunga ha kacanyha ye milango.
21 Nazo zifulemu zo mulango we Hanhu Hono Helile, zali ziling'aniye kose kose mbavu zose.

I cikumbicilo ce mabici

Na ha mwande yo mulango wo kwinjilila i Hanhu Hono Helile Helile muno, hejelaga cinhu cinji cono cali cihwanile nha cikumbicilo ca mabici.
22 Naco cali cina wutali wa mita monga na nusu, no wugazi wa mita monga. Ne mabici ga mu zinhuzi zayo, ne litende lyayo, ne majinji gayo, vyose vyali vinozwe na mabici. Hanyuma munhu yuyo, yakanonjela yakatya, “Ayi yo meza yono yili ha mwande yo Muwaha Mulungu.”

I milango

23 Kanyhi hali ho cili cipuluto cono cali cibitile sunga ko ku Hanhu Hono Helile, ne cipuluto cinji naco, sunga ku Hanhu Hono Helile, Helile muno.
24 Kanyhi hali ho yili milango, ne milango yiyo yadigukaga hejete, akuno na akuno.
25 Kanyhi milango yiyo ye Hanhu Hono Helle, yali mikakate mibici ye mitende hamonga ne makelubi, nha vyono wali wagakakate majinji. Na hadeha yo mulango wo kwinjilila ha balaza yo kwambila lubeho, wali wawiciye mahapa makangazi muno.
26 Na mu mhelo ye balaza yiyo, mwali mo gali madilisha, ne majinji gayo gose, gali makakate mibici ye mitende.


Ezekiyeli 42

I mazengo mejete, behi ne Nyumba yo Mulungu

1 Ale, u munhu yuyo yakasola, yakanhegula sunga ko ku luwa ya kunze, cikabitana nayo sunga ko kwi'zengo lyono lyali lili ku lubavu lwa kusumuka ye Nyumba yo Mulungu, hamba si kwali kutali muno ne lizengo, lyono lyali lili ku ndumilizo, ku lubavu lwa kumwezi ye Nyumba yo Mulungu.
2 Ne lizengo lilyo, lyali lina mulandala wa mita makumi mahano, no wugazi wa mita makumi mejete ne'hano.
3 Ku lubavu lumonga lizengo lilyo, lyali lilanjile ku cimweleda cicilya co wugazi wa mita yikumi, cono cali cidomile ne Nyumba yo Mulungu, no lubavu luyagwe, yali yilanjile kwi 'balangu lye mabwe lya he'kumbo lya hadeha. Ne lizengo lilyo, lyali lizenjigwe lili hadatu, nha cinji, balaza nijeleceze ne balaza yinji hacanyha yayo kali kadatu.
4 Na ku lubavu lwe sumuka lwe lizengo lilyo, kwali ko cili cipuluto co wugazi wa mita yihano, no wutali wa mita makumi mahano, hamonga no winjililo wayo, wali wuli ku lubavu lulo.
5 Nazo zinyumba za ku golofa ye lizengo lilyo, zali finyu muno muno kuciza zinyumba za haciligati yayo. Hambi vimweleda nha vyo vyacepula u wugazi we zinyumba za kucanyha, si nha vya hasi.
6 Ne zinyumba zose, mu golofa zose vyono yidatu, zali njeleceze du, zali zisina masumbili nhe mazengo ganji gose gono gali mazenje mumo mu luwa.
7 Ne lijinji lya kunze ye lizengo lilyo, lyono lyali lizipilime zinyumba zizo, lyali lina wutali wa mita makumi mejete ne'hano.
8 No mulandala we zinumba zono zali zili kudeha ye lijinji, wali wuficile wutali wa mita makumi mejete ne'hano. Ninga mucanyha yayo mwali na nyumba zono zali no wutali wono wiling'aniye no wutali we lizengo lyeneco.
9 Na hasika ye zinyumba zizo, ku lubavu lwa kucilima, kundumilizo ye lizengo, hono lyali lyanzize lijinji lye luwa,
10 baho hali ho wuli mulango wo kwinjilila mu luwa ya kunze.

Kanyhi hali na vinyumba vinji vyono vyali vizenjigwe mucanyha ye lijinji lye luwa ye Nyumba yo Mulungu ku lubavu lwa kutakama, hamba si kwali kutali muno ku wulawa mwi'zengo lya ku lubavu lwa kumwezi ye Nyumba yo Mulungu.
11 Na ha mwande ye zinyumba, hali hocili cipuluto, cono cihwanile acilya cono cili ku lubavu lwa kusumuka. Vyali vihwanile vyose i milango yayo, hamonga no wugazi wayo no wutali wayo.
12 Kanyhi hali ho wuli mulango wunji hasi ye vinyumba, ku lubavu lwa kutakama ye lizengo, ku ndumilizo ya kucilima kono lijinji lyali lyanzize.

13 U munhu yuyo yakanonjela yakatya, “I mazengo gaga gose gelile, hambi i wakohani wono wenjilaga hali Muwaha Mulungu, nha mo wakuwa kulila mumo i vilavywa vyono vyelile. Ku soko vyono i vinyumba vyeneco vyelile, holeka ne wakohani nawo wakuwa kuwika mumo i vilavywa vyono vyelile. I vilavywa vyo wusaje, ne makumbiko ge vilavywa so wubi, hamonga ne vilavywa vyo munhu mweneco mono yetumile lisutya.
14 Na lono i wakohani wakondya kwituma mu Nyumba yo Mulungu, yiwanojeye wavule i vivwalo vyono vyelile vyono wali wavwalile lono wali wali mu mulimo, wavilece mumo mu vinyumba vivyo, ho walawile wabite ku luwa ya kunze. Kanyhi yiwanojeye wavwale vivwalo vinji vyono vinojeye, lono weza kubita kwilajila kuko kunze kono wanhu wekongholelaga.”

I vipimo vye ciwanza ce Nyumba yo Mulungu

15 U munhu yuyo, yali yeza kupima mugati ye luwa ye ciwanza ce Nyumba yo Mulungu, hodu yakawa kunghoza ku mulango wa kucilima, yakawa kupima kunze ye luwa ye ciwanza ce Nyumba yo Mulungu.
16 Yakayisola i nghome yakwe yo kupimila, yakapima ku lubavu lwa kucilima, yakatuga mita magana mejete na makumi mahano.
17 Yakapima kanyhi na ku lubavu lwa kusumuka, na kutakama, na kumwezi
18 na mose mwali muling'aniye du,
19 mwina mita magana mejete mejete, na makumi mahano mahano.
20 Holeka lijinji lyose lyali lina mita magana mejete na makumi mahano mbavu zose. Nalyo lijinji lilyo, lyali lihatemanuye i hanhu hono helile, ne hanhu hono si helile.


Ezekiyeli 43

U Muwaha Mulungu yakwinjila mu Nyumba yakwe

1 Hanyuma yayo u munhu yuyo, yakasola, yakanhegula sunga ko ku mulango wulya, wono wali wulanjile ku lubavu lwa kucilima.
2 Na lanje, nghayiwona nhojo yo Muwaha Mulungu wa Isilayeli, yikuza yikulawilila ku lubavu lwa kucilima. Ne lizi lyo Mulungu likahulikwa likukoloma nho mukukunzo we lilenga. Ne yisi nayo yikang'alala so nhojo yiyo yo Mulungu.
3 No wugubulilwe wuwu, wukawa vihwanile no wugubulilwe wono nawuwona lono Mulungu yayongomezeza Yelusalemi, no wugubulilwe kanyhi wono nawuwona he kolongo lya Kebali. Nane ho baho baho nghagwa hasi wugubama.
4 Nayo i nhojo yo Muwaha Mulungu, yikawa kuza yikakolela mu mulango wa ku lubavu lulo lwa kucilima, yikawa kwinjila mu Nyumba yo Mulungu.

5 Ale baho, u Muhe wo Muwaha Mulungu, yakanyinula, yakanhegula sunga ko kwi'kumbo lya mugati. Nane kuko, nghayiwona Nyumba yo Mulungu yakondya kumemywa no lukumyo lo Muwaha Mulungu.
6 Nayo u munhu yuyulya, yali yemile baho behi nane. Hanyuma nghamuhulika u Muwaha Mulungu, yakulonga nane mu wulawa mu Nyumba yakwe mugati, yakunyitya,
7 “Gwe go munhu, lanje, bahano ho he 'goda lyangu lyo wutemi. Nane nokwikala bahano hamonga ne wanhu wangu Isilayeli, kanyhi nowa mutemi wawo cibitilila. Na ku wulawa lulu, i wanhu wangu Waisilayeli, hamonga ne watemi wawo wose, si wolokwijelela kanyhi kulikoza cevu litagwa lyangu lyono lyelile, so wuhemba kanyhi milungu yinji, hamba kuyizika kanyhi i mivimba ye watemi wawo i hanhu baha.
8 Hambi i watemi wawo, nha wejelela, wakaviwika vipimbilamulango vyawo, hamonga ne zifulemu ze milango ye zikaya zawo zo wutemi, behi muno muno ne vipimbilamulango vyangu, hamonga ne zifulemu ze milango ye Nyumba yangu. Na haciligati yangu na wene, hali ho lili 'jinji lyono lyali licitemanuye. Nawo wakalyavufya litagwa lyangu lyono lyelile ku soko ye makani gawo i mabi gono wetuma. Holeka nane nghawongomezeza mwi'kuwo lyangu.
9 Ninga aluno, yowa yiwanojeye walecele lukulu kuyihemba milungu yinji, kanyhi wayiwuse i mivimba ye watemi wawo yiwe kutali nane. Na hono wanoze nhavivyo, nane, nokwikala muli wene cibitilila.”

10 U Muwaha Mulungu yakajendelela kunonjela kanyhi yakatya, “Gwe go munhu, wuwalonjelaje i wanhu wa Isilayeli ze Nyumba yangu, vyono yinha zo wajele wayo hamonga na ze nzengwa yayo. Ho wakozwe cevu so metumo gawo mabi gono wetuma.
11 Na kolo wakolwa cevu ku soko ye mabi gono wetuma, nagwe baho, wuwaloloseze ze Nyumba yo Mulungu vyono zinha, ze cizenjele cayo, ne hwani yayo, ne ndawililo zayo, ne menjililo gayo, no wutemye wayo, wuwamanyize no wulajilize wayo hamonga ne malajilizo gayo gose. Nagwe wuzandicile zizi zose ha meso gawo, ho wasume kuziwona zizo zose, nawo ho wasume kuwukamisa wutemye wayo wose, hamonga no wulajilize wayo, no kuwitumama.
12 Awu wo wulajilize we Nyumba yo Mulungu. Mbavu zose ze mipaka yono yiyipilime Nyumba yo Mulungu, sunga ko kucanyha ye litunda, hakondya kuwikwa helile ha hono helile muno.”

I cikumbicilo

13 Avi navyo vyo vipimo vye cikumbicilo, vyono cali cinha, ne vipimo vivi vyali vipimo vivilya du vyono vyetumamilwa mu wupime we Nyumba yo Mulungu. Cikumbicilo cico, u musinji waco, wali wina musopeko wa cuma wo wugazi wa sentimita makumi mahano, no wukangazi wa sentimita makumi mahano nhavivilya, kanyhi cali ciwiciligwe mugo wo wutali wa sentimita makumi mejete ne'hano, wupilime musinji waco.
14 No wulawa baho ha musinji waco, cali cizenjeywe lijinji lye mhamba nongozi, yo wutali wa mita monga ku wubita kucanyha. Hanyuma kanyhi, baho hacanyha ye mhamba yiyo nongozi, hali hazenjigwe kanyhi lijinji linji lye mhamba ye kejete, lyono lyali lisulutiye linjiye mugati hadodo, sentimita makumi mahano, mugati ye lijinji lya hasi yayo. Ne lijinji lilyo lyali lina wutali wa mita nyejete ku wubita kucanyha. Na hacanyha yayo kanyhi ye mhamba yiyo, naho hali hajeleceze lijinji linji kanyhi lye mhamba ye kadatu. Nalyo lilyo lyali lisulutiye linjiye mugati hadodo sentimita makumi mahano, mugati ye lijinji lya hasi yayo.
15 Na mu mhamba yiyi ye kadatu, ya kucanyha, hali ho hanhu hono walavizyaga i makumbiko ge vilavywa vinyanywe. Naho hali nhavivyo no wutali wa mita nyejete. Ne mahembe ga mu zinhuzi zaco gose vyono mane, gali matali-tali, gacizize wutali we mayawo gono gali mapilimywe baho hacanyha ye cikumbicilo.
16 Na baho hacanyha ye cikumbicilo, hali heling'aniye du mbavu zose. Cali cina mita mutandatu mutandatu mbavu zose.
17 Na haciligati yayo, mu mhamba ye kejete, naho cali ciling'aniye mbavu zose. Cali cina mita mupunghati mupunghati mbavu zose, hamonga no mugo mugo wo wugazi wa sentimita makumi mejete ne 'hano kunze yayo. (No musopeko wayo wa cuma, wali wina wugazi wa sentimita makumi mahano.) Ne zingazi zo kubitila kucanyha ye cikumbicilo, zali zili ku lubavu lwa kucilima ye cikumbicilo.

U wusiwanulwe we cikumbicilo

18 Hanyuma u Muwaha Mulungu yakanonjela yakatya, “Gwe go munhu, ale wuhulicize zono nghukulonjela. Kolo cikumbicilo cakondya kuzengwa, yikunojeye wucisiwanulile mu nzila yo kulavizya makumbiko ge vilavywa vinyanywe muli cene. Hamonga no kucimiziza i sakami ye zi mitugo zono zowa zilavigwe so makumbiko gago.
19 Ne wakohani wo mulango wa Lawi, wo lulelo lwa Zadoki, wo wawo du wono wanojeye wanyizize no kunyitumamila'ne. Ane yo nene Muwaha Mulungu mono nghuzilajiliza zizi. Yikunojeye wuwapele ng'ombe nzeku ndodo, yo yiwe ye cilavywa co wubi.
20 Hanyuma wuyisole sakami yayo yinji, wuyibace mu mahembe ge cikumbicilo gose vyono mane, na mu zinhuzi zayo zose ze cikumbicilo, sunga na mu mugo wayo mbavu zose. Na mu nzila yiyo, kowa wakondya kuceza cikumbicilo no kucinozeza mulamu.
21 Hanyuma yayo wuyisole yiyo nzeku yono yilavigwe yikumbiko, so cilavywa co wubi, wuyikongojezeze i hanhu hono hahagulwa, kunze ye Nyumba yo Mulungu.
22 Ne lizuwa lye kejete, wusole mvulati yono yisina cilema co cose, wuyilavye yiwe cilavywa co wubi. Hanyuma wuceze cikumbicilo ne sakami, nha vyono wunozize ne sakami ye nzeku.
23 Na kolo wazituma zizo zose wakondya, wusole kanyhi nzeku yinji ndodo, hamonga na ngholo nduwilu ndodo, zose ziweze zono zisina vilema yaye.
24 Wundetele zose vyono nyejete. Nawo wakohani wazinyunyile mwino mucanyha yayo, hanyuma wazikongoje ziwe cilavywa kuli'ne.
25 Nagwe wujendelele kulavya nhondo zose nzeku, na mvulati, hamonga na ngholo nduwilu, sunga vifice vilo mupunghati, ziwe vilavywa vyo wubi. Ninga wulanjilile, zizo zose ziwe zisina cilema co cose.
26 Na mu vilo vyose vivyo mupunghati, i wakohani weze wajendeleye du nhondo zose kuceza cikumbicilo no kucinozeza mulamu, ho ciwe cakondya kuwikanizwa so witumamilwe.
27 Na kolo vyakondya kupuluta vivyo vilo mupunghati, nawo i wakohani nhondo, ho wokwanza kucitumamila cikumbicilo so mulimo wo kulavya vilavywa vye wanhu, i vilavywa vinyanywe hamonga ne vilavywa vyo mulamu. Nane nhondo ho nokwendezwa nanye wose, yo nene Muwaha Mulungu vyo nghutya.”


Ezekiyeli 44

Witumamilwe wo mulango wo lubavu lwa kucilima

1 Hanyuma yayo kanyhi, u munhu yuyo yakasola, yakangomola kanyhi ku nzila ya ku mulango wa kudeha, wa ku lubavu lwa kucilima ye Nyumba yo Mulungu. Nawo mulango wuwo wali mudindwe.
2 Nayo Muwaha Mulungu yakanonjela yakatya, “U mulango wuwuno, wokwikala mudindwe du nhavivi, hamba wulece kudigulwa yaye. Hamba hasina munhu wo wose, mono kokombolwa yawitumamile, ng'o. Hambi ane no Muwaha Mulungu u Mulungu wa Isilayeli, nha ngholela mu mulango wu wuno. Holeka yinojeye wikale mudindwe du.
3 Ninga hamba nhavivyo, u mutemi, yali wene du mono yatemile, yo yuyo mono kosuma kwinjila no kulya hali'ne, i ndigwa yono yelile. Nayo kokwinjilila mu nzila ye likumbo lyo mulango, kanyhi kolawaniziza mu nzila yiyo du kolo yakulawila kunze.”

I malajilizo, vyono yinojeye mu winjila mu Nyumba yo Mulungu

4 Hanyuma yayo kanyhi, u munhu yuyo, yakasola, yakanghoza mu mulango wo lubavu lwa kusumuka, wono wali wuli ku mwande ye Nyumba yo Mulungu. Nane nali neza kulanga nhavi, nghawona Nyumba yo Mulungu yimemile nhojo yo Muwaha Mulungu. Nane hobaho nghagwa hasi wugubama.
5 Nayo Muwaha Mulungu yakanonjela yakatya, “Gwe go munhu, alu wuhulicize viswanu, wuzikamise zose zono wukuziwona, na zono wukuzihulika. Hambi'ne nha nghusaka luno, nghuloloseze ze malajilizo, hamonga no wityatye wa mu Nyumba yo Mulungu. Wuzilanjilile muno muno ho wuwamanye i wanhu wono wanojeye kwinjila no kulawila mu Nyumba yo Mulungu kanyhi ne wanhuci wono si wakomboywe kwinjila.

6 “Kanyhi wuwalonjele wawo we cibede, i wanhu wa Isilayeli, wuwatye nhavi, vyono ane no Muwaha Mulungu, si nosuma kugakangalila i mawifyo gawo gono wagetumaga.
7 Hambi nha wawenjiza mu Nyumba yangu i wajenzi wono si walame, wono wasina cihuliko kuli'ne, ho wawe mugati, mwi'pango lono wakundavizya mafuta ne sakami vye makumbiko, vyono vikulavywa kuli'ne. Na mu nzila yiyo, wakawa kuyavufya Nyumba yangu. Holeka i wanhu wangu wakondya kucibena i cilagane, ku soko ye makani gawo gose ge mawifyo gono wakwituma.
8 Hamba si wasuma kuvikamisa vinhu vyangu vyono vyelile vya mu Nyumba yangu yaye, langa wene wakewicila i wanhu wajenzi, wawe wo wetumami wo mulimo wangu, mu Nyumba yangu.

9 “Ninga aluno nane, ane no Muwaha Mulungu nghutya nhavi, Hasina munhu wo wose mujenzi mono si yalame hamba munhu mono yasina cihuliko kuli'ne, hamba mujenzi wo wose mono vikalane ne wanhu wangu Isilayeli, mono kosuma kukombolwa ho yenjile mu Nyumba yangu.”

I Walawi wakuwuswa mu mulimo wo wukohani

10 U Muwaha Mulungu yakanonjela yakatya, “Alu ane neza kuwalipiciza wawo i Walawi, hamonga ne wanhu wanji wa muli Waisilayeli, wono vyahangana hamonga, wakawa kundeka no kuhemba i vihwani viseswe.
11 Holeka luno i wakohani wawa, wonyitumamila mu Nyumba yangu, u mulimo wo wulindilizi du we milango, hamonga ne milimo yinji yinji ya mu Nyumba yo Mulungu. Wowa wo wawulaji we mitugo zono wanhu wakulavya so vilavywa vinyanywe na so makumbiko, hamonga no kuwetumamila wanhu.
12 Na kanyhi vyono wene wali wo watonji we wanhu wa Isilayeli, mu wuhembi wo kuhemba vihwani viseswe, no wituma wubi. Holeka nane, ane no Muwaha Mulungu, nghelahiza nha vyono nane nowalipiciza du.
13 Holeka ku wulawa lulu, hamba si wonyitumamila kanyhi nha wakohani, hamba si wosuma kanyhi kuvipamiza i vinhu vyangu vyono vyelile, hamba si wokwinjilila lukulu kanyhi i Hanhu Hono Helile Helile, muno. Na wuwu wo wulipicizwe wawo, ku soko ye makani ge cevu gono wali kwituma.
14 Na ku wulawa lulu, ane nawanoza wawe walindilizi mu wulindilizi we Nyumba yo Mulungu du, na muli zose zono zikwitumwa mugati yayo.”

I wakohani

15 Kanyhi u Muwaha Mulungu yakatya, “Ale, wawo i wakohani Walawi, wo mulango wa Lawi, wo lulelo lwa Zadoki, wo wawo du wono wojendelele kunyitumamila mu Nyumba yangu ko wihuwilwa, hambi i wanhu wanji wa Isilayeli nha wakondya kwajilila no kundeka. Holeka wo wawo du wo lulelo lwa Zadoki, wono wosuma kunyitumamila no kusejelela hali'ne wawe kundavizya mafuta hamonga ne sakami vye makumbiko.
16 Kanyhi wo weneco du wono wosuma kwinjila mu Nyumba yangu, wawe kwitumama ha cikumbicilo, hamonga no kulongoza wuhembi mu Nyumba yo Mulungu.
17 Ninga nawo, lono wakwinjila mu mulango wa mwi'kumbo lya mugati, ye Nyumba yo Mulungu, yiwanojeye wawe wavwalile myenda ya bafuta. Walecele lukulu kuvwala cinhu co cose cihone no hamba, lono wali mu mulimo mu gati ye likumbo lya mugati, hamba lono wali mugati mu Nyumba yo Mulungu.
18 Heya, yiwanojeye wawe wavwalile vitambala vya bafuta mu mitwe yawo, kanyhi wavwalile suluwale za bafuta. Ninga langa walece kwiwopa ne misipi mu viwino vyawo, ho walece kugazwa ne lituguti.
19 Nawo yiwanojeye wawe kuyivula myenda yawo yose yono wowa wavwalile, lono wali mu mulimo, mu Nyumba yo Mulungu, no kuyiwika mumo mu vinyumba vyono vyelile, lono wakali si nalawila kwi'kumbo lya kudeha, kono ko wali wanhu. Heya, yowa yiwanojeye wayikawanize myenda yiyo, wavwale myenda yinji, ho walece kuwataciza wanhu wele, so vivwalo vyawo.

20 “Nawo i wakohani, yiwanojeye walece kuceta zimvwili zose mu mitwe yawo, hamba walece kuzileka ziwiluce sunga ziwe nhali muno. Langa wazilekaje ziwe no wutali wa hagati du.
21 Kanyhi i wakohani yiwanojeye walecele lukulu kung'wa divayi ye liwala lyo lyose lono wakwinjila mwi'kumbo lya mugati.
22 Kanyhi hasina mukohani wo wose mono kokombolwa yamutole mucekulu mono yafwiliywe no mulume, hamba yapejigwe talaka no mulume yunji. Langa yimunojeye yatole du muhinza we Ciisilayeli mono yakali namanya walume, hamba mucekulu we Ciisilayeli mono yafwiliywe no mulume wakwe mono yali mukohani.

23 “Kanyhi i wakohani yiwanojeye wawejize wanhu wangu wasume kumanya no kuziwagula zono zelile, na zono si zelile, no kuwamanyiza u wisimajile we vinhu vyavufuywe na vyono si vyavufywe.
24 Na hono wijele wiwanganile wowose muli wanhu, ale, wakohani wowozilamula mu wulunji we malajilizo gangu. Kanyhi yiwanojeye webate ne vilo vye zisikukuu ze cidini hamonga no wityatye wayo, na vyono winha wulajilize wangu, hamonga no kunozeza Sabato zangu.

25 “Kanyhi mukohani, yimunojeye yalecele lukulu kwiyavufya so wupamiza muvimba, lunji langa hono wuwe muvimba wo sogwe du, hamba wo nyina, hamba wo mwanagwe, hamba wo muwahawe hamba wo mwanakwawe, hamba wo lumbulye mono yakali natolwa.
26 Ninga nayo kolo yakondya kwiyeza kanyhi, yimunojeye yamule yekale vilo mupunghati,
27 hanyuma ho yenjile mwi'kumbo lya mugati lya mu Nyumba yo Mulungu, yalavye cilavywa cakwe co wubi. Nayo mu nzila yiyo, ho kosuma kwitumama kanyhi mu Nyumba yo Mulungu. Vyo nghutya ane no Muwaha Mulungu.

28 “Kanyhi i wakohani, wena wuhazi wo wukohani, nawo wo wuwezaga lye 'fungu lyawo lyono nawapela Isilayeli, wawe kwihaza lulelo na lulelo. Ninga si yiwanojeye wene, wawe na sawo yo yose mu Isilayeli, yono wosuma kutya vyono, ayi yo sawo yetu. Hambi ane nha yo nene du mono nowakwiza mu visakwa vyawo vyose.
29 Langa i wene, ndigwa yawo, yowa i vilavywa vyo wusaje, ne vilavywa vyo wubi, hamonga ne vilavywa vye masutya, hamonga ne vinhu vyose vyono wanhu wa Isilayeli wowa kundavizya, ane no Mulungu wawo.
30 Kanyhi i wakohani, yiwanojeye wapegwe i vilavywa vyose viswanu vye malimbuko, hamonga ne vilavywa vyose, vyono Waisilayeli wakundavizya ane no Mulungu wawo. Ne wanhu wose lono wakuteleka mikate yawo, yiwanojeye wawapele i wakohani i ligawo lilongozi lyo wusaje wenyu, ciwe cilavywa, nane nowamotya muno mu zinyumba zawo.
31 Kanyhi i wakohani, yiwanojeye walecele lukulu kulya ndeje, hamba nimu yo yose yono yifwiliye yeneco, hamba yono yiwulagwe ne nimu nzagwe.”


Ezekiyeli 45

I ligawo lye yisi, lyo Muwaha Mulungu

1 Na kolo mweza kuyigawa yisi no kuwapela mulango no wuhazi wawo we yisi, mukumbucile ligawo limonga, mumulavizyaje nayo Muwaha Mulungu. Ne ligawo lilyo linojeye lyeze lina wutali wa kilomita yikumi na nyejete na nusu, no wugazi wayo, weze kilomita yikumi limonga. Ne ligawo lilyo lyose lye yisi, lyowa lyelile.
2 Na mwi'gawo lyangu lilyo lye'si, mohagula i hanhu hono howa so Nyumba yo Mulungu, ne hanhu baho, haweze no wugazi wono wiling'aniye, wa mita magana mejete na makumi mahano kose kose. Ne hanhu baho heze kanyhi hapilimwe ne ciwanza co wugazi wa mita makumi mejete ne'hano kose kose.
3 Nagwe kohapima hanhu baho, wusole wutali wa kilomita yikumi na nyejete na nusu no wugazi wa kilomita yihano, hawe ho hanhu hono hozengwa Nyumba yo Mulungu, hamonga ne Hanhu Hone Helile, Helile muno.
4 Ne hanhu baho, ho howa helile du mu yisi yose, hono howa hawicigwe siya so wakohani du wono wakumwitumamila Muwaha Mulungu mu Nyumba yo Mulungu. Ne hanhu baho, ho hozengwa zikaya ze wakohani, hamonga ne Nyumba yo Mulungu.
5 Na ku lubavu lunji lwe hanhu baho, naho hanojeye hasiwanulwe hawe lye'gawo lye Walawi wono wakwitumama mu Nyumba yo Mulungu. No lubavu lulo, ko kozengwa zikaya zono wowa kwikala.

6 Na kanyhi, wupime hanhu hanji, behi ne hanhu baho hono helile, wutali wayo wuwe kilomita yikumi na nyejete na nusu, no wugazi wayo wuwe kilomita nyejete na nusu, hawe ho hanhu hono hozengwa zikaya, ne wanhu wose wa Isilayeli, wokwikala mu zikaya zizo.

I ligawo lye yisi yo mutemi

7 Kanyhi yinojeye mutemi nayo, yatuje ligawo lyakwe lye yisi. Ne ligawo lilyo, liweze ku wulawilila ku mupaka wa ku lubavu lwa kumwezi we hanhu hono helile, sunga kumwezi kwi'lenga lya Meditelaneya. Na ku wulawilila ku mupaka wa ku lubavu lwa kucilima, sunga ko ku mupaka wo lubavu lwa kucilima lwe yisi U wutali wayo ho wuwe wiling'aniye no wutali we zi'si zono wapejigwe we zimhuga za Isilayeli.
8 Ne hanhu baho, lyowa lye 'gawo du lyono lyowa lyo lyo mutemi, mu yisi ya Isilayeli. Nayo ho yalece kuwewonela wanhu, nayo koyileka yisi yose, yiwe sawo ye zimhuga ze wanhu wa Isilayeli du.

Wulajilize kuli mutemi

9 U Muwaha Mulungu yakutya nhavi, “Nha mwanza katali mukwituma wubi, anye mo watemi wa Isilayeli! Ninga aluno, muwulekaje wugazi wenyu, hamonga ne mawonezi genyu kuli wanhu. Alu mwitumaje ze ling'ani na ze mbeka. Mulecele lukulu kuwawusa kanyhi wanhu wangu mu yisi yawo. Ane vyo nghumutyenyi, ane no Muwaha Mulungu.

10 “Kanyhi munhu wo wose yimunojeye yetumamile vipimo vye ling'ani na vye mbeka.

11 “Efa ne Bati, zowa cipimo cimonga. Nayo Bati yowa ligawo lye likumi lya Homeli, na Efa nayo, ligawo lye likumi lya Homeli, nayo Homeli yowa co cipimo ce nhondo zose.
12 Nayo Shekeli, yowa Gela makumi mejete, na Shekeli makumi mahano, zowa makumi na monga.
13 Awu nawo wo wulavye wono molavya, Ligawo lyo mutandatu lya Efa mu Homeli monga ya ngano. Nanye mowa kulavya ligawo lyo mutandatu lye Efa mu Homeli ye Shayili.
14 Nalyo ligawo lye mafuta lyono lyawagwa mu Bati lye mafuta, lyowa yigawo lye likumi lya Bati, mu koli monga. Nayo koli monga yikuwa nha Bati yikumi ya Homeli.
15 Na Ngholo monga monga mu wulawa mu ngholo magana mejete mejete za mu wudimo wuswanu we Waisilayeli.

“Kanyhi yinojeye mulavye ne cilavywa co wusaje, hamonga ne zimitugo so cilavywa cinyanywe, na so vilavywa vyo mulamu, u wubi wenyu ho wusume kunenelwa. Yo nene no Muwaha Mulungu mono nghuzilajiliza zizi.

16 “Ne wanhu wose wa mono yisi, yiwanojeye wamutegulile mutemi wa Isilayeli i vilavywa vivino vyose.
17 Nayo mutemi, wowa wo mulimo wakwe kuzilavya zimitugo so vilavywa vinyanywe, hamonga ne vilavywa vyo wusaje, ne vilavywa vye divayi so nghungugo yose ya Isilayeli, mu mapango ge zisikukuu ze ndawo yo mwezi, na mu zi Sabato, sunga na mu zisikukuu zinji zinji zose. Mwene yo kolavya i vilavywa vyo wubi, na vyo wusaje, ne vilavywa vinyanywe, hamonga ne vilavywa vyo mulamu ho yawanozeze wilamule i wanhu wa Isilayeli.”

I zisikukuu

(Wut 12:1-20; Lawi 23:33-43)

18 U Muwaha Mulungu yakamutya nhavi, “Yikunojeye mwi'zuwa lilongozi lya mu mwezi mulongozi, wusole nzeku ndodo yono yisina cilema, wuyeze Nyumba yo Mulungu.
19 Nayo mukohani koyisola sakami nyhefu ye cilavywa cico co wubi, yabace mu zifulemu ze milango ye Nyumba yo Mulungu, na mu zinhuzi zose vyono yine, ze cikumbicilo. Sunga na mu zifulemu ze milango yose, ya mwi'kumbo lya mugati.
20 Na mwi'zuwa lyo mupunghati lya mu mwezi, kanyhi kosuma kunoza nhavivyo, so munhu wo wose mono yakwitumila wubi citu no wu wula kumanya. Na mu nzila yiyi, ho kosuma kuyinoza Nyumba yo Mulungu yiwe yelile.

21 “Na mwi'zuwa lye likumi na yize lya mu mwezi mulongozi, baho ho kokwanziza kuyinoza sikukuu ye Pasaka. Na mu vilo mupunghati, i wanhu wose wowa kulya mukate wono wusina cisase.
22 Na mwi'zuwa lilongozi lye sikukuu, u mutemi yalavye nzeku ndodo, yiwe so cilavywa co wubi, na ku soko yo wubi we wanhu wose.
23 Na mu vilo vyose, mu vilo vivyo vyo mupunghati, vye sikukuu, yimunojeye yamulavizye u Muwaha Mulungu, makumbiko ga nzeku ndodo mupunghati, nzeku monga monga nhondo zose, hamonga na ngholo nduwilu mupunghati, zono zisina cilema, ziwe vilavywa vinyanywe kanyhi. Yimunojeye yalavye mhene mvulati monga monga nhondo zose, mu vilo vivyo mupunghati so cilavywa co wubi.
24 Na kanyhi, yinojeye hawe ne cilavywa co wusaje ca lita zono zificile yikumi na mupunghati na nusu, hamonga ne mafuta ga lita yidatu, ho vilavywe hamonga ne nzeku monga, monga hamonga ne zingholo zinduwilu monga monga, zono zikulavywa likumbiko.

25 “Na mu zisikukuu ze vibanda, zono zowa kwanza mwi'zuwa lye likumi ne'hano, lya mu mwezi wo mupunghati, u mutemi kowa kulavya nhondo zose mu vilo mupunghati, i vilavywa vyo wubi vyono vihwanile du nhe vilo vye zisikukuu zinji zose. Nha cinji, i vilavywa vinyanywe, ne vilavywa vyo 'saje, hamonga ne vilavywa vye mafuta.”


Ezekiyeli 46

U mutemi ne zisikukuu

1 U Muwaha Mulungu yakutya, “U mulango wo lubavu lwa kucilima wo kwinjilila mwi'kumbo lya mugati, weze kwikala mudinde mu vilo vyose mutandatu vye milimo. Ninga mwi'zuwa lye Sabato, na mu zisikukuu zose ze ndawo yo mwezi, aho langa, weze kudigulwa wikala wuli mwazi.
2 Nayo mutemi kokwinjilila nzila ya mwi'kumbo lyo mulango wu wulya wo lubavu lwa kunze, yawe kwima behi ne masumbili go mulango, lono i wakohani wali mu mulimo wo kulavya i makumbiko ge vilavywa vyakwe vinyanywe, hamonga ne vilavywa vyakwe vyo mulamu. Nayo mutemi yimunojeye yahembe baho ha mulango. Na kolo yakondya kuhemba, ho yawe kuwuka baho. Ninga hamba nhavivyo, u mulango langa, wulece kudindwa sunga cinyamihe, ho wudindwe.
3 Na mu vilo vyose vye Sabato, na mu vilo vyose vye zisikukuu za mu ndawo yo mwezi, i wanhu wose wanojeye wamuhembe Muwaha Mulungu, baho ha mwande yo mulango wuwo.

4 “Nayo Mutemi yowa yimunojeye mwi'zuwa lye Sabato, yamulavizye Muwaha Mulungu makumbiko manyanywe, nyana za ngholo mutandatu zono zisina vilema, hamonga na nduwilu monga, nayo yono yisina cilema.
5 Na mu cilavywa ce mvulati monga monga, yeze kulavya hamonga ne cilavywa co wusaje, wa lita yikumi na mupunghati na nusu. Na mu nyana ze zingholo monga monga, yeze kulavya mu nghango yo yose yono kosuma kulavya. Na mu cilavywa co wusaje, yeze kulavya hamonga ne mafuta, lita yidatu yidatu.
6 Na mu sikukuu ye ndawo yo mwezi, yeze kulavya nzeku ndodo monga, hamonga na nyana ze zingholo, mutandatu, na mvulati monga, zose zono zisina vilema.
7 Nayo yeze kulavya cilavywa co wusaje lita yikumi na mupunghati na nusu, so nzeku monga, ne cilavywa ca munghango yono kosuma mweneco kulavya, so mvulati monga monga. Ninga i mafuta langa, go geze kulavywa hamonga ne cilavywa co wusaje lono yakuwulavya.
8 Ninga yimunojeye mutemi yawuce baho ha mulango yabite kudeha, yakolele mu mulango wu wulya du wono yenjiliye.

9 “Na kolo i wanhu wakuza kumuhemba Muwaha Mulungu, mu vilo vye zisikukuu, wuwalonjelaje nha vyono, wono wokwinjilila ku mulango we sumuka mu Nyumba yo Mulungu, kolo wakondya wuhembi, walawanizize ku mulango wa kutakama. Nawo wono wakwinjilila ku mulango wa kutakama, walawanizize ku mulango we sumuka. Halece kuwa no munhu wo wose mono kolawaniziza kanyhi ku mulango wono yenjiliye ng'o, langa yiwanojeye munhu wo wose yawe kulawaniziza mu mulango wono si yenjiliye.
10 Nayo mutemi yimunojeye yawe kwinjila lono i wanhu wakwinjila, kanyhi yawe kulawaniza hamonga ne wanhu lono wakulawila kunze.
11 Na mu vilo viwaha, na mu vilo viwaha vinji vinji vyono vyatemywa, yinojeye cilavywa co wusaje ciwe kulavywa, yeze lita yikumi na mupunghati na nusu so nzeku monga hamba so nduwilu monga, hamonga ne vinhu vinji vinji, vyono i wahembi wosaka weneco, walavye so ngholo monga monga. Ninga yinojeye lita yidatu za mafuta, zeze kulavywa hamonga ne cilavywa cimonga co wusaje.

12 “Nayo mutemi kolo yakuhila kumulavizya Muwaha Mulungu, i cilavywa cinyanywe, hamba cilavywa co mulamu, ale, u mulango wo lubavu lwa kucilima wono wudomile kwi'kumbo lya mugati, wunojeye wuwe mudigule wuli mwazi. Nayo mutemi kocilavizya cilavywa mu nzila yono kayitumamilaga mu vilo vye Sabato. Ninga kolo yakondya kulawaniza kudeha, yinojeye u mulango wudindwe kanyhi.”

I vilavywa vye nhondo zose

13 U Muwaha Mulungu yakutya nhavi, “Nhondo zose hakali mitondo, yinojeye yeze kulavywa kuli Muwaha Mulungu, ngholo monga monga ya mwaka monga, yono yisina cilema. Ne cilavywa cici, yinojeye ciwe kulavywa nhondo zose.
14 Kanyhi ne cilavywa co wusaje kilo nyejete, naco yinojeye ciwe kulavywa nhondo zose, hakali mitondo, hamonga ne mafuta lita monga, go kuhanza no wusaje. Ne lilajilizo lili lye cilavywa cici kuli Muwaha Mulungu, lyowa lya cibitilila.
15 Nhavivi vyo wowa kulavya cilavywa ce ngholo, no wusaje, hamonga ne mafuta kuli Muwaha Mulungu, nhondo zose hakali mitondo, cibitilila.”

U mutemi ne yisi

16 U Muwaha Mulungu yakulajiliza yakutya nhavi, “Hono mutemi yasole hanhu hanji mu yisi yakwe, yamutemele yumonga wa muli wanagwe, i hanhu baho, howa yawa sawo ya yuyo mwanagwe du hamonga ne we mhuga yakwe.
17 Ninga hono mutemi yamutemele yumonga wa muli wetumami wakwe, i hanhu baho langa holomuwuyila kanyhi mutemi mu mwaka wo wuhogolwe wo mwitumami yuyo kolo weza kufika. Hambi ayo nha yo sawo yakwe du mutemi hamonga ne wanagwe.
18 Kanyhi yimunojeye mutemi, yalecele lukulu kuwasolela i wanhu sawo yawo. Kanyhi yalanjilile, vyono i hanhu ho hose mu'si hono yakuwapela i wanagwe, heze i hanhu hono hakulawa mu yisi yakwe mweneco du. Ho yalece kuwewonela wanhu wangu mu nzila yo kuwaboka yisi yawo.”

I hanhu ho wutelecelo we zindigwa, mu Nyumba yo Mulungu

19 Hanyuma kanyhi u munhu yuyo, yakasola, yakanhegula ku winjililo we vinyumba, vyono vyali vilanjile ku lubavu lwa kusumuka, behi no mulango wo lubavu lwa kutakama we likumbo lya mugati. Ne vinyumba vivyo, vyali vyo vinyumba vyono vyelile vye wakohani. U munhu yuyo, yakandajila hanhu hanji ku lubavu lwa kumwezi ye vinyumba vivyo,
